<b>Jeremías 1</b><br><b>1</b> Amazwi kaJeremiya, indodana kaHilikhiya wabapristi a ababeseAnathothi elizweni lakoBhenjamini; b <b>2</b> okwafika kuye ilizwi leNkosi ensukwini zikaJosiya c indodana kaAmoni, inkosi yakoJuda, ngomnyaka wetshumi lantathu wokubusa kwakhe. d <b>3</b> Lafika futhi ensukwini zikaJehoyakhimi indodana kaJosiya, inkosi yakoJuda, e kwaze kwaba sekupheleni komnyaka wetshumi lanye f kaZedekhiya indodana kaJosiya, g inkosi yakoJuda, kwaze kwaba sekuthunjweni kweJerusalema h enyangeni yesihlanu. i , j 
<b>4</b> Kwasekufika kimi ilizwi leNkosi lisithi: <b>5</b> Ngingakakubumbi esiswini k ngakwazi, l njalo ungakaphumi esizalweni m ngakwehlukanisa; n ngakubeka waba ngumprofethi o ezizweni. p <b>6</b> Ngasengisithi: Hawu Nkosi Jehova! Khangela, angikwazi ukukhuluma, q ngoba ngingumntwana. r <b>7</b> Kodwa iNkosi yathi kimi: Ungatsho ukuthi: Ngingumntwana; ngoba uzakuya kubo bonke engikuthuma kubo, lakho konke engikulaya khona uzakukhuluma. s <b>8</b> Ungesabi ubuso babo, t , u ngoba mina ngilawe v , w , x ukukophula, y itsho iNkosi. 
<b>9</b> INkosi yasiselula isandla sayo, z yathinta umlomo wami; aa iNkosi yasisithi kimi: Khangela, ngibekile amazwi ami emlonyeni wakho. ab <b>10</b> Bona, ngikubekile lamuhla phezu kwezizwe laphezu kwemibuso, ac ukusiphuna, lokudiliza, lokubhubhisa, lokuphosela phansi, ukwakha, lokuhlanyela. ad <b>11</b> Ilizwi leNkosi laselifika kimi lisithi: Ubonani, Jeremiya? Ngasengisithi: Ngibona uswazi lwesihlahla se-alimondi*. ae <b>12</b> INkosi yasisithi kimi: Ubone kuhle, ngoba ngizalinda ilizwi lami ukuze ngilenze. 
<b>13</b> Njalo ilizwi leNkosi lafika kimi ngokwesibili lisithi: Ubonani? Ngasengisithi: Ngibona imbiza af ebilayo, ag njalo ubuso bayo bungasenyakatho. ah <b>14</b> INkosi yasisithi kimi: Okubi kuzaqutshulwa kuvela enyakatho ai phezu kwabo bonke abakhileyo belizwe. <b>15</b> Ngoba khangela, ngibiza insapho zonke zemibuso aj yenyakatho, ak itsho iNkosi; njalo zizafika, zimise ngasinye isihlalo salo sobukhosi ekungeneni kwamasango eJerusalema, al zimelene lemiduli yayo yonke inhlangothi zonke, zimelene lemizi yonke yakoJuda. 
<b>16</b> Ngizakhuluma izigwebo zami zimelene labo ngenxa yabo bonke ububi babo, am abangidelileyo, an , ao batshisela abanye onkulunkulu impepha, ap , aq bakhonza imisebenzi yezandla zabo. ar <b>17</b> Wena-ke, bopha ukhalo lwakho, as usukume, ukhulume kibo konke mina engikulaya khona. at Ungethuki ngenxa yobuso babo, au hlezi ngikwethuse phambi kwabo. 
<b>18</b> Ngoba khangela, mina ngikubekile lamuhla av ube ngumuzi ovikelweyo, aw , ax njalo ube yinsika yensimbi, ay njalo ube yimiduli yethusi, az ukumelana lelizwe lonke, ukumelana lamakhosi akoJuda, ukumelana leziphathamandla zayo, ukumelana labapristi bayo, lokumelana labantu belizwe. <b>19</b> Njalo bazakulwa bemelene lawe; kodwa kabayikukwehlula; ba ngoba ngilawe, itsho iNkosi, ukukophula. bb , bc 
<b>Jeremías 2</b><br><b>1</b> Laselifika kimi ilizwi leNkosi lisithi: <b>2</b> Hamba uyememezela endlebeni zeJerusalema usithi: Itsho njalo iNkosi: Ngiyakukhumbula, umusa wobutsha bakho, a uthando lokuganwa kwakho, b ukungilandela kwakho enkangala, c elizweni elingahlanyelwanga. d <b>3</b> UIsrayeli wayeyibungcwele eNkosini, e izithelo zokuqala zesivuno sayo; f bonke abamudlayo bazakuba lecala, g ububi buzabehlela, h itsho iNkosi. <b>4</b> Zwanini ilizwi leNkosi, ndlu kaJakobe, lensendo zonke zendlu kaIsrayeli. 
<b>5</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Yibubi bani oyihlo ababuthola kimi, ukuze baye khatshana lami, i balandele j okuyize, k babe yize? l , m <b>6</b> Njalo kabatshongo ukuthi: Ingaphi iNkosi eyasenyusa elizweni leGibhithe, n eyasikhokhela enkangala, o elizweni elilamagwadule p lelilemigodi, q elizweni r lokoma, s lelethunzi lokufa, t elizweni umuntu angalidabulanga, lalapho okungahlalanga muntu khona? 
<b>7</b> Njalo ngaliletha elizweni elivundileyo, u ukuze lidle izithelo zalo lokuhle kwalo; v kodwa selingenile lalingcolisa ilizwe lami, lenza ilifa lami laba yisinengiso. w <b>8</b> Abapristi kabatshongo ukuthi: Ingaphi iNkosi? Lalabo abaphatha umlayo kabangazanga; x lababusi baphambuka bemelene lami; y labaprofethi baprofetha ngoBhali, z balandela izinto ezingasiziyo. aa <b>9</b> Ngakho ngisezaphikisana lawe, ab itsho iNkosi, njalo ngizaphikisana labantwana babantwana benu. ac 
<b>10</b> Ngoba dlulelani ezihlengeni ad zeKhithimi ae libone, lithumele eKedari, af linakane kuhle, libone ukuthi kukhona into enjengale yini? <b>11</b> Isizwe sake santshintsha yini onkulunkulu baso, ag abangeyisibo onkulunkulu? ah Kodwa abantu bami bantshintshile uDumo lwabo ai ngalokho okungasizi lutho. aj <b>12</b> Mangalani kakhulu, mazulu, ak ngalokhu, lethuke, lichitheke kakhulu, itsho iNkosi. 
<b>13</b> Ngoba abantu bami benzile izinto ezimbi ezimbili; bangidelile al , am mina mthombo an , ao wamanzi aphilayo, ap ukuthi bazigebhele izikhongozelo, izikhongozelo ezidabukileyo, ezingegcine manzi. <b>14</b> UIsrayeli uyisigqili yini? aq Kumbe esizelwe ekhaya yini? ar Kungani waba yimpango? <b>15</b> Amabhongo ezilwane abhongele phezu kwakhe, as akhupha ilizwi lawo, enza ilizwe lakhe laba yincithakalo; at imizi yakhe itshisiwe au ingelamhlali. av , aw <b>16</b> Futhi abantwana beNofi ax leThahaphanesi ay badlele enkanda yakho. 
<b>17</b> Kawuzenzelanga lokhu yini, az ngokuthi udele iNkosi uNkulunkulu wakho, ba ngesikhathi ikukhokhela endleleni? bb <b>18</b> Khathesi-ke ulani lendlela yeGibhithe, bc ukuthi unathe amanzi eSihori? bd Futhi ulani lendlela yeAsiriya, be ukuthi unathe amanzi omfula? bf <b>19</b> Ububi bakho buzakulaya, bg lokuhlehlela kwakho nyovane kuzakukhuza. bh Ngakho yazi ubone ukuthi kuyinto embi lebabayo bi ukuthi uyidelile iNkosi uNkulunkulu wakho, bj lokuthi ukungesaba kakukho kuwe, itsho iNkosi uJehova wamabandla. 
<b>20</b> Ngoba kwasendulo ngephula ijogwe lakho, bk ngaqamula izibopho zakho; bl wasusithi: Kangiyikukhonza; kodwa phezu kwalo lonke uqaqa oluphakemeyo langaphansi kwaso sonke isihlahla esiluhlaza bm , bn wazulazula bo njengesifebe. bp , bq <b>21</b> Kube kanti mina ngakuhlanyela, br ulivini elihle, bs inhlanyelo ethembekileyo ngokupheleleyo. Pho, usuphenduke njani waba lugatsha olonakeleyo lwevini lemzini kimi? bt 
<b>22</b> Ngoba lanxa ugeza bu ngesoda*, bv uzithathele isepa enengi, bw kanti ububi bakho bube lichatha phambi kwami, bx itsho iNkosi uJehova. <b>23</b> Ungatsho njani ukuthi: Kangingcolanga, by kangilandelanga oBhali? Bona indlela yakho esigodini, bz wazi ukuthi wenzeni, kamela elisikazi elilejubane ca elizulazula ezindleleni zalo. cb <b>24</b> Ungubabhemi weganga cc , cd owejwayele inkangala, ophembela umoya enkanukweni yenhliziyo yakhe. ce Ngubani ongamvimbela enkanukweni yakhe? cf Bonke abamdingayo kabayikuzidinisa; ngenyanga yakhe bazamthola. cg 
<b>25</b> Nqanda unyawo lwakho ekungabini ngolungelasicathulo, ch lomphimbo wakho ekomeni; ci kodwa wena wathi: cj Kakulathemba; ck hatshi; ngoba ngathanda abemzini, cl njalo ngizabalandela. <b>26</b> Njengesela lilenhloni lapho selibanjiwe, ngokunjalo indlu kaIsrayeli ilenhloni, bona, amakhosi abo, iziphathamandla zabo, labapristi babo, labaprofethi babo, cm 
<b>27</b> besithi esigodweni: Wena ungubaba; lelitsheni: cn Wena wangizala. Ngoba baphendulele umhlana kimi, hatshi-ke ubuso; kodwa ngesikhathi co sokuhlupheka kwabo bazakuthi: Sukuma, usisindise! cp <b>28</b> Kodwa bangaphi onkulunkulu bakho ozenzele bona? cq Kabasukume uba bengakusindisa cr ngesikhathi sobubi bakho; cs ngoba njengenani lemizi yakho bangako onkulunkulu bakho, Juda. ct <b>29</b> Liphikisanelani lami? Lonke liphambekile kimi, cu itsho iNkosi. 
<b>30</b> Ngibatshayile abantwana benu cv ngeze; kabemukelanga ukuqondiswa; inkemba yenu ibadlile abaprofethi benu, cw njengesilwane esichithayo. <b>31</b> Wena sizukulwana, cx lina bonani ilizwi leNkosi. Bengiyinkangala yini kuIsrayeli? cy Kumbe ilizwe lobumnyama obunzima yini? Batsholoni abantu bami ukuthi: Sizulazulile; cz kasisayikuza kuwe? <b>32</b> Intombi ingazikhohlwa yini iziceciso zayo, kumbe umakoti amabhanti akhe? da Kanti abantu bami bangikhohliwe insuku ezingelanani. db 
<b>33</b> Kungani ulungisa indlela yakho ekudingeni uthando? dc Ngakho ubafundisile labo ababi izindlela zakho. dd <b>34</b> Lezigqokweni zakho kutholakala igazi lemiphefumulo yabayanga abangelacala; de kawubatholanga befohlela phakathi, kodwa phezu kwakho konke lokhu. <b>35</b> Kanti wena uthi: Ngoba kangilacala, isibili intukuthelo yakhe izaphenduka isuke kimi. Khangela, ngizakwehlulelana lawe, df ngoba uthi: Kangonanga. dg 
<b>36</b> Kungani uzulazula kangaka dh ukuntshintsha indlela yakho? di Uzayangiswa yiGibhithe layo, dj njengoba wayangiswa yiAsiriya. dk <b>37</b> Yebo, uzaphuma usuka lapha, lezandla zakho ziphezu kwekhanda lakho, dl ngoba iNkosi iwalile amathemba akho, njalo kawuyikuphumelela ngawo. 
<b>Jeremías 3</b><br><b>1</b> Kuthiwa: Uba indoda ilahla umkayo, a asuke kuyo, abe ngowenye indoda, izabuyela kuye futhi yini? b Lelolizwe belingayikungcoliswa kakhulu yini? c Kanti wena ufebile lezithandwa ezinengi; d kodwa buyela kimi, e itsho iNkosi. <b>2</b> Phakamisela amehlo akho emadundulwini, f ubone; kungaphi lapho ongalalwanga khona? Ezindleleni wabahlalela, g njengomArabhiya enkangala; njalo ulingcolisile ilizwe ngobufebe bakho langenkohlakalo yakho. h 
<b>3</b> Ngakho izihlambo zigodliwe; i njalo lalingekho izulu langemuva; j njalo waba lebunzi lewule, k wala ukuba lenhloni. l <b>4</b> Kawuyikuthi kusukela khathesi ukhale yini kimi uthi: Baba, ungumeluleki m wobutsha bami? n <b>5</b> Yena uzalugcina yini ulaka lwakhe phakade? o Uzalulondoloza kuze kube sekucineni yini? Khangela, ukhulumile, wenza izinto ezimbi, uphumelele. UIsrayeli loJuda bacelwa ukuphenduka 
<b>6</b> INkosi yasisithi kimi ensukwini zikaJosiya inkosi: Ubonile yini lokho uIsrayeli ohlehlele nyovane akwenzileyo? p Yena waya kuyo yonke intaba ephakemeyo, langaphansi kwaso sonke isihlahla esiluhlaza, q waphingela khona. r <b>7</b> Ngasengisithi esezenzile zonke lezizinto: s Buyela kimi; t kodwa kabuyelanga. Lodadewabo ongathembekanga u uJuda wakubona lokho. v 
<b>8</b> Ngasengibona, lapho ngenxa yazo zonke imbangela w uIsrayeli ohlehlela nyovane aphinga ngazo, x ngamxotsha ngamnika y incwadi yesehlukaniso, z ukuthi udadewabo ongathembekanga, uJuda, kesabanga, aa kodwa laye wayafeba. ab <b>9</b> Kwasekusithi ngenxa yendumela yokufeba kwakhe wangcolisa ilizwe, ac waphinga lamatshe lezihlahla. ad <b>10</b> Kube kanti kukho konke lokhu udadewabo ongathembekanga, uJuda, kabuyelanga kimi ngenhliziyo yakhe yonke, ae , af kodwa ngenkohliso, itsho iNkosi. 
<b>11</b> INkosi yasisithi kimi: UIsrayeli ohlehlela nyovane ag uzilungisise ah kuloJuda okhohlisayo. ai <b>12</b> Hamba uyememezela lamazwi ngasenyakatho aj uthi: Phenduka, ak Israyeli ohlehlela nyovane, al itsho iNkosi. Kangiyikwenza ulaka lwami lwehlele phezu kwakho, am ngoba ngilesihawu, an itsho iNkosi, njalo kangiyikugcina ulaka lwami kuze kube phakade. ao 
<b>13</b> Yazi kuphela isiphambeko sakho ukuthi uphambekile umelene leNkosi uNkulunkulu wakho, ap wahlakaza izindlela zakho aq kwabezizweni ar ngaphansi kwaso sonke isihlahla esiluhlaza, as njalo kawulalelanga ilizwi lami, at itsho iNkosi. <b>14</b> Phendukani, au bantwana abahlehlela nyovane, av itsho iNkosi, ngoba mina ngiliganile; njalo ngizalithatha, aw abe munye emzini, ax lababili kulo usendo, ngililethe eZiyoni. ay <b>15</b> Njalo ngizalinika az abelusi njengokwenhliziyo yami, ba abazalelusa ngolwazi lokuqedisisa. bb 
<b>16</b> Kuzakuthi-ke lapho selandile laba banengi elizweni, ngalezonsuku, itsho iNkosi, kabasayikuthi: Umtshokotsho wesivumelwano seNkosi; njalo kawuyikufika enhliziyweni, njalo kabayikuwukhumbula, futhi kabayikuwuhambela, kungabuyi kwenziwe futhi. <b>17</b> Ngalesosikhathi bazabiza iJerusalema ngokuthi yisihlalo sobukhosi seNkosi; lezizwe zonke zizabuthaniswa kuyo, bc ngebizo leNkosi, bd eJerusalema; futhi kazisayikulandela inkani yenhliziyo yazo embi. be 
<b>18</b> Ngalezonsuku indlu yakoJuda izakuya kuyo indlu yakoIsrayeli, bf ziphume kanyekanye elizweni lenyakatho, bg zize elizweni engalinika oyihlo libe yilifa labo. bh <b>19</b> Kodwa mina ngathi: Ngizakubeka njani phakathi kwabantwana, ngikunike ilizwe eliloyisekayo, ilifa elihle kakhulu lezizwe. Ngasengisithi: Uzangibiza bi ngokuthi: Baba; njalo ungaphambuki ekungilandeleni. <b>20</b> Isibili, njengomfazi ungathembekanga kumkakhe, bj ngokunjalo kalithembekanga kimi, bk , bl lina ndlu kaIsrayeli, itsho iNkosi. 
<b>21</b> Ilizwi lezwakala emadundulwini, bm ukukhala lokuncenga kwabantwana bakoIsrayeli, bn ngoba bephambule indlela yabo, bayikhohlwa iNkosi uNkulunkulu wabo. bo <b>22</b> Phendukani, lina bantwana abahlehlela nyovane; bp ngizakwelapha bq ukuhlehlela nyovane kwenu. br Khangela, thina siyeza kuwe, ngoba uyiNkosi uNkulunkulu wethu. <b>23</b> Isibili kuyize ukuthembela kusindiso oluvela emaqaqeni, lebunengini bezintaba. bs Isibili luseNkosini uNkulunkulu wethu usindiso lukaIsrayeli. bt 
<b>24</b> Ngoba ihlazo liwuqedile bu umtshikatshika wabobaba kusukela ebutsheni bethu, bv izimvu zabo lenkomo zabo, amadodana abo lamadodakazi abo. <b>25</b> Silala phansi ehlazweni lethu, lenhloni zethu zisimbozile; bw ngoba sonile eNkosini uNkulunkulu wethu, bx thina labobaba, kusukela ebutsheni bethu kuze kube lamuhla, by njalo kasilalelanga ilizwi leNkosi uNkulunkulu wethu. bz 
<b>Jeremías 4</b><br><b>1</b> Uba uphenduka, Israyeli, itsho iNkosi, phendukela kimi; a njalo uba ususa phambi kwami izinengiso zakho, kawuyikuphambuka. b <b>2</b> Futhi uzafunga: c Kuphila kukaJehova, d ngeqiniso, e ngokwahlulela, langokulunga; f lezizwe zizazibusisa kuyo, g lakuyo zizincome. h <b>3</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi kumadoda akoJuda leJerusalema: Qhathani i ingqatho yenu, j lingahlanyeli phakathi kwameva. k 
<b>4</b> Zisokeleni eNkosini, l lisuse amajwabu aphambili enhliziyo yenu, madoda akoJuda labahlali beJerusalema; hlezi ulaka lwami luphume njengomlilo, m lutshise, kungabi khona ongalucitsha, ngenxa yobubi bezenzo zenu. n , o Ilizwe lakoJuda lizahlaselwa <b>5</b> Bikani koJuda, lizwakalise eJerusalema, lithi: Vuthelani uphondo elizweni; p klabalalani ngokugcweleyo lithi: Buthanani, q singene emizini ebiyelweyo. r <b>6</b> Misani uphawu ngaseZiyoni, s balekani, lingemi; ngoba ngizaletha ububi buvela enyakatho, t lokwephuka okukhulu. 
<b>7</b> Isilwane sesenyukile siphuma ehlathini laso, u lomchithi wezizwe usukile, uphumile endaweni yakhe ukuze ulenze ilizwe lakho incithakalo; v imizi yakho izakuba ngamanxiwa, ingabi lomhlali. w <b>8</b> Ngenxa yalokhu bhincani amasaka, x lilani liqhinqe isililo; ngoba ulaka lweNkosi oluvuthayo kaluphendukanga lusuka kithi. y , z <b>9</b> Njalo kuzakuthi ngalolosuku, itsho iNkosi, inhliziyo yenkosi izaphela, lenhliziyo yeziphathamandla; labapristi bazamangala kakhulu, labaprofethi bazathithibala. aa 
<b>10</b> Ngasengisithi: Hawu, Nkosi Jehova! Isibili ubakhohlisile lokubakhohlisa lababantu leJerusalema ab usithi: Lizakuba lokuthula; kanti inkemba ifinyelela emphefumulweni. <b>11</b> Ngalesosikhathi kuzakuthiwa kulabobantu lakuyo iJerusalema: Umoya otshisayo ac wamadundulu ad enkangala, ae indlela yendodakazi yabantu bami, ungeyisiwo wokwela njalo ungeyisiwo wokuhlambulula. <b>12</b> Umoya ogcweleyo ovela kuwo uzakuza kimi. Khathesi lami ngizakhuluma izahlulelo zimelene labo. 
<b>13</b> Khangela, uzakwenyuka njengamayezi, lenqola zakhe njengesivunguzane; af amabhiza akhe alesiqubu kulezinkozi. ag Maye kithi, ngoba sichithiwe! ah <b>14</b> Jerusalema, hlambulula inhliziyo yakho kuphume inkohlakalo, ai ukuze usindiswe. Koze kube nini imicabango yakho emibi ihlala phakathi kwakho? <b>15</b> Ngoba ilizwi liyamemeza aj lisuka koDani, ak lizwakalisa uhlupho oluvela entabeni yakoEfrayimi. al 
<b>16</b> Khumbuzani izizwe, khangelani, lizwakalise limelene leJerusalema; kufika abalindi bevela elizweni elikhatshana, am baphakamisa ilizwi labo bemelene lemizi yakoJuda. <b>17</b> Njengabalindi bensimu bamelene layo inhlangothi zonke, an ngoba ingivukele, itsho iNkosi. <b>18</b> Indlela yakho lezenzo zakho kukwenzele lezizinto; ao lokhu kuyinkohlakalo yakho, ngoba kubuhlungu, ap ngoba kufinyelela enhliziyweni yakho. 
<b>19</b> Imibilini yami, imibilini yami! aq Ngilobuhlungu emidulini yenhliziyo yami. Inhliziyo yami yenza umsindo phakathi kwami; ar ngingeke ngithule; ngoba wena mphefumulo wami, uzwile ukukhala kophondo, as umkhosi wempi. at <b>20</b> Kuyamenyezelwa incithakalo phezu kwencithakalo, au ngoba ilizwe lonke lichithekile. Amathente ami ahle achithwa, av amakhetheni ami ngokucwayiza kwelihlo. aw <b>21</b> Koze kube nini ngibona isiboniso, ngizwe ukukhala kophondo? ax 
<b>22</b> Ngoba abantu bami bayizithutha, mina kabangazi; ay bangabantwana abayiziphukuphuku, kabalakuqedisisa; bahlakaniphele ukwenza okubi, az kodwa ukwenza okuhle kabakwazi. <b>23</b> Ngabona umhlaba, ba khangela-ke, wawungelasimo, ungelalutho; bb lemazulwini, kodwa kakukho ukukhanya kwawo. bc <b>24</b> Ngabona izintaba, bd khangela-ke, zazinyikinyeka, lamaqaqa wonke ayenyakazela. <b>25</b> Ngabona, khangela-ke, kwakungelamuntu, be lenyoni zonke zamazulu bf zibalekile. bg 
<b>26</b> Ngabona, khangela-ke, ilizwe elivundileyo laliyinkangala, bh lemizi yalo yonke yayidiliziwe phambi kweNkosi, phambi kokuvutha kolaka lwayo. bi <b>27</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi: Ilizwe lonke lizakuba lunxiwa, kodwa kangiyikuqeda ngokupheleleyo. bj <b>28</b> Ngenxa yalokhu umhlaba uzalila, bk lamazulu aphezulu abe mnyama; bl , bm ngenxa yokuthi ngikhulumile, ngikuhlosile, njalo kangiyikuzisola, bn njalo kangiyikubuyela emuva ngisuka kukho. 
<b>29</b> Umuzi wonke uzabaleka ngenxa yomsindo wabagadi bamabhiza labatshoki ngamadandili; bo bazangena ezixukwini, bakhwele emadwaleni; bp wonke umuzi uzatshiywa, njalo kungabi lamuntu ohlala kuwo. bq <b>30</b> Wena-ke ozachitheka, br uzakwenzani? Loba ugqoka okubomvu, loba uzicecisa ngezigqizo zegolide, bs loba ucomba amehlo akho ngomphendulo, bt uzenza ube muhle ngeze, izithandwa zakho bu zizakudelela, bv zidinga impilo yakho. 
<b>31</b> Ngoba ngizwile ilizwi kungathi ngelowesifazana ohelelwayo, bw ubuhlungu obunjengobeletha umntwana olizibulo, ilizwi lendodakazi yeZiyoni, ikhefuzela, iselula izandla zayo bx isithi: Maye-ke mina! Ngoba umphefumulo wami uphele amandla ngenxa yababulali. by 
<b>Jeremías 5</b><br><b>1</b> Hambani liye le lale ezitaladeni zeJerusalema, a libone khathesi, lazi, lidinge ezindaweni zayo ezingamagceke, uba lingathola umuntu, b uba ekhona owenza isahlulelo, c odinga iqiniso; khona ngizayithethelela. d <b>2</b> Loba-ke besithi: e Kuphila kukaJehova; f kanti bafunga amanga. g , h <b>3</b> Nkosi, amehlo akho kawakho eqinisweni yini? i Ubatshayile, j , k kodwa kabezwanga buhlungu; l ubaqothule, kodwa bala ukwemukela ukulaywa. m Benzile ubuso babo bube lukhuni kuledwala, n bala ukuphenduka. o 
<b>4</b> Mina ngasengisithi: Qiniso, laba ngabayanga, bayizithutha; p ngoba kabayazi indlela yeNkosi, q isahlulelo sikaNkulunkulu wabo. r <b>5</b> Mina ngizakuya kwabakhulu, ngikhulume labo, ngoba bona bayayazi indlela yeNkosi, s isahlulelo sikaNkulunkulu wabo; t kodwa labo kanyekanye balephulile ijogwe, u baqamula izibopho. v 
<b>6</b> Ngakho-ke isilwane w esiphuma ehlathini sizababulala, x impisi yezinkangala ibachithe, y ingwe ilinde imelene lemizi yabo; z wonke ophuma kiyo uzadatshudatshulwa; ngoba iziphambeko zabo zinengi, ukuhlehlela nyovane aa kwabo kwandile. ab <b>7</b> Ngingakuthethelela njani ngalokhu? ac Abantwana bakho bangidelile, ad bafunga ae ngabangeyisibo onkulunkulu. af Sengibasuthisile, ag basebefeba, ah babuthana ngamaxuku endlini yezifebe. ai <b>8</b> Babenjengamabhiza asuthayo ekuseni, bakhonya, aj omunye lomunye kumfazi kamakhelwane wakhe. ak 
<b>9</b> Kangiyikujezisa yini ngenxa yalezizinto? al itsho iNkosi; kumbe umphefumulo wami kawuyikuphindisela yini esizweni esinjengalesi? <b>10</b> Yenyukelani emidulwini yayo, lichithe, am kodwa lingenzi isiphetho esipheleleyo; an susani ingatsha zayo, ao ngoba kazisizo zeNkosi. <b>11</b> Ngoba indlu kaIsrayeli lendlu kaJuda zenze ngenkohliso enkulu zimelene lami, ap itsho iNkosi. <b>12</b> Bayiphikile iNkosi, aq bathi: Kayisiyo; njalo ububi kabuyikusehlela, futhi kasiyikubona inkemba loba indlala. ar 
<b>13</b> Labaprofethi bazakuba ngumoya, as lelizwi kalikho kubo; kuzakwenziwa njalo kibo! <b>14</b> Ngalokho itsho njalo iNkosi uNkulunkulu wamabandla: Ngoba likhuluma lelilizwi, khangela, ngizakwenza amazwi ami emlonyeni wakho at abe ngumlilo, au lalababantu babe zinkuni, av njalo uzabaqothula. <b>15</b> Khangelani, ngizaletha phezu kwenu isizwe aw esivela khatshana, ax ndlu kaIsrayeli, itsho iNkosi; yisizwe esilamandla, ay yisizwe esidala, isizwe ongalwaziyo ulimi lwaso, az longayikuzwa esikukhulumayo. ba 
<b>16</b> Isamba saso semitshoko bb sinjengengcwaba elivulekileyo; bc bonke bangamaqhawe. <b>17</b> Njalo sizakudla isivuno sakho lokudla kwakho; bd sizakudla amadodana akho lamadodakazi akho; sizakudla izimvu zakho lenkomo zakho; sizakudla ivini lakho lomkhiwa wakho; lemizi yakho ebiyelweyo be othembela kiyo, sizayithelela ubuyanga ngenkemba. <b>18</b> Kodwa langalezonsuku, itsho iNkosi, kangiyikwenza isiphetho esipheleleyo kini. bf 
<b>19</b> Kuzakuthi-ke lapho lisithi: INkosi uNkulunkulu wethu yenzeleni zonke lezizinto kithi? bg Khona uzakutsho kubo uthi: Njengoba lingidelile, bh lakhonza onkulunkulu bemzini elizweni lakini, ngokunjalo lizakhonza abezizwe elizweni elingeyisilo lenu. bi <b>20</b> Memezelani lokhu endlini kaJakobe, likuzwakalise koJuda, lisithi: <b>21</b> Zwanini-ke lokhu, lina bantu abayizithutha bj , bk labangelangqondo, bl elilamehlo, kodwa lingaboni, bm elilendlebe, kodwa lingezwa. 
<b>22</b> Kalingesabi yini? bn itsho iNkosi; kaliyikuthuthumela phambi kwami yini, engabeka itshebetshebe laba ngumngcele wolwandle bo ngesimiso saphakade bp olungeledlule? Lanxa amagagasi alo etshayana bq kube kanti angenqobe; lanxa ehlokoma br kube kanti kawayikudlula phezu kwawo. <b>23</b> Kodwa lababantu balenhliziyo evukelayo lephikayo; bs baphambukile, bahamba. <b>24</b> Futhi kabatsho enhliziyweni zabo ukuthi: Ake sesabe iNkosi uNkulunkulu wethu, enika izulu bt lakuqala bu lelamuva bv ngesikhathi salo, esigcinela bw amaviki amisiweyo esivuno. bx 
<b>25</b> Iziphambeko zenu ziphambule lezizinto, by lezono zenu zaligodlela okuhle. bz <b>26</b> Ngoba phakathi kwabantu bami kutholakala abakhohlakeleyo; bacatheme ca njengabathiya inyoni; babeka umgibe ochithayo, babambe abantu. <b>27</b> Njengezongo ligcwele inyoni, zinjalo izindlu zabo zigcwele inkohliso; ngakho-ke sebebakhulu, banothile. <b>28</b> Bazimukile, cb bancwaba, yebo, bedlula imisebenzi yomubi; kabameli udaba, cc udaba lwezintandane, cd kube kanti bayaphumelela; ce lelungelo labaswelayo kabalahluleli. 
<b>29</b> Kangiyikubajezisa ngalezizinto yini? cf kutsho iNkosi. Umphefumulo wami kawuyikuphindisela esizweni esinjengalesi yini? <b>30</b> Okwethusayo lokwesabekayo kwenzakele elizweni. cg <b>31</b> Abaprofethi baprofetha ch ngamanga, ci labapristi babusa ngezandla zabo; cj labantu bami bakuthanda kunjalo; ck kodwa lizakwenzani ekupheleni kwakho? 
<b>Jeremías 6</b><br><b>1</b> Lina bantwana bakoBhenjamini, a buthanelani ukubaleka liphume phakathi kweJerusalema, b livuthele uphondo c eThekhowa, d liphakamise uphawu lomlilo e eBeti-Hakeremi: f ngoba ububi bubona phansi busenyakatho, g lencithakalo enkulu. <b>2</b> Indodakazi yeZiyoni ngiyifananise lowesifazana omuhle obuthakathaka. h <b>3</b> Abelusi bazafika kuye lemihlambi yabo; i bazamisa amathente beqondene laye inhlangothi zonke, j beluse ngulowo lalowo endaweni yakhe. k 
<b>4</b> Zehlukaniseleni impi limelene layo; l sukumani, kasenyuke emini enkulu. m Maye kithi! ngoba ilanga selithambeme ▼▼ selithambeme: Heb seliphendukile. , o, ngoba amathunzi okuhlwa aselulekile. <b>5</b> Sukumani, senyuke ngobusuku, sidilize izigodlo zayo. <b>6</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla: Gamulani izihlahla, p libuthelele umduli wokuvimbezela limelene leJerusalema. q Yiwo lo umuzi ozajeziswa; r kulocindezelo kuphela phakathi kwawo. 
<b>7</b> Njengomthombo umpompoza amanzi awo, s ngokunjalo umpompoza inkohlakalo yawo; udlakela t , u lencithakalo kuzwakala kuwo; v phambi kwami kulenhlungu lamanxeba njalonjalo. <b>8</b> Layeka, Jerusalema, hlezi umphefumulo wami wehlukane lawe; w hlezi ngikwenze unxiwa, ilizwe elingahlalwanga. x <b>9</b> Itsho njalo INkosi yamabandla: Bazayikhothoza lokuyikhothoza insali kaIsrayeli njengevini; y buyisela isandla sakho ezitsheni njengomvuni wevini. 
<b>10</b> Ngizakhuluma kubani, z ngixwayise, ukuze bezwe? Khangela, indlebe yabo kayisokwanga, aa kabalakulalela; ab khangela, ilizwi leNkosi lilihlazo kibo, ac kabathokozi ngalo. <b>11</b> Ngakho ngigcwele ulaka lweNkosi, ngikhathele yikuzithiba. ad Ngizakuthululela phezu kwabantwana ae esitaladeni lemhlanganweni af wamajaha ndawonye, ag , ah ngoba lendoda kanye lomfazi bazathathwa, ai omdala kanye logcwele izinsuku. aj 
<b>12</b> Lezindlu zabo zizanikelwa kwabanye, ak , al amasimu labafazi kanyekanye; am ngoba ngizakwelulela isandla sami phezu kwabahlali belizwe, itsho iNkosi. <b>13</b> Ngoba kusukela komncinyane wabo kuze kufike komkhulu wabo an bonke bayizihwaba zenzuzo embi; ao njalo kusukela komprofethi kuze kube kompristi bonke benza ngenkohliso. ap <b>14</b> Bapholisa kancane inxeba labantu bami, aq besithi: Ukuthula, ukuthula; ar lapho kungekho ukuthula. as 
<b>15</b> Baba lenhloni yini lapho besenza isinengiso? at Hatshi, kababanga lanhloni lakanye, au futhi kabakwazi ukuyangeka. Ngakho bazakuwa phakathi kwabawayo; av ngesikhathi sokubahambela kwami bazakhubeka, itsho iNkosi. <b>16</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Manini ezindleleni, libone, libuze ngemikhondo yasendulo, aw ukuthi ingaphi indlela enhle, lihambe kuyo, njalo lizazuzela ax imiphefumulo yenu ukuthula. ay Kodwa bathi: Kasiyikuhamba ngayo. az <b>17</b> Futhi ngilibekele abalindi, ba ngisithi: Lalelani ukukhala bb kophondo. bc Kodwa bathi: Kasiyikulalela. 
<b>18</b> Ngakho zwanini, lina zizwe, wazi, wena bandla, okuphakathi kwabo. <b>19</b> Zwana, wena mhlaba; bd khangela, ngizaletha ububi phezu kwalababantu, isithelo semicabango yabo, be ngoba bengalalelanga amazwi ami, lomlayo wami, kodwa bawalile. <b>20</b> Izelani kimi bf impepha bg ivela eShebha, bh lomhlanga olephunga elimnandi ovela elizweni elikhatshana? bi Iminikelo yenu yokutshiswa kayemukeleki, bj lemihlatshelo yenu kayimnandi kimi. 
<b>21</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi: Khangela, ngizabeka izikhubekiso phambi kwalababantu, bk njalo oyise lamadodana bakhubeke kanyekanye kuzo, umakhelwane lomngane wakhe, bazabhubha. <b>22</b> Itsho njalo INkosi: Khangela, abantu bayeza bevela bl elizweni lenyakatho, bm lesizwe esikhulu sizavuswa ezinhlangothini zomhlaba. bn <b>23</b> Bazabamba idandili lomkhonto; bo balesihluku, bp kabalasihawu; bq ilizwi labo lihlokoma njengolwandle; br bagade amabhiza, behleliwe njengabantu bempi bemelene lawe, ndodakazi yeZiyoni. 
<b>24</b> Siyizwile indumela yakho, izandla zethu ziyadangala; bs usizi lusibambile, ubuhlungu obunjengobowesifazana obelethayo. bt <b>25</b> Lingaphumi liye egangeni, lingahambi endleleni, ngoba kukhona inkemba yesitha, ukwesaba inhlangothi zonke. bu <b>26</b> Wena ndodakazi yabantu bami, bhinca amasaka, bv ubhuquze bw emlotheni, bx uzenzele isililo, by esinjengesendodana eyiyo yodwa, ukukhala okubuhlungu kakhulu; bz ngoba umchithi uzafika kithi masinyane. 
<b>27</b> Ngikubekile ube ngumphotshongo ca lenqaba phakathi kwabantu bami, cb ukuze wazi uhlole indlela yabo. cc <b>28</b> Bonke bangabahlamuki abalenkani, cd behamba behleba; ce balithusi lensimbi, cf bonke bangabonakalisi. cg <b>29</b> Imvutho itshile, umnuso usuqedwe ngumlilo; ch umncibilikisi uncibilikisela ize, ci ngoba ababi kabakhutshwa. cj <b>30</b> Bazababiza ngokuthi bayisiliva esilahliweyo, ck ngoba iNkosi ibalahlile. 
<b>Jeremías 7</b><br><b>1</b> Ilizwi elafika kuJeremiya livela eNkosini lisithi: <b>2</b> Mana esangweni lendlu yeNkosi a umemezele khona lelilizwi uthi: Zwanini ilizwi leNkosi, lina bonke bakoJuda, abangena kulamasango ukuzakhonza iNkosi. <b>3</b> Itsho njalo INkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu wakoIsrayeli: Lungisani indlela zenu lezenzo zenu, b khona ngizalenza ukuthi lihlale kulindawo. c <b>4</b> Lingathembi lina emazwini amanga d okuthi: La alithempeli leNkosi, ithempeli leNkosi, ithempeli leNkosi. 
<b>5</b> Ngoba uba lilungisisa lokulungisa izindlela zenu lezenzo zenu; e uba lisenza lokwenza isahlulelo phakathi komuntu lomakhelwane wakhe; f <b>6</b> lingacindezeli g owezizweni, h intandane, i , j lomfelokazi, lingachithi igazi elingelacala kulindawo; k futhi lingalandeli abanye onkulunkulu l ukuze kube kubi kini; m <b>7</b> khona ngizalenza ukuthi lihlale kulindawo, n , o elizweni p engalinika oyihlo, q kusukela endulo kuze kube nininini. r <b>8</b> Khangelani, lina lithembela s emazwini amanga angasizi lutho. t 
<b>9</b> Lizakweba, libulale, lifebe, u lifunge amanga, v litshisele uBhali impepha, w lilandele abanye onkulunkulu elingabaziyo yini? x <b>10</b> Beselisiza lime phambi kwami kulindlu, ebizwa ngebizo lami, lithi: Sikhululwe ukuze senze zonke lezizinengiso. <b>11</b> Indlu le, y ebizwa ngebizo lami, isibe lubhalu z lwabaphangi aa emehlweni enu yini? Khangelani, mina sengibonile, itsho iNkosi.

<b>15</b> Njalo ngizalixotsha lisuke ebusweni bami, njengalokho ngaxotsha bonke abafowenu, ak inzalo yonke kaEfrayimi. al <b>16</b> Ngakho wena ungabakhulekeli lababantu, am , an ungabaphakamiseli ao ukukhala loba umkhuleko, ap ungalabheli kimi, aq ngoba kangiyikukuzwa. ar AbakoJuda badumisa onkulunkulu babezizwe <b>17</b> Kawukuboni yini abakwenza emizini yakoJuda lezitaladeni zeJerusalema? 
<b>18</b> Abantwana batheza inkuni, laboyise baphembe umlilo, labesifazana baxove inhlama, as ukuze benzele indlovukazi yamazulu at amaqebelengwana, au bathululele abanye onkulunkulu iminikelo yokunathwayo, av , aw ukuze bangithukuthelise. ax <b>19</b> Bathukuthelisa mina yini? ay itsho iNkosi. Kabazenzeni yini az kuze kusanganiseke ubuso babo? ba 
<b>20</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi uJehova: Khangela, intukuthelo yami lolaka lwami kuzathululelwa kulindawo, bb phezu komuntu, laphezu kwenyamazana, bc laphezu kwezihlahla zasendle, laphezu kwesithelo somhlabathi; njalo kuzakutsha, kungacitshwa. bd , be AbakoJuda bazatshaywa ngenxa yokuhlamuka kwabo <b>21</b> Itsho njalo INkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Fakani iminikelo yenu yokutshiswa kanye lemihlatshelo yenu, bf lidle inyama. bg 
<b>22</b> Ngoba kangikhulumanga kuboyihlo, bh njalo kangibalayanga ngosuku engabakhupha ngalo elizweni leGibhithe, mayelana lendaba zeminikelo yokutshiswa kumbe imihlatshelo. bi <b>23</b> Kodwa ngabalaya le into ngisithi: Lalelani ilizwi lami, bj , bk njalo ngizakuba nguNkulunkulu wenu, bl lina-ke libe ngabantu bami; njalo hambani ngayo yonke indlela engililaya yona, bm ukuze kulilungele. bn 
<b>24</b> Kodwa kabalalelanga, kabathobanga indlebe yabo; bo kodwa bp bahamba ngamacebo, bq ngenkani yenhliziyo yabo embi, br bahlehlela nyovane, bs hatshi-ke phambili. <b>25</b> Kusukela osukwini oyihlo abaphuma ngalo elizweni leGibhithe kuze kube yilolusuku, ngithume kini zonke inceku zami, abaprofethi, bt insuku ngensuku ngivuka ngovivi, ngithuma. bu , bv <b>26</b> Kanti kabangilalelanga, bw njalo kababekanga indlebe yabo, bx kodwa benza intamo yabo yaba lukhuni; by benza kubi kulaboyise. bz 
<b>27</b> Ngakho uzakhuluma wonke lamazwi kibo, ca kodwa kabayikukulalela; futhi uzababiza, cb kodwa kabayikukuphendula. <b>28</b> Ngakho uzakuthi kibo: Lesi yisizwe esingalaleli ilizwi leNkosi, uNkulunkulu waso, lesingemukeli ukulaywa; cc iqiniso liphelile, cd liqunyiwe emlonyeni waso. <b>29</b> Gela ce inwele zakho, cf wena Jerusalema, uzilahle, uphakamise isililo emadundulwini; cg ngoba iNkosi isalile yasidela isizukulwana solaka lwayo. 
<b>30</b> Ngoba abantwana bakoJuda benzile ububi emehlweni ami, itsho iNkosi; babekile izinengiso zabo endlini ch ebizwa ngebizo lami, ci ukuze bayingcolise. cj <b>31</b> Bakhile-ke indawo eziphakemeyo ck zeTofethi, cl ezisesihotsheni sendodana kaHinomu, cm ukuze batshise amadodana abo lamadodakazi abo ngomlilo; cn engingabalayanga khona, co , cp lokungavelanga enhliziyweni yami. 
<b>32</b> Ngakho khangela, insuku ziyeza, cq itsho iNkosi, lapho engasayikubizwa ngokuthi yiTofethi, cr loba isihotsha sendodana kaHinomu, cs kodwa isihotsha sokubulala; ngoba bazangcwaba eTofethi, kuze kungabi landawo. ct <b>33</b> Lezidumbu zalababantu zizakuba yikudla kwenyoni zamazulu, cu lezezinyamazana zomhlaba, kungekho cv ozazethusa. <b>34</b> Khona ngizakwenza cw kuphele emizini yakoJuda cx lezitaladeni zeJerusalema ilizwi lenjabulo, cy lelizwi lentokozo, ilizwi lomyeni, cz lelizwi likamakoti, ngoba ilizwe lizakuba lunxiwa. da 
<b>Jeremías 8</b><br><b>1</b> Ngalesosikhathi, itsho iNkosi, bazakhupha amathambo amakhosi akoJuda, a lamathambo eziphathamandla zayo, lamathambo abapristi, lamathambo abaprofethi, lamathambo abahlali beJerusalema, emangcwabeni abo; <b>2</b> baweneke b elangeni c lenyangeni, lakulo lonke ibutho lamazulu, d abakuthandayo, labakusebenzelayo, labakulandelayo, labakudingayo, labakukhonzayo; e , f kawayikubuthwa, g kawayikungcwatshwa; h azakuba ngawomquba phezu kobuso bomhlaba. i 
<b>3</b> Njalo ukufa kuzakhethwa kulempilo yiyo yonke insali yabaseleyo balolusapho olubi, j kuzo zonke indawo zabaseleyo lapho engibaxotshele khona, k itsho iNkosi yamabandla. Izono lokugweba <b>4</b> Uzakuthi kibo futhi: Itsho njalo INkosi: Bazakuwa l bangavuki yini? Uzaphambuka angaphenduki yini? <b>5</b> Pho, baphambukelani lababantu eJerusalema ngokuphambuka m okulaphakade? n Babambelela enkohlisweni, o bayala ukuphenduka. p 
<b>6</b> Ngilalele ngezwa, q kabakhulumanga kuhle; kakulamuntu ozisolayo ngobubi bakhe esithi: Ngenzeni? Wonke uphendukela emjahweni wakhe r njengebhiza ligijimela s empini. t <b>7</b> Yebo ingabuzane emazulwini liyazazi izikhathi zalo ezimisiweyo; u , v lejuba lehemu w lenkonjane kuyagcina isikhathi sokubuya kwakho; x kodwa abantu bami kabazazi izimiso zeNkosi. y , z <b>8</b> Litsho njani ukuthi: Sihlakaniphile, lomlayo weNkosi ulathi? aa Khangelani, isibili usiba lwamanga lwababhali lusebenzela ize. ab 
<b>9</b> Abahlakaniphileyo bayangekile, ac besabe kakhulu bathethwe; ad khangela, balalile ilizwi leNkosi; pho balenhlakanipho bani? <b>10</b> Ngakho omkabo ngizabanika abanye, ae amasimu abo kwabazakudla ilifa labo; ngoba kusukela komncinyane kuze kufike komkhulu bonke bayizihwaba zenzuzo embi, kusukela kumprofethi kuze kube kumpristi wonke wenza ngenkohliso. <b>11</b> Ngoba balipholise kancane inxeba lendodakazi yabantu bami, besithi: Ukuthula, ukuthula; khona kungelakuthula. 
<b>12</b> Baba lenhloni yini lapho sebenze amanyala? Hatshi, kababanga lanhloni lakanye, af futhi kabakwazi ukuyangeka; ngakho bazakuwa phakathi kwabawayo; ngesikhathi sokuhanjelwa kwabo bazawiselwa phansi, itsho iNkosi. <b>13</b> Isibili ngizabaqeda, itsho iNkosi; kakuyikuba khona ag izithelo zevini evinini; ah , ai njalo kakuyikuba khona imikhiwa emkhiweni, aj lehlamvu lizabuna; ak lengibanike khona kuzakwedlula kibo. 
<b>14</b> Sihlaleleni? Buthanani, al singene emizini evikelweyo, am sithule khona; an ngoba iNkosi uNkulunkulu wethu isithulisile, yasinathisa amanzi enyongo, ao ngoba sonile eNkosini. <b>15</b> Salindela ukuthula, ap kodwa kakubanga lokuhle; isikhathi sokwelatshwa, kodwa khangela, luhlupho. aq <b>16</b> Ukuthimula kwamabhiza akhe kwezwakala ar kusukela koDani; as ilizwe lonke lathuthumela emsindweni wokukhonya at kwalamandla akhe; au ngoba afikile, adla ilizwe av lokugcwala kwalo, umuzi labahlala kiwo. 
<b>17</b> Ngoba khangelani, ngithumela phakathi kwenu izinyoka, aw amabululu, ax angelakulunjwa, njalo azaliluma, ay itsho iNkosi. UJeremiya ukhalela ilizwe lakoJuda <b>18</b> Ukududuzwa kwami kusosizini, inhliziyo yami iphelelwa ngamandla kimi. az <b>19</b> Khangelani, ilizwi lokukhala kwendodakazi yabantu bami livela elizweni elikhatshana. ba INkosi kayikho eZiyoni yini? <b>Jeremías 9</b><br><b>1</b> Kungathi ngabe ikhanda lami lingamanzi, a lamehlo ami umthombo wezinyembezi, ukuze ngikhalele ababuleweyo bendodakazi yabantu bami b emini lebusuku! <b>2</b> Kungathi ngabe ngilendawo yokulala yezihambi c enkangala, ukuze ngitshiye abantu bami, ngisuke kubo! Ngoba bonke bayizifebe, d ixuku e labakhohlisayo. f <b>3</b> Njalo bagobisa ulimi lwabo njengedandili labo besenzela amanga; g kodwa kabaliqineli h iqiniso elizweni; i ngoba baqhubeka kusukela kokubi kusiya kokubi, mina-ke kabangazi, j , k itsho iNkosi. 
<b>4</b> Qaphelani, ngulowo lalowo umakhelwane wakhe, l lingathembi lawuphi umfowenu, m ngoba wonke umfowenu uzakhohlisa lokukhohlisa, n laye wonke umakhelwane uzahamba ngokuhleba. o <b>5</b> Njalo bazakhohlisa, ngulowo lalowo umakhelwane wakhe, bengakhulumi iqiniso; balufundisile ulimi lwabo ukukhuluma amanga, bezidinisa ngokwenza okubi. p <b>6</b> Indawo yakho yokuhlala iphakathi kwenkohliso; ngenkohliso bayala ukungazi, q itsho iNkosi. 
<b>7</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla: Khangela, ngizabancibilikisa, r ngibahlole; s ngoba ngizakwenza njani t ngenxa yendodakazi yabantu bami? u <b>8</b> Ulimi lwabo lungumtshoko wokubulala, v , w lukhuluma inkohliso; x ngulowo lalowo ukhuluma ukuthula ngomlomo wakhe kumakhelwane wakhe, y kodwa ngaphakathi kwakhe uyamcathamela. z <b>9</b> Kangiyikubajezisa yini ngenxa yalezizinto? aa itsho iNkosi; umphefumulo wami kawuyikuphindisela yini esizweni esinjengalesi? 
<b>10</b> Ngizaphakamisa ukukhala lesililo phezu kwezintaba, laphezu kwezindawo zokuhlala zenkangala ingoma yokulila, ab , ac ngoba kutshisiwe, ad okokuthi kungekho odlulayo; njalo bangezwa ilizwi lezifuyo; kusukela enyonini zamazulu kusiya enyamazaneni ae kubalekile, kuhambile. af <b>11</b> Ngizayenza iJerusalema ibe zinqumbi, ag umlindi ah wemigobho; ai lemizi yakoJuda ngizayenza ibe lunxiwa, ingabi lamhlali. Ilizwe liyachitheka labantu bayakhala

<b>15</b> Ngalokho itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Khangela, ngizabondla ap lababantu ngomhlonyane, aq ngibanathise amanzi enyongo. ar <b>16</b> Njalo ngizabahlakaza phakathi kwezizwe as , at abangazanga bazazi bona laboyise, au ngithume inkemba emva kwabo, av , aw ngize ngibaqede. <b>17</b> Itsho njalo INkosi yamabandla: Qaphelani, libize abesifazana abalilayo ukuthi beze; ax lithumele kwabesifazana abahlakaniphileyo, ukuthi beze. 
<b>18</b> Kabaphangise, basiphakamisele isililo, ukuze amehlo ethu ehlise inyembezi, ay lenkophe zethu zimpompoze amanzi. <b>19</b> Ngoba ilizwi lokulila liyezwakala liphuma eZiyoni lisithi: Yeka ukuchitheka kwethu! az Silenhloni kakhulu, ngoba silitshiyile ilizwe, ngoba babhidlizile izindawo zethu zokuhlala. ba <b>20</b> Kanti zwanini ilizwi leNkosi, lina besifazana, lendlebe zenu zilemukele ilizwi lomlomo wayo; lifundise amadodakazi enu ukuqhinqa isililo, kube ngulowo lalowo umakhelwane wakhe ingoma yokulila.

<b>24</b> kodwa ozincomayo kazincome ngalokhu, bl , bm ukuthi uyaqedisisa uyangazi mina, bn ukuthi ngiyiNkosi esebenzisa uthandolomusa, isahlulelo, lokulunga emhlabeni; ngoba kulezizinto ngiyathokoza, bo itsho iNkosi. <b>25</b> Khangela, insuku ziyeza, bp itsho iNkosi, lapho ngizajezisa bonke bq abasokileyo ekungasokini: br <b>26</b> IGibhithe, loJuda, loEdoma, labantwana bakoAmoni, loMowabi, bs labo bonke abaphoselwe engonsini, abahlala enkangala; ngoba zonke izizwe kazisokwanga, layo yonke indlu kaIsrayeli kayisokwanga enhliziyweni. bt 
<b>Jeremías 10</b><br><b>1</b> Zwanini ilizwi iNkosi elikhuluma kini, lina ndlu kaIsrayeli. <b>2</b> Itsho njalo INkosi: Lingafundi indlela yabahedeni, lingatshaywa luvalo ngezibonakaliso zamazulu, a ngenxa yokuthi abahedeni bayazesaba. <b>3</b> Ngoba imikhuba yabantu iyize; b ngoba kuganyulwa isihlahla ehlathini, c umsebenzi wezandla zengcitshi, ngehloka. d <b>4</b> Basicecisa ngesiliva langegolide, e basiqinisa ngezipikili langezando, f , g ukuze singazamazami. 
<b>5</b> Zinjengesihlahla h selala somsebenzi obaziweyo, i kodwa kazikhulumi; j kusweleka zithwalwe, k ngoba zingehambe; lingazesabi, ngoba zingelakwenza okubi, futhi kakukho ukwenza okuhle kuzo. l <b>6</b> Njengoba kungekho onjengawe, m Nkosi; umkhulu, lebizo lakho likhulu ngobuqhawe. n <b>7</b> Ngubani ongekwesabe, o wena Nkosi yezizwe? p ngoba kukufanele. Ngoba phakathi kwabo bonke abahlakaniphileyo bezizwe, q lakuyo yonke imibuso yabo, kakho onjengawe. r 
<b>8</b> Kodwa kanyekanye banjengezinyamazana s bayiziphukuphuku; t isigodo siyimfundiso sokuyize. u <b>9</b> Isiliva esikhandiweyo v silethwa sivela eTarshishi, w legolide livela eUfazi, x umsebenzi wengcitshi, y lowezandla zomkhandi wegolide; z ziluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka lokuyibubende izigqoko zabo; aa konke kungumsebenzi wabahlakaniphileyo. ab <b>10</b> Kodwa iNkosi inguNkulunkulu weqiniso, inguNkulunkulu ophilayo lenkosi yaphakade. ac Ekuthukutheleni kwayo umhlaba uyazamazama, lezizwe kazilakulumela ulaka lwayo.

<b>14</b> Wonke umuntu unjengenyamazana elwazini; aq wonke umkhandi wegolide uyangiswa yisithombe esibaziweyo; ar ngoba isithombe sakhe esibunjwe ngokuncibilikisa siyinkohliso, as njalo kakulamphefumulo kuzo. at <b>15</b> Ziyize, au umsebenzi wezinkohliso; ngesikhathi sempindiselo yazo zizabhubha. av <b>16</b> Isabelo sikaJakobe kasinjengazo, aw ngoba ungumbumbi wakho konke, loIsrayeli uyintonga yelifa lakhe; ax iNkosi yamabandla libizo layo. ay <b>17</b> Qoqa impahla yakho uyikhuphe elizweni, az wena mhlalikazi wenqaba. ba 
<b>18</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi: Khangela, ngizabajikijela ngaphandle abahlali belizwe bb ngalesisikhathi, bc ngibacindezele bd ukuze bakuthole. be Ukuchitheka kwelakoJuda <b>19</b> Maye kimi ngenxa yokwephuka kwami! bf Inxeba lami linzima; bg kodwa mina ngathi: Isibili lokhu yinhlupheko bh yami engizayithwala. bi <b>20</b> Ithente lami lidiliziwe, bj lentambo zami zonke ziqamukile; abantwana bami basukile kimi, njalo kabakho; bk kakho ongelula ithente lami futhi, amise amakhetheni ami. bl 
<b>21</b> Ngoba abelusi bm sebenjengenyamazana, bn kabadinganga iNkosi; ngakho-ke kabaphumelelanga, bo lomhlambi wabo wonke uhlakazekile. <b>22</b> Khangela, umsindo wombiko ufikile, bp lokuzamazama okukhulu okuvela elizweni lenyakatho, bq okuzakwenza imizi yakoJuda ibe lunxiwa, br indawo yokuhlala yemigobho. bs <b>23</b> Nkosi, ngiyazi ukuthi indlela yomuntu kayikho kuye; bt kakukho emuntwini ohambayo ukuthi aqondise ukunyathela kwakhe. bu <b>24</b> Nkosi, ngilaya, kodwa ngesahlulelo, bv hatshi olakeni lwakho, hlezi unginciphise.

<b>Jeremías 11</b><br><b>1</b> Ilizwi elafika kuJeremiya livela eNkosini, lisithi: <b>2</b> Zwanini amazwi a alesisivumelwano, b likhulume ebantwini bakoJuda lakubahlali beJerusalema. <b>3</b> Njalo uthi kibo: Itsho njalo INkosi uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Uqalekisiwe umuntu ongalaleli amazwi alesisivumelwano, c <b>4</b> engawalayela oyihlo ngosuku engibakhupha ngalo elizweni leGibhithe, esithandweni sensimbi, d ngisithi: Lalelani ilizwi lami, e liwenze njengakho konke engililaya khona; ngalokho lizakuba ngabantu bami, f lami ngibe nguNkulunkulu wenu, 
<b>5</b> ukuze ngimise isifungo engasifunga kuboyihlo g ukuthi ngibanike ilizwe eligeleza uchago loluju, njengalamuhla. h Ngasengiphendula ngathi: Ameni, Nkosi. <b>6</b> INkosi yasisithi kimi: Memezela wonke lamazwi emizini yakoJuda i lezitaladeni zeJerusalema, usithi: Zwanini amazwi alesisivumelwano, j liwenze. k , l <b>7</b> Ngoba ngafakaza lokufakaza kuboyihlo ngosuku engabakhuphula ngalo elizweni leGibhithe, kuze kube lamuhla, ngivuka ngovivi ngifakaza, m ngisithi: Lalelani ilizwi lami. n 
<b>8</b> Kodwa kabalalelanga, o kababekanga indlebe yabo, kodwa bahamba, ngulowo lalowo ngobulukhuni benhliziyo yabo embi. p Ngakho ngizaletha phezu kwabo wonke amazwi alesisivumelwano, q engabalaya ukuthi bawenze, r kodwa kabawenzanga. <b>9</b> INkosi yasisithi kimi: Kutholakele ugobe phakathi kwabantu bakoJuda, s laphakathi kwabahlali beJerusalema. 
<b>10</b> Sebebuyele eziphambekweni zabokhokho babo, t abala ukuzwa ilizwi lami; bona balandela abanye onkulunkulu ukuze babakhonze; indlu yakoIsrayeli lendlu yakoJuda zisephulile isivumelwano sami engasenza laboyise. u <b>11</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi: Khangela, ngizaletha okubi phezu kwabo, abangeke baphume kukho; lapho bekhala kimi, v kangiyikubezwa. <b>12</b> Khona imizi yakoJuda labahlali beJerusalema bezahamba bakhale kubonkulunkulu ababatshisela impepha, kodwa kabayikubasindisa lokubasindisa ngesikhathi sobubi babo. 
<b>13</b> Ngoba njengenani lemizi yakho babenjalo onkulunkulu bakho, w Juda, lanjengenani lezitalada zeJerusalema limisile amalathi kulokhu okuyangisayo, amalathi okutshisela uBhali impepha. <b>14</b> Ngakho wena ungabakhulekeli lababantu, x futhi ungabaphakamiseli ukukhala kumbe umkhuleko, y ngoba kangiyikuzwa ngesikhathi sokukhala kwabo kimi ngenxa yenhlupheko yabo. z <b>15</b> Othandekayo wami aa ulani ab endlini yami, lokhu enze amanyala labanengi, ac lenyama engcwele isiyedlule kuwe yini? ad Lapho usenza okubi, kulapho uthokoza. ae 
<b>16</b> INkosi yabiza ibizo lakho ngokuthi: Isihlahla somhlwathi esiluhlaza esihle ngezithelo ezinhle; af ngomsindo wenhlokomo enkulu izasithungela ngomlilo, ag lengatsha zaso zephuke. ah <b>17</b> Ngoba iNkosi yamabandla, eyakuhlanyelayo, ai ikhulumile okubi ngawe, ngenxa yobubi bendlu yakoIsrayeli lobendlu yakoJuda, abakwenzileyo bemelene labo ngokwabo ukungicunula ngokutshisela uBhali impepha. aj Icebo lokubulala uJeremiya <b>18</b> INkosi yasingazisa ukuthi ngazi; wasungitshengisa izenzo zabo. 
<b>19</b> Kodwa mina nganginjengewundlu kumbe inkabi kusiwa ekuhlatshweni; ak ngoba ngangingazi ukuthi baceba amacebo bemelene lami, al besithi: Asichithe isihlahla lesithelo saso; am asimqume asuke an elizweni labaphilayo, ao ukuze ibizo lakhe lingabe lisakhunjulwa. ap <b>20</b> Kodwa, wena Nkosi yamabandla, umahluleli olungileyo, ohlola izinso aq lenhliziyo, ar , as kangibone impindiselo yakho kibo; at ngoba ngembule udaba lwami kuwe. 
<b>21</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi ngamadoda eAnathothi, au adinga impilo yakho, av esithi: Ungaprofethi ngebizo leNkosi, aw ukuze ungafi ngesandla sethu. ax <b>22</b> Ngakho itsho njalo INkosi yamabandla: Khangela, ngizawajezisa; amajaha azakufa ngenkemba, amadodana awo lamadodakazi awo azakufa ngendlala, <b>23</b> njalo kakuyikuba lensali kuwo; ngoba ngizaletha okubi phezu kwamadoda ay eAnathothi, az ngomnyaka wempindiselo yawo. ba , bb 
<b>Jeremías 12</b><br><b>1</b> Ulungile wena Nkosi, a lapho ngiphikisana lawe; kanti ngizakhuluma lawe ngezahlulelo zakho. b Kungani indlela yabakhohlakeleyo iphumelela? c Kungani behlezi kuhle d bonke abenza inkohliso ngenkohliso? e <b>2</b> Ubahlanyele, yebo, bagxilile impande; bayaqhubeka, yebo, bathele izithelo; useduze emlonyeni wabo, f kanti ukhatshana lezinso zabo. <b>3</b> Kodwa wena, Nkosi, uyangazi, g ungibonile, wahlola inhliziyo yami ngakuwe. h Bahudule i njengezimvu zokuhlatshwa, j ubehlukanisele k usuku lokuhlatshwa. l 
<b>4</b> Koze kube nini ilizwe lilila, m lohlaza lweganga lonke lubuna? Ngenxa yobubi babakhileyo kulo n izinyamazana lenyoni ziyabhubha; o ngoba bathi: Kayiyikubona isiphetho sethu. <b>5</b> Uba ugijime labezinyawo, bakudinisa, pho, ungancintisana njani lamabhiza? p Uba uthembela elizweni elilokuthula, pho, uzakwenza njani ekukhukhumaleni kweJordani? q 
<b>6</b> Ngoba ngitsho abafowenu lendlu kayihlo, r ngitsho labo bakuphatha ngenkohliso, s yebo, bona bayamemeza emva kwakho ngokugcweleyo; ungabakholwa, t loba bekhuluma izinto ezinhle kuwe. Usizi lweNkosi <b>7</b> Ngiyidelile indlu yami, ngilitshiyile ilifa lami; u nginikele othandiweyo womphefumulo wami esandleni sezitha zakhe. <b>8</b> Ilifa lami kimi linjengesilwane ehlathini; v lingiphumisele ilizwi; ngakho-ke ngilizondile. 
<b>9</b> Ilifa lami kimi linjengenyoni elamabalabala edla inyama, w inyoni ezidla inyama ziyihanqile zimelene layo; x wozani, libuthe zonke izilo zeganga, lizilethe ukuthi zidle. y <b>10</b> Abelusi abanengi basichithile z isivini sami, aa banyathelele phansi isabelo sami, ab basenzile isabelo sami esiloyisekayo saba yinkangala elunxiwa. <b>11</b> Basenze saba lunxiwa, sililela kimi ac silunxiwa; ilizwe lonke lilunxiwa, ngoba kakulamuntu okubeka enhliziyweni. ad 
<b>12</b> Abachithi bafikile phezu kwawo wonke amadundulu enkangala; ngoba inkemba yeNkosi izakudla kusukela komunye umkhawulo welizwe kusiya komunye umkhawulo welizwe; kakulakuthula kuyo yonke inyama. <b>13</b> Bahlanyele ingqoloyi, kodwa bazavuna ameva; ae bazihluphe, kabayikuzuza; af njalo lizakuba lenhloni ngezivuno zenu, ag ngenxa yokuvutha kolaka lweNkosi. ah 
<b>14</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Mayelana labo bonke omakhelwane bami ababi ai abathinta ilifa aj engenze isizwe sami uIsrayeli ukuthi sidle ilifa lalo: ak Khangela, ngizabasiphuna elizweni labo, ngiyisiphune indlu yakoJuda isuke phakathi kwabo. <b>15</b> Kuzakuthi-ke emva kokubasiphuna kwami, ngizabuya al ngibe lesihawu kubo, am ngibabuyise, an , ao kube ngulowo lalowo elifeni lakhe, langulowo lalowo elizweni lakhe. 
<b>16</b> Kuzakuthi-ke uba bezazifunda ngokukhuthala izindlela zabantu bami, ukuthi bafunge ngebizo lami bathi: Kuphila kukaJehova; ap njengalokho bafundisa abantu bami ukufunga ngoBhali, khona bezakwakhiwa phakathi kwabantu bami. aq <b>17</b> Kodwa uba bengalaleli, ngizasisiphuna lesosizwe, ngisisiphune ngisibhubhise, ar itsho iNkosi. 
<b>Jeremías 13</b><br><b>1</b> Itsho njalo INkosi kimi: Hamba uzithengele ibhanti lelembu a elicolekileyo, b ulifake ekhalweni lwakho, ungalifaki emanzini. <b>2</b> Ngasengithenga ibhanti njengokwelizwi leNkosi, ngalifaka ekhalweni lwami. c <b>3</b> Ilizwi leNkosi laselifika kimi ngokwesibili lisithi: <b>4</b> Thatha ibhanti olithengileyo elisokhalweni lwakho, usukume uye eYufrathi, ulifihle khona engoxweni yedwala. d <b>5</b> Ngasengihamba, ngalifihla ngaseYufrathi njengoba iNkosi ingilayile. 
<b>6</b> Kwasekusithi ekupheleni kwensuku ezinengi, iNkosi yasisithi kimi: Sukuma, uye eYufrathi, uyethatha khona ibhanti engakulaya ukuthi ulifihle khona. <b>7</b> Ngasengisiya eYufrathi, ngagebha, ngalithatha ibhanti endaweni lapho engangilifihle khona; khangela-ke, ibhanti lalonakele, e lalingasasizi lutho. f <b>8</b> Laselifika kimi ilizwi leNkosi lisithi: <b>9</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Ngalindlela g ngizakonakalisa h ukuziqhenya kukaJuda i lokuziqhenya okukhulu kweJerusalema. 
<b>10</b> Lababantu ababi, abala ukuzwa amazwi ami, abahamba ngobulukhuni benhliziyo yabo, j belandela abanye onkulunkulu ukubakhonza, lokubakhothamela, bazakuba njengalelibhanti elingasizi lutho. k <b>11</b> Ngoba njengoba ibhanti linamathela okhalweni lomuntu, ngokunjalo nginamathelise kimi indlu yonke yakoIsrayeli lendlu yonke yakoJuda, itsho iNkosi; ukuze kimi babe ngabantu, l lebizo, m , n , o lendumiso, p , q , r lenkazimulo; s kodwa kabalalelanga. Umfanekiso wezimbiza ezigcwaliswa ngewayini 
<b>12</b> Ngakho uzakhuluma kibo lelilizwi: Itsho njalo iNkosi uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Yonke imbodlela t izagcwaliswa ngewayini. u Njalo bazakuthi kuwe: v Isibili kasikwazi yini ukuthi yonke imbodlela izagcwaliswa ngewayini? <b>13</b> Ubususithi kibo: Itsho njalo iNkosi: Khangela, ngizagcwalisa bonke abakhileyo balelilizwe, lamakhosi ahlala esihlalweni sobukhosi sikaDavida, w labapristi, x labaprofethi, y labo bonke abahlali beJerusalema, ngokudakwa. z 
<b>14</b> Njalo ngizabaphahlaza omunye komunye, aa laboyise lamadodana ndawonye, itsho iNkosi; kangiyikuhawukela, kangiyikuyekela, kangiyikuba lomusa, ukuthi ngingabachithi. AbakoJuda bazathunjwa ngenxa yokungaphenduki kwabo <b>15</b> Zwanini, libeke indlebe, lingaziqhenyi, ngoba iNkosi ikhulumile. <b>16</b> Inikeni iNkosi uNkulunkulu wenu udumo, ab ingakalethi ubumnyama, ac lenyawo zenu zingakakhubeki ezintabeni zokuhwalala, lisalindela ukukhanya, ad ikwenze kube lithunzi lokufa, ae ikwenze kube ngumnyama onzima. af 
<b>17</b> Kodwa uba lingalaleli, umphefumulo wami uzakhala inyembezi endaweni zensitha ngenxa yokuziqhenya kwenu, ag lelihlo lami lizakhala inyembezi kabuhlungu, ah lehlise inyembezi, ngoba umhlambi weNkosi uthunjiwe. <b>18</b> Tshono enkosini ai lendlovukazini aj , ak uthi: Zithobeni, hlalani phansi; ngoba imiqhele yenu izawela phansi, umqhele wodumo lwenu. al <b>19</b> Imizi yeningizimu izavalwa, am njalo kakho ozayivula; uJuda uzathunjwa, yena wonke, uzathunjwa ngokupheleleyo. 
<b>20</b> Phakamisani amehlo enu, libone labo abavela enyakatho. an Ungaphi umhlambi owaphiwa wona, umhlambi wakho omuhle? <b>21</b> Uzakuthini uba esenza isijeziso phezu kwakho, ngoba wena ubafundise ukuthi babe yizinduna ao lenhloko phezu kwakho; ap inhlungu kaziyikukubamba yini njengowesifazana obelethayo? aq <b>22</b> Njalo uba usithi enhliziyweni yakho: Kungani lezizinto zenzakele kimi? ar Ngenxa yobunengi bobubi bakho amalogwe akho embuliwe, as lezithende zakho zaphathwa ngodlakela. 
<b>23</b> UmEthiyophiya angasiphendula yini isikhumba sakhe, kumbe ingwe amabala ayo? Khona lani lizakuba lakho ukwenza okuhle elejwayele ukwenza okubi. at <b>24</b> Ngakho ngizabahlakaza njengamakhoba edluliswa ngomoya wenkangala. au <b>25</b> Le yinkatho yakho, isabelo sezilinganiso zakho ezivela kimi, av itsho iNkosi, ngoba ungikhohliwe, aw wathemba emangeni. ax <b>26</b> Ngakho lami ngizakwembula amalogwe akho phezu kobuso bakho, ay , az ukuze kubonakale ihlazo lakho. ba 
<b>27</b> Ngibonile ubufebe bakho, lokukhonya kwakho, bb inkohlakalo zokuphinga kwakho, phezu kwamaqaqa egangeni, amanyala akho. bc Maye kuwe, Jerusalema! Kawuyikuhlambuluka yini? bd Kusezadlula nini? 
<b>Jeremías 14</b><br><b>1</b> Ilizwi leNkosi elafika kuJeremiya mayelana lendaba zembalela. <b>2</b> UJuda uyalila, lamasango akhe adangele; amnyama kuze kube semhlabathini, lokukhala kweJerusalema kwenyukile. <b>3</b> Njalo izikhulu zakhe zithuma abancinyane bazo emanzini; bafika emithonjeni, bangatholi manzi; babuyele inkonxa zabo zingelalutho, belenhloni, beyangekile, bembomboze ikhanda labo. <b>4</b> Ngoba umhlabathi udabukile, ngoba kungekho izulu elizweni, abalimi balenhloni, bambomboza ikhanda labo. 
<b>5</b> Yebo impala layo iyazala egangeni, ikutshiye, ngoba kungelatshani. <b>6</b> Labobabhemi beganga bayema emadundulwini, baphembele umoya a njengemigobho; b amehlo abo ayaphela, ngoba kungelatshani. <b>7</b> Lanxa izono zethu zifakaza zimelana lathi, Nkosi, yenza ngenxa yebizo lakho c; ngoba ukuhlehlela nyovane kwethu kunengi d, sonile kuwe e. <b>8</b> Wena themba likaIsrayeli, uMsindisi wakhe ngesikhathi sembandezelo, kungani uzakuba njengowezizweni elizweni f, lanjengesihambi esiphambukela eceleni ukulala ubusuku g? 
<b>9</b> Kungani uzakuba njengomuntu odidekileyo, njengeqhawe elingelakusindisa h. Kanti wena uphakathi kwethu, Nkosi, lebizo lakho libizwe phezu kwethu i; ungasitshiyi j. <b>10</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi ngalababantu: Ngokunjalo bathanda ukuzula, kabazinqandanga inyawo zabo; ngakho iNkosi kayithokozi ngabo k, khathesi izakhumbula ububi babo, izihambele izono zabo l. <b>11</b> INkosi yasisithi kimi: Ungakhulekeli lababantu m kube kuhle n. 
<b>12</b> Lapho bezila ukudla, kangiyikuzwa ukukhala kwabo o; lalapho benikela umnikelo wokutshiswa lomnikelo wokudla, kangiyikuthokoza ngabo; kodwa ngizabaqeda p ngenkemba q langendlala langomatshayabhuqe wesifo r. <b>13</b> Ngasengisithi: Hawu, Nkosi Jehova! Khangela, abaprofethi bathi kubo: Kaliyikubona inkemba, kaliyikuba lendlala s, kodwa ngizalinika ukuthula okuqinisekileyo kulindawo t. 
<b>14</b> INkosi yasisithi kimi: Abaprofethi baprofetha amanga ngebizo lami u. Kangibathumanga, njalo kangibalayanga, njalo kangikhulumanga kibo; baprofetha kini umbono wamanga v, lokuhlahlula, lokuyize w, lenkohliso yenhliziyo yabo x. <b>15</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi mayelana labaprofethi abaprofetha ebizweni lami, ngoba mina ngingabathumanga y, kanti bathi z: Inkemba lendlala kakuyikuba khona kulelilizwe: Ngenkemba langendlala labobaprofethi bazaqedwa aa. 
<b>16</b> Labantu, abaprofetha kibo, bazakuba ngabaphoselwa ezitaladeni zeJerusalema ngenxa yendlala lenkemba; njalo kungekho obangcwabayo ab, bona, omkabo, lamadodana abo, lamadodakazi abo; ngoba ngizathululela ububi babo phezu kwabo. <b>17</b> Ngakho uzakutsho lelilizwi kibo elokuthi: Amehlo ami kawehlise inyembezi ebusuku lemini ac, zingakhawuli ad, ngoba intombi emsulwa yabantu bami yephukile ngokwephuka okukhulu ae, ukutshaywa okubuhlungu kakhulu af. 
<b>18</b> Uba ngiphumela emaphandleni, khangela-ke, ababulewe ngenkemba ag. Njalo uba ngingena emzini, khangela-ke, abagulayo ngendlala ah. Yebo, bobabili umprofethi lompristi ai babhodabhoda aj elizweni kodwa bengazi ak. <b>19</b> Usumalile isibili uJuda yini? Umphefumulo wakho unengwa yiZiyoni yini al? Usitshayeleni njalo singabi lokwelatshwa am? Salindela ukuthula, kodwa kakubanga lokuhle an; lesikhathi sokwelatshwa, kodwa khangela, uvalo ao. <b>20</b> Siyazi, Nkosi, ukukhohlakala kwethu lobubi babobaba, ngoba sonile kuwe ap. 
<b>21</b> Ungasideleli, ngenxa yebizo lakho aq, ungayangisi isihlalo sobukhosi sodumo lwakho; khumbula, ungasephuli isivumelwano sakho lathi ar. <b>22</b> Kukhona yini phakathi as kokuyize kwabezizwe okunganisa izulu at? Kumbe amazulu anganika izihlambo yini au? Akusuwe yini, Nkosi, Nkulunkulu wethu? Ngakho sizalindela kuwe av; ngoba nguwe owenze zonke lezizinto aw. 
<b>Jeremías 15</b><br><b>1</b> INkosi yasisithi kimi: Loba bekumi phambi kwami a oMozisi b , c loSamuweli, d , e inhliziyo yami ibingebe kulababantu; baxotshe phambi kwami, bayekele baphume. <b>2</b> Kuzakuthi-ke uba besithi kuwe: Sizaphuma siye ngaphi? Uzabatshela-ke uthi: Itsho njalo iNkosi: Abokufa baya ekufeni, f labenkemba baya enkembeni, labendlala baya endlaleni, labokuthunjwa baya ekuthunjweni. 
<b>3</b> Ngoba ngizabamisela izinhlobo ezine, g itsho iNkosi: Inkemba yokubulala, lezinja zokudonsa, lenyoni zamazulu h lezinyamazana zomhlaba zokudla lezokubhubhisa. <b>4</b> Ngizabanikela babe yinto eyesabekayo kuyo yonke imibuso yomhlaba, ngenxa kaManase indodana kaHezekhiya, inkosi yakoJuda, i ngalokho akwenza eJerusalema. <b>5</b> Ngoba ngubani ozakuhawukela, wena Jerusalema? Njalo ngubani ozakulilela? j Njalo ngubani ozaphambukela eceleni ukubuza ngokuthula kwakho? 
<b>6</b> Wena ungidelile, k itsho iNkosi, wabuyela emuva; l ngakho ngizakwelula isandla sami ngimelene lawe, ngikuchithe; ngikhathele yikuhawukela. m <b>7</b> Njalo ngizabela n ngoluthi lokwela emasangweni elizwe; o ngizabemuka abantwana, p ngibhubhise abantu bami; kabaphendukanga endleleni zabo. q <b>8</b> Abafelokazi babo bandile kimi kuletshebetshebe lolwandle. r Ngilethe phezu kwabo, phezu kukanina, ijaha, umchithi emini enkulu; s ngiwisele phezu kwabo uvalo lezesabiso t bengananzelele. 
<b>9</b> Owazala abayisikhombisa usekhathele; u usephefumule okokucina; v ilanga lakhe litshone kusesemini; w waba lenhloni, wayangeka; lensali yabo ngizayinikela enkembeni phambi kwezitha zabo, itsho iNkosi. Ukusola kukaJeremiya <b>10</b> Maye mina, x mama, ukuthi ungizele, umuntu wokuphikisana lomuntu wokuxabana emhlabeni wonke! Kangebolekisanga ngenzuzo, y futhi kabangebolekisanga ngenzuzo, kanti bayangithuka, ngulowo lalowo wabo. 
<b>11</b> INkosi yathi: Isibili kuzakuba kuhle ngensali yakho; z isibili ngizakuncengela esitheni ngesikhathi sokubi langesikhathi sosizi. aa <b>12</b> Insimbi ingayephula yini insimbi evela enyakatho ab lethusi? ac <b>13</b> Inotho yakho lokuligugu kwakho ngizakunikela kube yimpango, ad kungelantengo, ae langenxa yezono zakho zonke, ngitsho emingceleni yakho yonke. af <b>14</b> Ngizakwenza ukuthi udlule kanye lezitha zakho uye elizweni ongalaziyo; ag ngoba umlilo ubasiwe olakeni lwami; ah uzabhebha phezu kwenu. 
<b>15</b> Wena uyazi, Nkosi; ai ngikhumbula, aj ungihambele, ungiphindiselele kwabangizingelayo; ak , al ungangisusi ekubekezeleni kwakho; yazi ukuthi am ngenxa yakho ngithwele ihlazo. an <b>16</b> Amazwi akho atholakala njalo ngawadla; ao lelizwi lakho kimi laba yinjabulo njalo laba yintokozo yenhliziyo yami; ap ngoba ibizo lakho labizwa phezu kwami, wena Nkosi, Nkulunkulu wamabandla. <b>17</b> Kangihlalanga aq emhlanganweni wabahleka usulu, ar kumbe ngijabule; ngenxa yesandla sakho ngahlala ngedwa; as ngoba ungigcwalise ngentukuthelo. 
<b>18</b> Kungani ubuhlungu bami bube khona njalonjalo, lenxeba lami lingelapheki, at lisala ukuphola? au Isibili uzakuba njengomqambimanga av kimi yini, njengamanzi angathembekanga? aw <b>19</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi: Uba ubuya, ax ngizakubuyisa, ume phambi kwami; ay , az njalo uba ukhupha okuligugu kokungasizi lutho, uzakuba njengomlomo wami. Bona kababuyele kuwe, kodwa wena ungabuyeli kubo. 
<b>20</b> Ngoba ngizakwenza ube ngumduli wethusi ovikelweyo kulababantu; ba njalo bazakulwa bemelene lawe, kodwa kabayikukwehlula, bb ngoba mina ngilawe ukukusindisa lokukophula, bc itsho iNkosi. <b>21</b> Ngizakophula esandleni sababi, bd ngikuhlenge esandleni sabalesihluku. be 
<b>Jeremías 16</b><br><b>1</b> Ilizwi leNkosi laselifika kimi lisithi: <b>2</b> Ungazithatheli umfazi, njalo ungabi lamadodana kumbe amadodakazi kulindawo. <b>3</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi mayelana lamadodana lamayelana lamadodakazi azalelwe kulindawo, lamayelana labonina abawazalayo lamayelana laboyise abawazalele kulelilizwe: 
<b>4</b> Bazakufa izimfa zemikhuhlane; a kabayikulilelwa, b futhi kabayikungcwatshwa; c bazakuba ngumquba phezu kobuso bomhlaba; d njalo bazaqedwa e yinkemba f layindlala; g lezidumbu zabo zizakuba yikudla kwezinyoni zamazulu h lokwenyamazana zomhlaba. <b>5</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi: Ungangeni endlini yesililo, i ungayikulila, ungabakhaleli; ngoba ngikususile ukuthula kwami kulababantu, itsho iNkosi, uthandolomusa lezihawu. 
<b>6</b> Njalo abakhulu labancinyane bazakufa kulelilizwe; kabayikungcwatshwa, j kabayikubalilela, k kabayikuzisika, l kabayikuziphuca m ngenxa yabo. <b>7</b> Futhi kabayikubahlephulela isinkwa esililweni, ukubaduduza ngenxa yofileyo; futhi kakho ozabanathisa inkezo yezinduduzo ngenxa kayise langenxa kanina. <b>8</b> Njalo ungangeni endlini yedili, ukuhlala labo, ukudla lokunatha. 
<b>9</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Khangelani, ngizakwenza ukuthi kuphele kule indawo, emehlweni enu, n lensukwini zenu, ilizwi lentokozo, lelizwi lenjabulo, ilizwi lomyeni, lelizwi likamakoti. <b>10</b> Njalo kuzakuthi lapho utshela lababantu wonke lamazwi, babesebesithi kuwe: INkosi ikhulumelani konke lokhu okubi okukhulu imelene lathi, o njalo buyini ububi bethu, njalo yisiphi isono sethu esisonileyo simelene leNkosi uNkulunkulu wethu? 
<b>11</b> Khona uzakuthi kubo: p Ngenxa yokuthi oyihlo bangidelile, itsho iNkosi, balandela abanye onkulunkulu, q babasebenzela, babakhonza, bangidela mina, kabagcinanga umlayo wami; <b>12</b> lina-ke lenze okubi kulaboyihlo; r ngoba khangelani, lilandela, ngulowo lalowo ubulukhuni benhliziyo yakhe embi, s ukuthi bangangilaleli. <b>13</b> Ngakho ngizaliphosela ngaphandle kwalelilizwe, t , u liye elizweni elingalaziyo, v lina laboyihlo; lapho-ke lizasebenzela abanye onkulunkulu emini lebusuku, ngoba kangiyikulinika umusa. 
<b>14</b> Ngakho, khangela, w insuku ziyeza, x itsho iNkosi, lapho kungasayikuthiwa: Kuphila kweNkosi, y eyakhuphula abantwana bakoIsrayeli elizweni leGibhithe; <b>15</b> kodwa: Kuphila kweNkosi, z eyakhuphula abantwana bakoIsrayeli elizweni lenyakatho aa lakuwo wonke amazwe eyayibaxotshele kiwo; ngoba ngizababuyisa futhi elizweni lakibo engalinika oyise. ab 
<b>16</b> Khangela, ngizathumela kubagoli benhlanzi ac abanengi, itsho iNkosi, babesebebagola; lemva kwalokho ngizathumela kubazingeli abanengi, babazingele bebasusa kuyo yonke intaba, lakulo lonke uqaqa, lasezingoxweni zamadwala. ad <b>17</b> Ngoba amehlo ami aphezu kwazo zonke izindlela zabo; ae kazifihlakalanga ebusweni bami, lobubi babo kabucatshanga phambi kwamehlo ami. af 
<b>18</b> Kuqala-ke ngizaphindisela isiphambeko sabo lesono sabo ag ngokuphindiweyo, ah ngoba bengcolise ilizwe lami, ai bagcwalisa ilifa lami ngezidumbu zamanyala abo lezinengiso zabo. <b>19</b> Nkosi, wena mandla ami, lenqaba yami, aj lesiphephelo sami ngosuku lwembandezelo, ak abezizwe bazafika kuwe bevela emikhawulweni yomhlaba, bathi: Isibili obaba badlile ilifa lamanga, okuyize, al lokungekho inzuzo kukho. am , an <b>20</b> Umuntu angazenzela onkulunkulu yini, kanti kabayisibo onkulunkulu? ao 
<b>21</b> Ngakho, khangela, ngizabenza bazi okwalesisikhathi, ap ngizabenza bazi isandla sami lobuqhawe bami; bazakwazi-ke ukuthi ibizo lami linguJehova. aq 
<b>Jeremías 17</b><br><b>1</b> Isono sakoJuda sibhaliwe ngepheni yensimbi, a ngomcijo wendayimana; b sibhalwe ngokugujwa esibhebheni senhliziyo yabo, c lezimpondweni d zamalathi enu; e , f <b>2</b> abantwana babo besakhumbula amalathi abo g lezixuku zabo h ngasezihlahleni eziluhlaza phezu kwamaqaqa aphakemeyo. i <b>3</b> Wena ntaba yami esegangeni, j ngizanikela imfuyo yakho lakho konke okuligugu kwakho empangweni, k lezindawo zakho eziphakemeyo, ngenxa yesono kuyo yonke imingcele yakho. 
<b>4</b> Lawe, ngitsho ngokwakho, uzayekela ilifa lakho engakunika lona; l njalo ngizakwenza usebenzele izitha zakho m elizweni ongalaziyo; n , o ngoba usuphembe umlilo ekuthukutheleni kwami; uzavutha kuze kube nininini. <b>5</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Uqalekisiwe umuntu othembela emuntwini, p esenza inyama ibe yingalo yakhe, q lonhliziyo yakhe iphambuka eNkosini. <b>6</b> Ngoba uzakuba njengesihlahlanyana enkangala, r , s esingayikubona nxa okuhle kusiza; t kodwa uzahlala ezindaweni ezomileyo enkangala, elizweni letshwayi, u elingahlalwayo. v 
<b>7</b> Ubusisiwe umuntu othembela eNkosini, w lothemba lakhe liyiNkosi. x <b>8</b> Ngoba uzakuba njengesihlahla esihlanyelwe ngasemanzini, y esinabisela impande zaso ngasemfuleni, esingesabi lapho kufika ukutshisa, kodwa ihlamvu laso lizakuba luhlaza; z kasikhathazeki emnyakeni wokubalela, kasiyekeli ukuthela isithelo. <b>9</b> Inhliziyo aa ilenkohliso okwedlula konke, ab futhi imbi kakhulu; ac ngubani ongayazi? 
<b>10</b> Mina Nkosi ngihlola inhliziyo, ad ngilinge izinso, ae ngitsho ukupha wonke umuntu njengokwezindlela zakhe, af njengokwesithelo sezenzo zakhe. <b>11</b> Njengethendele elifukamela amaqanda lingawachamuseli, ag unjalo lowo owenza inotho kodwa hatshi ngokulunga; uzakutshiya ah phakathi kwensuku zakhe, ai lekucineni kwakhe abe yisithutha. aj <b>12</b> Isihlalo sobukhosi esilenkazimulo, esiphakanyisiweyo kwasekuqaleni, siyindawo yendlu yethu engcwele. 
<b>13</b> Nkosi, themba likaIsrayeli, ak bonke abakudelayo bazayangeka; al labasuka kimi bazabhalwa emhlabathini, am ngoba bayidelile iNkosi, an , ao umthombo wamanzi aphilayo. ap <b>14</b> Ngisilisa, Nkosi, njalo ngizasila; aq ngisindisa, njalo ngizasindiswa; ngoba uyindumiso yami. ar <b>15</b> Khangela, bathi kimi: Lingaphi ilizwi leNkosi? as Kalize khathesi! at 
<b>16</b> Kodwa mina kangiphangisanga au , av ukuthi ngingabi ngumelusi aw emva kwakho; futhi kangilufisanga usuku olwesabekayo; ax wena uyakwazi; okwaphuma emlonyeni wami ay kwakuphambi kobuso bakho. az <b>17</b> Ungabi luvalo kimi; wena uyisiphephelo sami osukwini lobubi. ba <b>18</b> Kabayangeke labo abangizingelayo, bb kodwa mina kangingayangeki, bc kabesabe bona, kodwa mina kangingesabi; behlisele usuku lobubi, ubephule bd ngokwephula okuphindiweyo. be Isabatha limele ukugcinwa 
<b>19</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi kimi: Hamba uyekuma esangweni labantwana babantu, angena ngalo amakhosi akoJuda, bf aphuma ngalo, lakuwo wonke amasango eJerusalema, <b>20</b> ubususithi kubo: Zwanini ilizwi leNkosi, bg lina makhosi akoJuda, loJuda wonke, labo bonke abahlali beJerusalema, bh abangena ngala amasango! <b>21</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Ziqapheleni, lingathwali mthwalo ngosuku lwesabatha, bi kumbe liwungenise ngamasango eJerusalema. 
<b>22</b> Futhi lingakhuphi umthwalo ezindlini zenu ngosuku lwesabatha, lingenzi loba yiwuphi umsebenzi; bj kodwa lungcweliseni usuku lwesabatha, bk njengalokhu ngalaya oyihlo. bl <b>23</b> Kodwa kabalalelanga, kababekanga indlebe yabo, bm kodwa benza intamo yabo yaba lukhuni ukuze bangezwa, bn lokuze bangemukeli ukulaywa. bo <b>24</b> Kuzakuthi-ke uba lina lingilalela lokungilalela, itsho iNkosi, ukuthi lingangenisi mthwalo ngamasango alumuzi ngosuku lwesabatha, bp kodwa lilungcwelise usuku lwesabatha, bq ukuthi lingenzi loba yiwuphi umsebenzi ngalo; 
<b>25</b> khona kuzangena ngamasango alumuzi amakhosi leziphathamandla abahlala esihlalweni sobukhosi sikaDavida, br begade izinqola lamabhiza, bs wona leziphathamandla zawo, abantu bakoJuda, labahlali beJerusalema; lalumuzi uzahlalwa kuze kube nininini.

<b>Jeremías 20</b><br><b>1</b> Kwathi uPashuri a umpristi, indodana kaImeri, umpristi, b , c owayengumbonisi oyinduna endlini yeNkosi, d wamuzwa uJeremiya eprofetha lezizinto, <b>2</b> uPashuri wasemtshaya uJeremiya umprofethi, wamfaka esitokisini e esisesangweni lakoBhenjamini elingaphezulu, f elalingasendlini yeNkosi.

<b>14</b> othi: Ngizazakhela indlu enkulu lamakamelo aphezulu abanzi; ak azisikele amawindi, yembeswe ngemisedari, al iconjwe ngokubomvu. am <b>15</b> Uzabusa yini, ngoba utshisekela ulaka ugqoke imisedari? an Uyihlo kadlanga yini wanatha, wenza isahlulelo lokulunga, ao kwasekusiba kuhle kuye? ap <b>16</b> Wehlulela udaba lomyanga loswelayo aq; khona kwaba kuhle ar. <b>17</b> Kodwa amehlo akho lenhliziyo yakho kakukho kokunye at ngaphandle kwemhawini wakho, au lekuchitheni igazi elingelacala, av lencindezelweni, aw , ax lekwenzeni udlakela. <b>18</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi mayelana loJehoyakhimi indodana kaJosiya, inkosi yakoJuda: ay Kabayikumkhalela az besithi: Hawu mfowethu! kumbe: Hawu dadewethu! ba Kabayikumkhalela besithi: Hawu nkosi! loba: Hawu, bukhosi bakhe! bb <b>19</b> Uzangcwatshwa ngokungcwatshwa kukababhemi, ehudulwa alahlwe ngaphandle kwamasango eJerusalema. bc 
<b>20</b> Yenyukela eLebhanoni, umemeze, uphakamise ilizwi lakho eBashani, umemeze useAbarimi, bd ngoba zonke izithandwa zakho zibhujisiwe. be <b>21</b> Ngakhuluma lawe ekonwabeni kwakho, kodwa wathi: Kangiyikulalela. Lokhu bekuyindlela yakho kusukela ebutsheni bakho, bf ukuthi ungalilaleli ilizwi lami. bg <b>22</b> Umoya uzakudla bonke abelusi bakho, bh lezithandwa zakho zizakuya ekuthunjweni; bi isibili khona uzakuba lenhloni, uyangeke, ngenxa yobubi bakho bonke. 
<b>23</b> Wena ohlala eLebhanoni, bj owakhe isidleke phakathi kwemisedari, bk uzahawukelwa kangakanani ekuzeni kwenhlungu kuwe, ubuhlungu obunjengobowesifazana ohelelwayo. bl <b>24</b> Kuphila kwami, bm itsho iNkosi, loba uKoniya indodana kaJehoyakhimi, inkosi yakoJuda, bn ebeyindandatho elophawu esandleni sami sokunene, bo kanti bengizakuhluthula kuso, <b>25</b> ngikunikele esandleni sabadinga impilo yakho, bp lesandleni salabo owesaba ubuso babo, bq ngitsho esandleni sikaNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni, lesandleni samaKhaladiya, 
<b>26</b> ngikuphosele phandle br , bs kanye lonyoko owakubelethayo, bt liye kwelinye ilizwe, elingazalelwanga kulo; njalo lizafela khona. <b>27</b> Kodwa elizweni abaphakamisa umphefumulo wabo ukubuyela kulo, kabayikubuyela kulo. bu <b>28</b> Lumuntu uKoniya uyisithombe esidelelekayo lesiphahlazekileyo yini? bv Uyisitsha okungelantokozo kuso yini? bw Baphoselwani phandle, bx yena lenzalo yakhe, yebo, bephoselwa elizweni abangalaziyo? by , bz <b>29</b> Lizwe, lizwe, lizwe, zwana ilizwi leNkosi. ca 
<b>30</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Bhalani lumuntu ukuthi kalabantwana, cb umuntu ongayikuphumelela ensukwini zakhe; ngoba kakulamuntu enzalweni yakhe ozaphumelela, ohlezi esihlalweni sobukhosi sikaDavida, cc esabusa koJuda. 
<b>Jeremías 23</b><br><b>1</b> Maye kubelusi abachitha behlakaza izimvu zedlelo lami! a itsho iNkosi. <b>2</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli, imelene labelusi abelusa abantu bami: b Lizihlakazile izimvu zami, lazixotsha, kalizethekelelanga. Khangelani, ngizaphindisela phezu kwenu c ububi bezenzo zenu, d itsho iNkosi. <b>3</b> Mina-ke ngizabutha insali yezimvu zami e iphume kuwo wonke amazwe lapho engizixotshele khona, f ngizibuyise futhi esibayeni sazo; njalo zizazala, zande. g 
<b>4</b> Ngizamisa phezu kwazo abelusi abazazelusa; h njalo kazisayikwesaba, kaziyikutshaywa luvalo, kaziyikulahleka, itsho iNkosi. <b>5</b> Khangela, insuku ziyeza, i , j itsho iNkosi, lapho engizavusela uDavida iHlumela elilungileyo; k njalo iNkosi izabusa l iphumelele, m izakwenza isahlulelo lokulunga emhlabeni. n <b>6</b> Ensukwini zalo uJuda uzasindiswa, loIsrayeli ahlale o evikelekile. p Njalo leli libizo layo ezabizwa ngalo: INkosi q Ukulunga Kwethu. r 
<b>7</b> Ngakho khangela, s insuku ziyeza, t itsho iNkosi, lapho abangasayikuthi: Kuphila kweNkosi, eyabakhuphula abantwana bakoIsrayeli elizweni leGibhithe. <b>8</b> Kodwa: Kuphila kweNkosi eyakhuphula eyaletha inzalo yendlu kaIsrayeli isuka elizweni lenyakatho lakuwo wonke amazwe engangiyixotshele khona. u Njalo bazahlala elizweni lakibo. Abapristi bamanga 
<b>9</b> Mayelana labaprofethi: Inhliziyo yami yephukile ngaphakathi kwami; v amathambo ami wonke ayathuthumela; w nginjengesidakwa, lanjengomuntu owehlulwe liwayini, ngenxa yeNkosi, langenxa yamazwi obungcwele bayo. x <b>10</b> Ngoba ilizwe ligcwele izifebe; y ngoba ngenxa yesiqalekiso z ilizwe liyalila; aa amadlelo enkangala ab omile, ac lokuhamba kwabo kubi, lamandla abo kawaqondanga. <b>11</b> Ngoba lomprofethi lompristi kabamesabi uNkulunkulu; ad ngitsho endlini yami ngitholile ububi babo, ae itsho iNkosi. 
<b>12</b> Ngakho indlela yabo izakuba kibo njengezindlela ezitshelelayo af emnyameni; ag bafuqelwa phansi, bawele kuyo; ngoba ngizabehlisela okubi ngomnyaka wokuhanjelwa kwabo, ah itsho iNkosi. <b>13</b> Ngibonile-ke ubuwula kubaprofethi ai beSamariya; aj baprofetha ngoBhali, ak baduhisa abantu al bami uIsrayeli. 
<b>14</b> Kodwa kubaprofethi beJerusalema ngibonile into eyesabekayo; am bayafeba, an bahambe emangeni, ao baqinisa izandla zabenzi bobubi ap ukuze bangaphenduki ngulowo ebubini bakhe; bonke sebenjengeSodoma aq kimi, labakhileyo bayo njengeGomora. ar <b>15</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla mayelana labaprofethi: as Khangela, ngizabadlisa at umhlonyane, au ngibanathise av amanzi enyongo; aw ngoba kusukela kubaprofethi beJerusalema ukungamesabi uNkulunkulu kuphumele ilizwe lonke. 
<b>16</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla: Lingalaleli amazwi abaprofethi abaprofetha kini; balenza libe yize; bakhuluma umbono wenhliziyo yabo, ax hatshi ovela emlonyeni weNkosi. <b>17</b> Baqhubeka besithi kwabangidelelayo: INkosi ithe: Lizakuba lokuthula; ay njalo bathi kuye wonke ohamba ngenkani yenhliziyo yakhe: Ububi kabuyikukwehlela. az <b>18</b> Ngoba ngubani omi kucebo leNkosi, ba wabona wezwa ilizwi layo? Ngubani oqaphelise ilizwi lakhe, walizwa? 
<b>19</b> Khangela, isivunguzane seNkosi, ulaka luphumile, bb ngitsho isivunguzane esibuhlungu; bc sizakwehlela ngamandla phezu kwekhanda labakhohlakeleyo. bd <b>20</b> Ulaka lweNkosi kaluyikuphenduka, be ize yenze, njalo ize iqinise iminakano yenhliziyo yayo. Ekucineni kwezinsuku lizakuqedisisa lokukuqedisisa. bf , bg <b>21</b> Kangibathumanga lababaprofethi, bh kanti bona bagijima; kangikhulumanga labo, kanti bona baprofetha.

Kangigcwalisi yini amazulu lomhlaba? bo itsho iNkosi. <b>25</b> Ngizwile ukuthi abaprofethi batheni, abaprofetha amanga ebizweni lami bp besithi: Ngiphuphile, ngiphuphile! bq 
<b>26</b> Koze kube nini kusenhliziyweni yabaprofethi abaprofetha amanga? br Yebo, bangabaprofethi benkohliso yenhliziyo yabo; bs <b>27</b> abacabanga ukwenza abantu bami bakhohlwe ibizo lami ngamaphupho abo abawalandisa ngulowo umakhelwane wakhe, bt njengaboyise bakhohlwa ibizo lami ngenxa kaBhali. bu <b>28</b> Umprofethi olephupho, kalilandise iphupho; bv lolelizwi lami, kalikhulume ilizwi lami ngeqiniso. <b>34</b> Mayelana lomprofethi, lompristi, labantu, ch abazakuthi: Umthwalo weNkosi; ci ngizamjezisa lalowomuntu lendlu yakhe. <b>35</b> Lizakhuluma ngalindlela, ngulowo kumakhelwane wakhe, langulowo kumfowabo: INkosi iphendule yathini? cj Lokuthi: INkosi ikhulumeni? <b>36</b> Kodwa umthwalo weNkosi kalisayikukhuluma ngawo.
