<b>37</b> Uzakutsho ngokunjalo kumprofethi: INkosi ikuphenduleni? cl njalo: INkosi ikhulumeni? <b>38</b> Kodwa uba lisithi: Umthwalo weNkosi; ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi: Ngoba likhuluma lamazwi okuthi: Umthwalo weNkosi; njalo ngithumele kini ngisithi: Lingabokuthi: Umthwalo weNkosi; <b>39</b> ngakho khangelani, mina ngizalikhohlwa ngokupheleleyo, cm ngitshiye lina lomuzi engalinika wona laboyihlo, cn lisuke ebusweni bami. <b>40</b> Njalo ngizabeka phezu kwenu ihlazo elilaphakade lokuyangeka okulaphakade, co okungayikukhohlakala. 
<b>Jeremías 24</b><br><b>1</b> INkosi yangenza ngibone, a khangela-ke, izitsha ezimbili zemikhiwa zibekwe phambi kwethempeli leNkosi, emva kokuthi uNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni esethumbe b uJekoniya indodana kaJehoyakhimi, inkosi yakoJuda, c leziphathamandla zakoJuda, d lababazi, e , f labakhandi, g wabasusa eJerusalema, wabasa eBhabhiloni. <b>2</b> Esinye isitsha sasilemikhiwa emihle kakhulu njengemikhiwa evuthwe kuqala; h kodwa esinye isitsha sasilemikhiwa emibi kakhulu, i eyayingeke idliwe ngenxa yobubi bayo. 
<b>3</b> INkosi yasisithi kimi: Ubonani, Jeremiya? Ngasengisithi: Imikhiwa, imikhiwa emihle, emihle kakhulu; lemibi, emibi kakhulu, engeke idliwe ngenxa yobubi bayo. <b>4</b> Lafika kimi futhi ilizwi leNkosi lisithi: <b>5</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Njengalimikhiwa emihle, ngokunjalo ngizananzelela abathunjiweyo bakoJuda, engibaxotshe ngabasusa kulindawo ngabasa elizweni lamaKhaladiya, j kube kuhle. k 
<b>6</b> Ngoba ngizabeka ilihlo lami phezu kwabo l ngokuhle, m ngibabuyisele kulelilizwe; n ngibakhe, ngingabadilizi; o , p njalo ngizabahlanyela, ngingabasiphuni. q , r <b>7</b> Njalo ngizabanika inhliziyo yokuthi bangazi mina, s ukuthi ngiyiNkosi; njalo bazakuba ngabantu bami, mina ngibe nguNkulunkulu wabo; t ngoba bazabuyela kimi ngenhliziyo yabo yonke. u 
<b>8</b> Kodwa njengemikhiwa emibi engeke idliwe ngenxa yobubi bayo, v isibili itsho njalo iNkosi: Ngokunjalo ngizanika uZedekhiya, inkosi yakoJuda, w leziphathamandla zakhe, lensali yeJerusalema, eseleyo kulelilizwe, lalabo abahlala elizweni leGibhithe, x <b>9</b> ngibanikele babe yinto eyesabekayo, babe yibubi, kuyo yonke imibuso yomhlaba, y babe lihlazo, z lesaga, aa inhlekisa, ab lesiqalekiso, ac kuzo zonke indawo engizabaxotshela khona. 
<b>10</b> Njalo ngizathumela ad phakathi kwabo inkemba, ae indlala, lomatshayabhuqe wesifo, af baze baqedwe basuke elizweni engalinika bona laboyise. 
<b>Jeremías 25</b><br><b>1</b> Ilizwi elafika kuJeremiya mayelana labantu bonke bakoJuda, ngomnyaka wesine kaJehoyakhimi indodana kaJosiya, inkosi yakoJuda a (kwakungumnyaka wokuqala kaNebhukadirezari, inkosi yeBhabhiloni), <b>2</b> alikhulumayo uJeremiya umprofethi kubo bonke abantu bakoJuda, lakubo bonke abahlali beJerusalema, esithi: 
<b>3</b> Kusukela emnyakeni wetshumi lantathu kaJosiya, indodana kaAmoni, inkosi yakoJuda, b kuze kube lamuhla, sekuyiminyaka engamatshumi amabili lantathu, ilizwi leNkosi lifikile kimi, njalo ngilikhulumile kini, ngivuka ngovivi ngikhuluma; c kodwa kalilalelanga. d <b>4</b> Futhi iNkosi ithumile kini zonke izinceku zayo, abaprofethi, ivuka ngovivi ibathuma; e kodwa kalilalelanga, f kalibekanga indlebe yenu ukuthi lizwe; g 
<b>5</b> besithi: Phendukani khathesi ngulowo lalowo h endleleni yakhe embi, i lebubini bezenzo zenu, j lihlale elizweni iNkosi elinike lina laboyihlo k kusukela nininini kuze kube nininini; l <b>6</b> njalo lingalandeli abanye onkulunkulu ukubasebenzela lokubakhonza, m lingangithukuthelisi n ngomsebenzi wezandla zenu; o khona ngingayikulenzela okubi. <b>7</b> Kodwa kalingilalelanga, itsho iNkosi, ukuze lingithukuthelise p ngomsebenzi wezandla zenu, kuze kube kubi kini. q 
<b>8</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla: Ngenxa yokuthi lingawalalelanga amazwi ami, <b>9</b> khangela, ngizathuma, ngilande zonke izinsapho zenyakatho, r itsho iNkosi, lakuNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni inceku yami, ngibalethe bamelane lalelilizwe s bamelane labakhileyo balo, bamelane lazo zonke lezizizwe inhlangothi zonke, t ngibatshabalalise, ngibenze babe yisesabiso, u babe ngokuncifelwayo, v babe zincithakalo ezilaphakade. 
<b>10</b> Futhi ngizaphelisa ngisuse kubo ilizwi lentokozo lelizwi lenjabulo, ilizwi lomyeni lelizwi likamakoti, w umsindo wamatshe okuchola, x , y lokukhanya kwesibane. z <b>11</b> Lelizwe lonke leli lizakuba lunxiwa aa libe yisesabiso; ab lalezizizwe zizasebenzela inkosi yeBhabhiloni ac iminyaka engamatshumi ayisikhombisa. ad <b>12</b> Kuzakuthi-ke iminyaka engamatshumi ayisikhombisa isiphelele ae ngijezise inkosi yeBhabhiloni lalesosizwe, itsho iNkosi, ngobubi babo, lelizwe lamaKhaladiya, ngilenze libe ngamanxiwa laphakade. af 
<b>13</b> Ngehlisele phezu kwalelolizwe wonke amazwi ami engawakhuluma ngimelene lalo, konke okubhaliweyo kulolugwalo, ag uJeremiya akuprofethayo ngazo zonke izizwe. <b>14</b> Ngoba izizwe ezinengi ah , ai lamakhosi amakhulu aj , ak zizabasebenzisa bona uqobo; al , am njalo ngizabaphindisela njengokwenza kwabo an lanjengokomsebenzi wezandla zabo. Inkezo yewayini egcwele ulaka lweNkosi <b>15</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli kimi: Thatha inkezo yewayini yalolulaka esandleni sami, ao wenze zonke izizwe engikuthuma kuzo ziyinathe. 
<b>16</b> Njalo zizanatha, ap zidiyazele, aq zihlanye, ar , as ngenxa yenkemba engizayithuma phakathi kwazo. at <b>17</b> Ngasengithatha inkezo esandleni seNkosi, nganathisa zonke izizwe au iNkosi eyayingithume kizo, <b>18</b> iJerusalema, lemizi yakoJuda, lamakhosi ayo, leziphathamandla zayo, ukubenza babe yincithakalo, av babe yisesabiso, aw babe yinto yokuncifelwa, ax babe yisiqalekiso, ay njengalamuhla; <b>19</b> uFaro inkosi yeGibhithe, az lezinceku zakhe, leziphathamandla zakhe, labantu bakhe bonke; 
<b>20</b> labo bonke abaxubeneyo, ba lawo wonke amakhosi elizwe bb leUzi, bc lawo wonke amakhosi elizwe lamaFilisti, bd leAshikeloni, be , bf leGaza, bg , bh leEkhironi, bi lensali yeAshidodi; bj <b>21</b> iEdoma, bk , bl loMowabi, bm , bn labantwana bakoAmoni; bo , bp <b>22</b> lawo wonke amakhosi eTire, bq , br lawo wonke amakhosi eSidoni, bs , bt lamakhosi ezihlenge ezingaphetsheya kolwandle; bu <b>23</b> iDedani, bv leTema, bw leBuzi, bx labo bonke abaqunywe ezingonsini; 
<b>24</b> lamakhosi wonke by eArabhiya, bz lamakhosi wonke abantu abaxubeneyo ca abahlala enkangala; cb <b>25</b> lamakhosi wonke eZimri, cc lamakhosi wonke eElamu, cd lamakhosi wonke eMede; ce <b>26</b> lawo wonke amakhosi enyakatho, cf aseduze lakhatshana, omunye lomunye, layo yonke imibuso yomhlaba, esebusweni bomhlaba; lenkosi yeSheshaki izanatha emva kwabo. cg <b>27</b> Ubususithi kibo: Itsho njalo INkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Nathani ch , ci lidakwe, cj lihlanze, ck liwe, lingasukumi, ngenxa yenkemba engizayithuma phakathi kwenu. cl 
<b>28</b> Kuzakuthi-ke lapho besala ukuthatha inkezo esandleni sakho ukuthi banathe, cm uthi kibo: Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla: Lizanatha isibili. cn <b>29</b> Ngoba khangela, ngiyaqala ukwenza okubi emzini obizwa ngebizo lami, co lina-ke belizakuba ngabangelacala isibili yini? cp Kaliyikuba ngabangelacala; cq ngoba ngizabizela inkemba phezu kwabo bonke abakhileyo bomhlaba, cr itsho iNkosi yamabandla. 
<b>30</b> Wena-ke profetha wonke lamazwi umelene labo, uthi kubo: INkosi izabhonga iphezulu, cs iphumise ilizwi layo ct isendaweni yayo engcwele; cu izabhonga kakhulu phezu kwendawo yayo yokuhlala, cv imemeze cw iphendule njengabanyathela izithelo zevini, cx imelene labo bonke abakhileyo bomhlaba. <b>31</b> Umsindo uzafika emkhawulweni womhlaba; cy ngoba iNkosi ilempikisano lezizwe, cz izakwehlulela yonke inyama; da abakhohlakeleyo izabanikela enkembeni, itsho iNkosi. 
<b>32</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla: Khangela, ububi buzaphuma esizweni busiya esizweni, lesivunguzane esikhulu db sizavuswa sisuka ezinhlangothini zomhlaba. dc <b>33</b> Langalolosuku ababuleweyo beNkosi bazasukela komunye umkhawulo womhlaba baze bafike komunye umkhawulo; dd kabayikulilelwa, de kabayikubuthwa, df kabayikungcwatshwa; dg , dh bazakuba ngumquba phezu kobuso bomhlaba. di 
<b>34</b> Qhinqani isililo, dj belusi, dk likhale, lizibhuqe emlotheni, dl zikhulu zomhlambi; ngoba izinsuku zokuhlatshwa lezokuhlakazeka kwenu zigcwalisekile, njalo lizakuwa njengesitsha esiloyisekayo. <b>35</b> Lokubaleka kuzaphela kubelusi, dm lokuphunyuka kuzikhulu zomhlambi. <b>36</b> Kuzakuba lelizwi lokukhala kwabelusi dn lokuqhinqa isililo kwezikhulu zomhlambi; ngoba iNkosi izachitha idlelo labo. <b>37</b> Lezibaya ezilokuthula do zizachithwa dp ngenxa yokuvutha kolaka lweNkosi. dq 
<b>38</b> Itshiyile ubhalu lwayo dr njengesilwane esitsha, ds ngoba ilizwe labo seliyincithakalo ngenxa yokuvutha komcindezeli, dt langenxa yokuvutha kolaka lwayo. du 
<b>Jeremías 26</b><br><b>1</b> Ekuqaleni kokubusa kukaJehoyakhimi indodana kaJosiya inkosi yakoJuda kwafika lelilizwi livela eNkosini a lisithi: <b>2</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Mana egumeni lendlu yeNkosi, b ukhulume kuyo yonke imizi yakoJuda, efikayo ukuzakhonza endlini yeNkosi, wonke amazwi engikulaya ukuthi uwakhulume kubo; c unganciphisi lalizwi. d <b>3</b> Mhlawumbe bazalalela, e baphenduke ngulowo lalowo endleleni yakhe embi, ukuze ngizisole f ngokubi ebengicabanga ukukwenza kubo g ngenxa yobubi bezenzo zabo. h 
<b>4</b> Uzakuthi-ke kibo: Itsho njalo INkosi: Uba lingayikungilalela, i ukuthi lihambe ngomlayo wami engiwubeka phambi kwenu, <b>5</b> ukuthi lilalele amazwi enceku zami abaprofethi j engibathume kini, k yebo ngivuka ngovivi ngibathuma, kodwa kalilalelanga, l <b>6</b> ngizakwenza-ke lindlu ibe njengeShilo, m ngenze lumuzi ube yisiqalekiso kuzo zonke izizwe zomhlaba. n , o <b>7</b> Ngakho abapristi labaprofethi labo bonke abantu bamuzwa uJeremiya ekhuluma lamazwi endlini yeNkosi. p 
<b>8</b> Kwathi uJeremiya eseqedile ukukhuluma konke iNkosi eyayimlaye ukuthi akukhulume ebantwini bonke, abapristi labaprofethi labo bonke abantu bambamba besithi: Uzakufa lokufa! q <b>9</b> Kungani uprofetha ngebizo leNkosi usithi: Lindlu izakuba njengeShilo, r lomuzi lo uzakuba lunxiwa, ungabi lamhlali? s Bonke abantu basebembuthanela uJeremiya endlini yeNkosi. <b>10</b> Kwathi iziphathamandla zakoJuda sezizwile lezizinto, zenyuka zivela endlini yenkosi zafika endlini yeNkosi, zahlala emnyango wesango elitsha lendlu yeNkosi. t 
<b>11</b> Abapristi labaprofethi basebekhuluma kuzo iziphathamandla lakubo bonke abantu besithi: Umuntu lo ufanele isigwebo sokufa, ngoba uprofethe emelene lalumuzi, u njengoba lizwile ngendlebe zenu. <b>12</b> Wasekhuluma uJeremiya kuzo zonke iziphathamandla lakubo bonke abantu esithi: INkosi yangithuma ukuprofetha ngimelene lalindlu ngimelene lalumuzi wonke amazwi eliwezwileyo. <b>13</b> Ngakho-ke lungisani indlela zenu lezenzo zenu, v lilalele ilizwi leNkosi uNkulunkulu wenu; khona iNkosi izazisola ngobubi ebukhulume imelene lani. w 
<b>14</b> Kodwa mina, khangela, ngisesandleni senu; x yenzani kimi njengokulungileyo lanjengokuqondileyo emehlweni enu. y <b>15</b> Kodwa yazini isibili ukuthi uba lingibulala, isibili lizakwehlisela igazi elingelacala phezu kwenu laphezu kwalumuzi laphezu kwabakhileyo bawo; ngoba ngeqiniso iNkosi ingithume kini ukuthi ngikhulume wonke la amazwi endlebeni zenu. <b>16</b> Iziphathamandla labo bonke abantu basebesithi kubapristi lakubaprofethi: Lumuntu kafanele isigwebo sokufa, ngoba ukhulumile kithi ebizweni leNkosi uNkulunkulu wethu. 
<b>17</b> Kwasekusukuma abanye z babadala belizwe, aa bakhuluma kulo lonke ibandla labantu, besithi: <b>18</b> UMika umMorashethi waprofetha ensukwini zikaHezekhiya inkosi yakoJuda, ab wakhuluma kibo bonke abantu bakoJuda esithi: Itsho njalo INkosi yamabandla: IZiyoni izalinywa njengensimu, ac leJerusalema ibe zinqumbi, lentaba yendlu ibe zindawo eziphakemeyo zegusu. 
<b>19</b> UHezekhiya inkosi yakoJuda layo wonke uJuda bambulala isibili yini? Kayesabanga yini iNkosi, ad wancenga ubuso beNkosi, njalo iNkosi yazisola ngokubi eyayikukhulume imelene labo? ae , af , ag Ngalokho senza ububi obukhulu simelene lemiphefumulo yethu. ah <b>20</b> Kwakukhona njalo umuntu owaprofetha ebizweni leNkosi, uUriya indodana kaShemaya oweKiriyathi-Jeyarimi, ai owaprofetha ngalumuzi langalelilizwe njengokwamazwi wonke kaJeremiya. 
<b>21</b> Kwathi inkosi uJehoyakhimi aj lawo wonke amaqhawe akhe lazo zonke iziphathamandla zakhe besizwa amazwi akhe, inkosi yadinga ukumbulala; kwathi uUriya ekuzwa, wesaba, wabaleka, waya eGibhithe. <b>22</b> Inkosi uJehoyakhimi yasithuma amadoda eGibhithe, uElinathani ak indodana kaAkhibhori, al lamanye amadoda kanye laye eGibhithe, <b>23</b> basebemkhipha uUriya eGibhithe, bamletha enkosini uJehoyakhimi, owambulala ngenkemba, am waphosela isidumbu sakhe emangcwabeni abantwana babantu. 
<b>24</b> Kodwa isandla sikaAhikhamu an indodana kaShafani ao sasiloJeremiya, ukuze bangamnikeli esandleni sabantu ukuthi bambulale. 
<b>Jeremías 27</b><br><b>1</b> Ekuqaleni kokubusa a kukaJehoyakhimi indodana kaJosiya, inkosi yakoJuda, b kwafika lelilizwi kuJeremiya livela eNkosini, lisithi: <b>2</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi kimi: Zenzele imichilo c lamajogwe, d ukugaxe entanyeni yakho, <b>3</b> ukuthumele enkosini yeEdoma e lenkosini yakoMowabi f lenkosini yabantwana bakoAmoni g lenkosini yeTire h lenkosini yeSidoni, i ngesandla sezithunywa ezifika eJerusalema kuZedekhiya inkosi yakoJuda; j 
<b>4</b> uzilaye ukuthi zithi emakhosini azo: Itsho njalo Nkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Lizakutsho njalo emakhosini enu: <b>5</b> Mina ngenze umhlaba, k umuntu, lenyamazana, okuphezu kobuso bomhlaba, ngamandla ami amakhulu langengalo yami eyeluliweyo, l ngiwunike lowo okuqondileyo emehlweni ami. m <b>6</b> Khathesi mina sengiwanikele wonke lamazwe esandleni sikaNebhukadinezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni inceku yami; n njalo ngimnikile lenyamazana zeganga ukuthi zimsebenzele. o 
<b>7</b> Lezizwe zonke zizasebenzela yena, p lendodana yakhe, q lendodana yendodana yakhe, r kuze kufike isikhathi selakhe ilizwe uqobo; s , t khona izizwe ezinengi lamakhosi amakhulu bezamkhonzisa. u <b>8</b> Kuzakuthi-ke isizwe lombuso okungayikumsebenzela yena, uNebhukadinezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni, lokungayikubeka intamo yakho ngaphansi kwejogwe lenkosi yeBhabhiloni, v lesosizwe ngizasijezisa, itsho iNkosi, ngenkemba w langendlala langomatshayabhuqe wesifo, ngize ngikuqede ngesandla sakhe. 
<b>9</b> Lina-ke lingabalaleli abaprofethi benu x labavumisi benu y labaphuphi benu labachasisi bemibono benu z labalumbi benu aa abakhuluma kini besithi: Kaliyikuyisebenzela inkosi yeBhabhiloni. ab <b>10</b> Ngoba baprofetha amanga kini, ac , ad ukuze balisuse liye khatshana lelizwe lakini, lokuze ngilixotshe liphume, libhubhe. <b>11</b> Kodwa isizwe esizangenisa intamo yaso ngaphansi kwejogwe lenkosi yeBhabhiloni, ae siyisebenzele, ngizasiyekela elizweni laso, itsho iNkosi, ukuthi silisebenze, sihlale kulo. 
<b>12</b> Ngakhuluma lakuZedekhiya inkosi yakoJuda af njengokwalawomazwi wonke ngathi: Ngenisani intamo zenu ngaphansi kwejogwe lenkosi yeBhabhiloni, ag lisebenzele yona labantu bayo, liphile. ah <b>13</b> Lizafelani, wena labantu bakho, ai ngenkemba, ngendlala, langomatshayabhuqe wesifo, aj njengokutsho kweNkosi mayelana lesizwe esingayikuyisebenzela inkosi yeBhabhiloni? ak <b>14</b> Ngakho lingawalaleli amazwi abaprofethi abakhuluma kini besithi: Kaliyikuyisebenzela inkosi yeBhabhiloni; al ngoba baprofetha amanga kini. am 
<b>15</b> Ngoba kangibathumanga, itsho iNkosi, kodwa baprofetha amanga ngebizo lami, an ukuze ngilixotshe, ao libhubhe, lina labaprofethi abaprofetha kini. <b>16</b> Ngasengikhuluma kubapristi lakulababantu bonke, ngisithi: Itsho njalo INkosi: Lingawalaleli amazwi abaprofethi benu abaprofetha kini besithi: ap Khangelani, izitsha zendlu yeNkosi zizabuyiswa khathesi masinyane zivela eBhabhiloni; aq ngoba baprofetha amanga kini. ar <b>17</b> Lingabalaleli; sebenzelani inkosi yeBhabhiloni, as liphile. <b>18</b> Kodwa uba bengabaprofethi, njalo uba ilizwi leNkosi likubo, khathesi kabayincenge iNkosi yamabandla, au ukuthi izitsha eziseleyo endlini yeNkosi lendlini yenkosi yakoJuda leJerusalema zingayi eBhabhiloni. av <b>19</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla mayelana lezinsika, aw langolwandle, ax langezisekelo, ay langensali yezitsha ezisele kulumuzi, 
<b>20</b> uNebhukadinezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni angazithathanga, ekuthumbeni kwakhe uJekoniya indodana kaJehoyakhimi inkosi yakoJuda eJerusalema emusa eBhabhiloni, az lazo zonke izikhulu zakoJuda leJerusalema; <b>21</b> yebo, itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli mayelana lezitsha eziseleyo endlini kaJehova lendlini yenkosi yakoJuda leJerusalema: <b>22</b> Zizasiwa eBhabhiloni, ba zibe khona kuze kube lusuku bb engizakuzihambela ngalo, bc itsho iNkosi; khona ngizazenyusa, bd ngizibuyisele kulindawo. be , bf 
<b>Jeremías 28</b><br><b>1</b> Kwasekusithi ngalowomnyaka, a ekuqaleni kokubusa kukaZedekhiya inkosi yakoJuda, ngomnyaka wesine, ngenyanga yesihlanu, uHananiya b indodana kaAzuri umprofethi c owayengoweGibeyoni d wakhuluma kimi endlini yeNkosi emehlweni abapristi labantu bonke, esithi: <b>2</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli, isithi: Ngilephulile ijogwe lenkosi yeBhabhiloni. e 
<b>3</b> Kusesephakathi kweminyaka emibili yezinsuku ngizabuyisela kulindawo f izitsha zonke zendlu yeNkosi, g uNebhukadinezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni azisusa kulindawo wazisa eBhabhiloni. <b>4</b> Futhi uJekoniya indodana kaJehoyakhimi inkosi yakoJuda, labo bonke abathunjwa bakoJuda, abaya eBhabhiloni, ngizababuyisela kulindawo, itsho iNkosi; ngoba ngizalephula ijogwe lenkosi yeBhabhiloni. h <b>5</b> Wasekhuluma uJeremiya umprofethi kuHananiya umprofethi emehlweni abapristi lemehlweni abantu bonke ababemi endlini yeNkosi; 
<b>6</b> uJeremiya umprofethi wasesithi: Ameni! i INkosi kayenze njalo, iNkosi kayiqinise amazwi akho owaprofethileyo, ukuthi ibuyise izitsha zendlu yeNkosi, labo bonke abathunjwa, kusuke eBhabhiloni kuze kulindawo. <b>7</b> Kodwa ake uzwe lelilizwi engilikhuluma endlebeni zakho lendlebeni zabo bonke abantu: <b>8</b> Abaprofethi ababephambi kwami lababephambi kwakho kwasendulo baprofetha bemelene lamazwe amanengi bemelene lemibuso emikhulu, ngempi langobubi langomatshayabhuqe wesifo. 
<b>9</b> Umprofethi oprofetha j ngokuthula, k lapho ilizwi lomprofethi selifikile, lowomprofethi uzakwaziwa ukuthi iNkosi imthumile ngeqiniso. <b>10</b> Umprofethi uHananiya waselisusa ijogwe entanyeni kaJeremiya umprofethi, walephula. l <b>11</b> UHananiya wasekhuluma emehlweni abantu bonke esithi: Itsho njalo iNkosi: Ngokunjalo ngizalephula ijogwe likaNebhukadinezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni m ngilisuse entanyeni yezizwe zonke kusesephakathi kweminyaka emibili yezinsuku. n UJeremiya umprofethi wasehamba ngendlela yakhe. 
<b>12</b> Laselifika ilizwi leNkosi kuJeremiya, emva kokuthi uHananiya umprofethi esephulile ijogwe elisusa entanyeni kaJeremiya umprofethi, o lisithi: <b>13</b> Hamba uyetshela uHananiya usithi: Itsho njalo iNkosi: Wephulile amajogwe esigodo; kodwa uzawenza amajogwe ensimbi endaweni yawo. <b>14</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Ngigaxile ijogwe lensimbi p entanyeni yazo zonke lezizizwe q ukuze zisebenzele uNebhukadinezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni; futhi zizamsebenzela, r njalo ngimnikile lezinyamazana zeganga. s 
<b>15</b> UJeremiya umprofethi wasesithi kuHananiya umprofethi: Ake uzwe, Hananiya; iNkosi kayikuthumanga, t kodwa wena wenza lababantu ukuthi bathembe u amanga. v <b>16</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi: Khangela, ngizakuxotsha ebusweni bomhlaba; lonyaka uzakufa, ngoba ukhulumile ukuhlamuka umelene leNkosi. w <b>17</b> Wafa-ke uHananiya umprofethi ngalowomnyaka ngenyanga yesikhombisa. 
<b>Jeremías 29</b><br><b>1</b> La-ke ngamazwi encwadi ayithumelayo uJeremiya umprofethi ivela eJerusalema isiya kunsali yabadala babathunjiweyo a lakubapristi b lakubaprofethi lebantwini bonke ayebathumbile uNebhukadinezari besuka eJerusalema wabasa eBhabhiloni <b>2</b> (emva kokuphuma kukaJekoniya inkosi, c , d lendlovukazi, e , f labathenwa, g iziphathamandla zakoJuda lezeJerusalema, h lababazi, i labakhandi, j besuka eJerusalema), 
<b>3</b> ngesandla sikaElasa indodana kaShafani, k loGemariya indodana kaHilikhiya, l uZedekhiya inkosi yakoJuda eyabathuma eBhabhiloni kuNebhukadinezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni, m isithi: <b>4</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli, kibo bonke abathunjiweyo engabenza bathunjwa besuswa eJerusalema basiwa eBhabhiloni: <b>5</b> Yakhani izindlu, n lihlale kizo, lihlanyele izivande, lidle izithelo zazo; 
<b>6</b> lithathe abafazi, lizale amadodana lamadodakazi, lithathele amadodana enu abafazi, lendise amadodakazi enu emadodeni, ukuze azale amadodana lamadodakazi, lande khona, linganciphi. <b>7</b> Lidinge ukuthula komuzi lapho engilithumbele khona, liwukhulekele eNkosini; o ngoba ekuthuleni kwawo lizakuba lokuthula. <b>8</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Akuthi abaprofethi benu labavumisi benu p abaphakathi kwenu bangalikhohlisi; q lingawalaleli amaphupho enu eliwenza aphutshwe; r 
<b>9</b> ngoba baprofetha amanga kini ngebizo lami; s kangibathumanga, t itsho iNkosi. <b>10</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi: Qiniso, lapho iminyaka engamatshumi ayisikhombisa isigcwalisekile eBhabhiloni, u ngizalihambela, v ngiqinise ilizwi lami elihle kini, w ukuthi ngilibuyise kulindawo. x , y <b>11</b> Ngoba mina ngiyazi imicabango engiyicabanga ngani, itsho iNkosi, imicabango yokuthula, kayisiyo eyokubi, ukuthi ngilinike isiphetho lokulindelweyo. z <b>12</b> Khona lizangibiza aa liyekhuleka kimi, ngilizwe. 
<b>13</b> Njalo lizangidinga, ab , ac , ad lingithole, lapho lingidingisisa ngenhliziyo yenu yonke. ae <b>14</b> Njalo ngizatholwa yini, af itsho iNkosi, ngibuyise ukuthunjwa kwenu, ag ngiliqoqe kuzo zonke izizwe ah lakuzo zonke indawo lapho engilixotshele khona, ai itsho iNkosi, ngilibuyise endaweni engalithumba livela khona. <b>15</b> Ngoba litshilo: INkosi isivusele abaprofethi eBhabhiloni. 
<b>16</b> Ngoba utsho njalo uJehova ngenkosi ehlezi esihlalweni sobukhosi sikaDavida aj langabo bonke abantu abahlala kulumuzi, abafowenu abangaphumelanga ekuthunjweni kanye lani: <b>17</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla: Khangelani, ngizathumela phakathi kwabo inkemba, indlala, lomatshayabhuqe wesifo, ak ngibenze babe njengemikhiwa eyenyanyekayo, engadlekiyo ngenxa yobubi. al 
<b>18</b> Ngixotshane labo ngenkemba, ngendlala, langomatshayabhuqe wesifo, ngibanikele ukuba yisesabiso kuyo yonke imibuso yomhlaba, am babe yisiqalekiso, an , ao lento eyesabekayo, ap , aq , ar lokuncifelwayo, as lehlazo, at phakathi kwazo zonke izizwe au lapho engibaxotshele khona; av <b>19</b> ngenxa yokuthi bengawalalelanga amazwi ami, itsho iNkosi, engawathumela kibo ngenceku zami abaprofethi, ngivuka ngovivi ngibathuma, aw kodwa kalilalelanga, ax itsho iNkosi. 
<b>20</b> Lina-ke zwanini ilizwi leNkosi, lina lonke bathunjiweyo, engabathuma besuka eJerusalema besiya eBhabhiloni: ay <b>21</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli, mayelana loAhabi indodana kaKolaya, lamayelana loZedekhiya indodana kaMahaseya, abaprofetha amanga kini ngebizo lami: az Khangelani, ngizabanikela esandleni sikaNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni, ozababulala phambi kwamehlo enu. 
<b>22</b> Njalo kuzathathwa kubo ba isiqalekiso yibo bonke abathunjiweyo koJuda abaseBhabhiloni bb kuthiwe: Kungathi iNkosi ikumise njengoZedekhiya lanjengoAhabi, inkosi yeBhabhiloni eyamosa ngomlilo; bc <b>23</b> ngenxa yokuthi benze ubuthutha koIsrayeli, bafeba labafazi babomakhelwane babo, bd baqamba amazwi angamanga ngebizo lami, be , bf engingabalayanga wona; njalo yimi engaziyo, engingumfakazi, bg itsho iNkosi. <b>24</b> Uzakhuluma kuShemaya umNehelami usithi: 
<b>25</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli, isithi: Ngenxa yokuthi wena uthumele izincwadi ngebizo lakho kubo bonke abantu abaseJerusalema, lakuZefaniya bh indodana kaMahaseya umpristi, bi lakubo bonke abapristi, usithi: <b>26</b> INkosi isikwenze ube ngumpristi esikhundleni sikaJehoyada umpristi, bj ukuthi libe ngababonisi bendlu yeNkosi bk phezu kwakhe wonke umuntu ohlanyayo bl ozitshaya umprofethi, bm ukuthi limfake esitokisini elezankosi entanyeni. bn 
<b>27</b> Khathesi-ke, kungani ungamkhuzanga uJeremiya umAnathothi bo ozitshaya umprofethi kini? bp <b>28</b> Ngoba ngenxa yalokhu usethumele kithi eBhabhiloni esithi: Kuzakuba kude; yakhani izindlu, bq lihlale kizo, lihlanyele izivande, lidle izithelo zazo. <b>29</b> UZefaniya umpristi waseyifunda lincwadi endlebeni zikaJeremiya umprofethi. <b>30</b> Laselifika ilizwi leNkosi kuJeremiya lisithi: 
<b>31</b> Thumela kubo bonke abathunjiweyo usithi: Itsho njalo iNkosi ngoShemaya umNehelami: Ngenxa yokuthi uShemaya eprofethe kini, mina-ke ngingamthumanga, br , bs walenza ukuthi lithembe amanga, bt <b>32</b> ngakho itsho iNkosi: Khangela, ngizajezisa uShemaya umNehelami kanye lenzalo yakhe; kayikuba lamuntu ozahlala phakathi kwalababantu, angaboni okuhle engizakwenzela abantu bami, bu itsho iNkosi, ngoba ukhulume ukuhlamuka emelene leNkosi. bv 
<b>Jeremías 30</b><br><b>1</b> Ilizwi elafika kuJeremiya livela eNkosini lisithi: <b>2</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli, isithi: Zibhalele encwadini wonke amazwi engiwakhulume kuwe. a <b>3</b> Ngoba khangela, insuku ziyeza, b itsho iNkosi, ukuthi ngizabuyisa ukuthunjwa kwabantu bami c uIsrayeli loJuda, d itsho iNkosi, ngibabuyisele elizweni engalinika oyise, njalo bazakudla ilifa lalo. e <b>4</b> Lala ngamazwi iNkosi eyawakhuluma mayelana loIsrayeli lamayelana loJuda. f 
<b>5</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi: Sizwile ilizwi lokuthuthumela; kulokwesaba, hatshi ukuthula. <b>6</b> Ake libuze, libone uba indoda ingabeletha yini? Kungani ngibona ngulowo lalowo owesilisa isandla sakhe siphezu kokhalo lwakhe njengowesifazana obelethayo, g lobuso bonke sebuphenduke baba mhlotshana? h <b>7</b> Maye! ngoba lukhulu lolosuku, i okokuthi kalukho olunjengalo; j kuyisikhathi sokuhlupheka kukaJakobe, kodwa uzasindiswa kukho.

<b>Jeremías 31</b><br><b>1</b> Ngalesosikhathi, a itsho iNkosi, ngizakuba nguNkulunkulu wensapho zonke zakoIsrayeli; b bona-ke bazakuba ngabantu bami. <b>2</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Abantu babaseleyo benkemba bathola umusa enkangala; ngitsho uIsrayeli, lapho ngisiyamphumuza. c <b>3</b> INkosi yabonakala kimi ikhatshana isithi: Yebo, ngikuthandile d ngothando olulaphakade, e ngakho-ke ngikudonsile ngothandolomusa. f 
<b>4</b> Ngizabuya ngikwakhe, g wakheke, wena ntombi emsulwa yakoIsrayeli; uzabuya uvunuliswe ngezigujana zakho, h uphume phakathi kokugida i kwabathokozayo. j <b>5</b> Uzabuya uhlanyele izivini phezu kwezintaba k zeSamariya; l abahlanyeli bazahlanyela, badle izithelo ▼▼ badle izithelo: Heb bazingcolise.. <b>6</b> Ngoba kuzakuba khona usuku abazamemeza ngalo abalindi entabeni yakoEfrayimi n besithi: Sukumani, senyuke siye eZiyoni, eNkosini uNkulunkulu wethu. o 
<b>7</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi: Mhlabeleleni uJakobe ngentokozo, p limemezele phakathi q kwenhloko yezizwe; r zwakalisani, dumisani, lithi: Nkosi, sindisa abantu bakho, s insali yakoIsrayeli. <b>8</b> Khangelani, ngizabaletha bevela elizweni lenyakatho, t ngibabuthe u emaceleni omhlaba; v phakathi kwabo kukhona iziphofu labaqhulayo, w abesifazana abakhulelweyo lababelethayo ndawonye; bazabuya lapha belibandla elikhulu. 
<b>9</b> Bazabuya belokukhala, x ngizabakhokhela belokuncenga; y ngizabahambisa emifuleni yamanzi z ngendlela eqondileyo, aa abangayikukhubeka kuyo; ngoba nginguyise kaIsrayeli, ab loEfrayimi ac ulizibulo lami. ad <b>10</b> Zwanini ilizwi leNkosi, lina zizwe, lilimemeze ezihlengeni ezikhatshana, lithi: Yena owachitha uIsrayeli uzambutha, ae amgcine, njengomelusi umhlambi wakhe. af <b>11</b> Ngoba iNkosi imhlengile uJakobe, yamhlawulela ag emsusa esandleni solamandla kulaye. ah 
<b>12</b> Ngakho bazakuza bahlabelele ekuphakameni kweZiyoni, ai bagelezele aj kokuhle kweNkosi, ak ngamabele, al , am langewayini elitsha, an , ao , ap langamafutha, aq langamazinyane ezimvu, lenkomo; lomphefumulo wabo ube njengesivande esithelelweyo, ar kabasayikubuya babe losizi. as <b>13</b> Khona intombi izathokoza ekugideni, at lamajaha labadala kanyekanye; ngoba ngizaphendula ukulila kwabo kube yintokozo, ngibaduduze, ngibathokozise, bangabi losizi. au 
<b>14</b> Njalo ngizasuthisa umphefumulo wabapristi ngamanono, av labantu bami bazasuthiswa ngokuhle kwami, itsho iNkosi. <b>15</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Ilizwi lezwakala aw eRama, ax ukulila, ukukhala inyembezi okubuhlungu kakhulu; ay uRasheli ekhalela abantwana bakhe, esala ukududuzwa ngabantwana bakhe, ngoba bengasekho. az <b>16</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Bamba ilizwi lakho ekukhaleni, lamehlo akho ezinyembezini; ngoba kulomvuzo womsebenzi wakho, itsho iNkosi, ngoba bazabuya bevela elizweni lesitha. ba 
<b>17</b> Kukhona-ke ithemba enzalweni yakho, bb itsho iNkosi; ngoba abantwana bakho bazabuyela emngceleni wakibo. bc <b>18</b> Ngizwile lokuzwa uEfrayimi ezililela bdesithi: Ungilayile, njalo ngalaywa njengethole elingathambanga; ngiphenduka, njalo ngizaphendula; be ngoba uyiNkosi uNkulunkulu wami. <b>19</b> Isibili emva kokuphenduka kwami, bf ngazisola, lemva kokufundiswa kwami ngatshaya phezu kwethangazi lami; bg ngaba lenhloni, njalo ngayangeka, ngoba ngathwala ihlazo lobutsha bami. bh 
<b>20</b> UEfrayimi uyindodana yami eligugu yini, ungumntwana wami othokozisayo yini? bi Ngoba selokhu ngakhuluma ngimelene laye, ngisamkhumbula ngoqotho; ngenxa yalokho imibilini yami iyakhala ngaye; bj isibili ngizakuba lesihawu kuye, kutsho iNkosi. <b>21</b> Zibekele izitshengiso zendlela, bk uzimisele izinsika zeziqondiso, ubeke inhliziyo yakho kumgwaqo omkhulu, bl indlela owahamba ngayo; bm phenduka, ntombi emsulwa yakoIsrayeli, uphendukele kule imizi yakini. 
<b>22</b> Koze kube nini uzulazula, bn ndodakazi ehlehlela nyovane? bo Ngoba iNkosi idale into entsha emhlabeni: Owesifazana uzahanqa indoda. bp <b>23</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Bazabuya batsho lelilizwi elizweni lakoJuda lemizini yakho, lapho ngibuyisela ukuthunjwa kwabo: bq Kwangathi iNkosi ingakubusisa, br khaya lokulunga, bs ntaba yobungcwele. bt <b>24</b> Njalo kuzahlala kulo uJuda layo yonke imizi yakhe ndawonye, bu abalimi labahamba lemihlambi. 
<b>25</b> Ngoba ngisuthise bv umphefumulo okhatheleyo, bw ngigcwalise wonke umphefumulo ophela amandla. <b>26</b> Ngasengiphaphama, ngabona; lobuthongo bami babumnandi kimi. <b>27</b> Khangela, insuku ziyeza, bx itsho iNkosi, lapho ngizahlanyela indlu kaIsrayeli lendlu kaJuda by ngenhlanyelo yabantu bz lenhlanyelo yenyamazana.

<b>Jeremías 32</b><br><b>1</b> Ilizwi elafika kuJeremiya livela eNkosini emnyakeni wetshumi kaZedekhiya, inkosi yakoJuda, a owawungumnyaka wetshumi lesificaminwembili kaNebhukadirezari. b <b>2</b> Ngoba ngalesosikhathi ibutho lenkosi yeBhabhiloni layivimbezela iJerusalema; uJeremiya umprofethi wayevalelwe-ke c , d egumeni lentolongo, e elalisendlini yenkosi yakoJuda. 
<b>3</b> Ngoba uZedekhiya inkosi yakoJuda wayemvalele esithi: Kungani wena uprofetha usithi: Itsho njalo iNkosi: Khangela, ngiwunikela lumuzi esandleni senkosi yeBhabhiloni, f njalo izawuthumba; g <b>4</b> loZedekhiya inkosi yakoJuda h kayiyikuphunyuka esandleni samaKhaladiya, i kodwa uzanikelwa isibili esandleni senkosi yeBhabhiloni, lomlomo wakhe uzakhuluma lomlomo wayo, j lamehlo akhe abone amehlo ayo; k 
<b>5</b> izakhokhela-ke uZedekhiya imuse eBhabhiloni, l , m , n abe khona ngize ngimhambele, o itsho iNkosi; lanxa lisilwa lamaKhaladiya, p kaliyikuphumelela. <b>6</b> UJeremiya wasesithi: Ilizwi leNkosi lafika kimi lisithi: <b>7</b> Khangela, uHanameli indodana kaShaluma q umalumakho uzafika kuwe esithi: Zithengele insimu yami eseAnathothi, r ngoba nguwe olelungelo lokuhlenga ukuthi uthenge. s 
<b>8</b> Kwasekufika kimi uHanameli indodana kamalumami, njengokutsho kweNkosi, egumeni lentolongo, t wasesithi kimi: Ake uthenge insimu yami eseAnathothi u eselizweni lakoBhenjamini, ngoba ulelungelo lelifa, ulohlengo; zithengele yona. Ngasengisazi ukuthi yilizwi leNkosi. <b>9</b> Ngasengiyithenga insimu kuHanameli indodana kamalumami, eseAnathothi, v ngamlinganisela imali w engamashekeli* ayisikhombisa lenhlamvu ezilitshumi zesiliva. <b>10</b> Ngasengibhala incwadi, ngayiphawula, x ngenza abafakazi bafakaze, y ngalinganisa imali ngesikali. z 
<b>11</b> Ngasengithatha incwadi yokuthenga enanyekiweyo njengokomthetho aa lezimiso, ab levulekileyo, ac <b>12</b> nganika incwadi yokuthenga kuBharukhi ad indodana kaNeriya, ae indodana kaMahaseya, phambi kwamehlo kaHanameli indodana kamalumami, laphambi kwamehlo abafakazi ababephawule incwadi yokuthenga, af phambi kwamehlo awo wonke amaJuda ayehlezi egumeni lentolongo. ag <b>13</b> Ngasengilaya uBharukhi ah phambi kwamehlo abo ngisithi: 
<b>14</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Thatha lezizincwadi, le incwadi yokuthenga, zombili enanyekiweyo ai lale incwadi evulekileyo, aj uzifake embizeni yebumba, ukuze zihlale insuku ezinengi. <b>15</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Kuzabuya kuthengwe izindlu lamasimu lezivini ak kulelilizwe. al <b>16</b> Ngasengikhuleka eNkosini, senginike uBharukhi indodana kaNeriya am incwadi yokuthenga, an ngisithi: 
<b>17</b> Hawu Nkosi Jehova! Khangela, nguwe owenze amazulu lomhlaba ao ngamandla akho amakhulu ap langengalo yakho eyeluliweyo; aq kakuladaba olunzima kakhulu kuwe; ar <b>18</b> owenza uthandolomusa kwabayizinkulungwane, as uphindisela isiphambeko saboyise at , au kusifuba sabantwana babo emva kwabo; av , aw uNkulunkulu omkhulu, olamandla, ax iNkosi yamabandla libizo layo; ay 
<b>19</b> omkhulu ngeseluleko, az lolamandla ngokwenza; ngoba amehlo akho avulekele zonke indlela zamadodana abantu, ba ukunika ngulowo lalowo ngokwendlela zakhe, bb lanjengesithelo sezenzo zakhe; <b>20</b> owamisa izibonakaliso lezimangaliso elizweni leGibhithe, bc , bd kuze kube lamuhla, lakoIsrayeli laphakathi kwabantu; wasuzenzela be ibizo bf njengalamuhla. <b>21</b> Wabakhupha abantu bakho uIsrayeli elizweni leGibhithe ngezibonakaliso, bg langezimangaliso, langesandla esilamandla, bh langengalo eyeluliweyo, bi , bj langokwesabeka okukhulu; bk 
<b>22</b> wabanika lelilizwe, owafunga kuboyise ukubanika lona, bl ilizwe eligeleza uchago loluju. bm , bn <b>23</b> Basebengena badla ilifa lalo, kodwa kabalilalelanga ilizwi lakho, bo kabahambanga ngomlayo wakho, kabakwenzanga konke owabalaya khona ukuthi bakwenze; ngakho ubehlisele bonke lobububi. <b>24</b> Khangela amadundulu; afikile emzini ukuwuthumba, lomuzi unikelwe esandleni samaKhaladiya bp alwa emelene lawo; ngenxa yenkemba bq lendlala br lomatshayabhuqe wesifo; bs lalokho owakukhulumayo kwenzakele; khangela-ke, uyakubona. 
<b>25</b> Kanti, wena Nkosi Jehova, uthe kimi: Zithengele insimu ngemali, bt wenze abafakazi bafakaze; bu ngoba umuzi unikelwe esandleni samaKhaladiya. bv <b>26</b> Laselifika ilizwi leNkosi kuJeremiya lisithi: <b>27</b> Khangela, ngiyiNkosi, uNkulunkulu wayo yonke inyama; bw kambe, kukhona loba yini okunzima kakhulu kimi? bx <b>28</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi: Khangela, by ngiwunikela lumuzi esandleni samaKhaladiya, lesandleni sikaNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni, ozawuthumba. 
<b>29</b> LamaKhaladiya alwa emelene lalumuzi azangena, awuthungele lumuzi ngomlilo, awutshise kanye lezindlu bz obekunikelelwa phezu kwazo impepha kuBhali, ca lobekuthululelwa phezu kwazo iminikelo yokunathwayo kwabanye onkulunkulu, cb ukuze bangithukuthelise. cc <b>30</b> Ngoba abantwana bakoIsrayeli labantwana bakoJuda bebesenza okubi kuphela emehlweni ami, kusukela ebutsheni babo; cd ngoba abantwana bakoIsrayeli bangizondisile ce kuphela ngesenzo sezandla zabo, cf itsho iNkosi. 
<b>31</b> Ngoba lumuzi ubukimi ngokolaka lwami langokwentukuthelo yami, kusukela ngosuku abawakha ngalo kuze kube lamuhla, ukuze ngiwususe ebusweni bami; cg <b>32</b> ngenxa yabo bonke ububi babantwana bakoIsrayeli lobabantwana bakoJuda abakwenzayo ukungithukuthelisa, ch bona, ci amakhosi abo, iziphathamandla zabo, abapristi babo, labaprofethi babo, labantu bakoJuda, labahlali beJerusalema. <b>33</b> Basebengiphendulela umhlana, cj hatshi-ke ubuso; lanxa ngibafundisile ngivuka ngovivi ngibafundisa; ck kanti kabalalelanga ukwemukela ukulaywa. cl 
<b>34</b> Kodwa bamisa izinengiso zabo endlini, cm obizo lami libizwa kuyo, cn ukuze bayingcolise. co <b>35</b> Basebesakha indawo eziphakemeyo zikaBhali cp ezisesihotsheni sendodana kaHinomu, cq ukuthi badabulise amadodana abo lamadodakazi abo emlilweni aye kuMoleki, cr engingabalayanga khona cs lokungafikanga enhliziyweni yami, ukuthi benze lesisinengiso, ukuze benze uJuda one. ct 
<b>36</b> Khathesi-ke, ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli, mayelana lomuzi lo elithi ngawo: Unikelwe esandleni senkosi yeBhabhiloni cu ngenkemba langendlala langomatshayabhuqe wesifo: cv <b>37</b> Khangelani, ngizabaqoqa bevela emazweni wonke cw lapho engibaxotshele khona cx ekuthukutheleni kwami langokufutheka kwami langolaka olukhulu; cy njalo ngizababuyisa kulindawo, ngibenze bahlale bevikelekile. cz <b>38</b> Yebo, bazakuba ngabantu bami, lami ngibe nguNkulunkulu wabo. da 
<b>39</b> Ngizabanika inhliziyonye db , dc lendlelanye, ukuze bangesabe zonke insuku, dd kube kuhle kubo de lakubantwana babo emva kwabo. <b>40</b> Njalo ngizakwenza labo isivumelwano esilaphakade, df , dg ukuze ngingaphenduki ekubalandeleni, ukuthi ngibenzele okuhle, ngifake ukungesaba enhliziyweni yabo, dh ukuze bangaphenduki basuke kimi. <b>41</b> Yebo, ngizathokoza ngabo ukubenzela okuhle, di ngibahlanyele ngobuqotho kulelilizwe, ngenhliziyo yami yonke langomphefumulo wami wonke. dj 
<b>42</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi: Njengoba ngehlisele phezu kwalababantu bonke lobububi obukhulu, dk ngokunjalo ngizabehlisela konke okuhle engikukhulumileyo ngabo. dl <b>43</b> Amasimu azathengwa-ke kulelilizwe dm elithi ngalo: Liyinkangala engelamuntu lenyamazana, dn linikelwe esandleni samaKhaladiya. do 
<b>44</b> Bazathenga amasimu ngemali, babhale izincwadi, dp baphawule, dq benze abafakazi bafakaze, dr elizweni lakoBhenjamini ds lemaphandleni eJerusalema dt lemizini yakoJuda du lemizini yezintaba dv lemizini yesihotsha dw lemizini yeningizimu; dx ngoba ngizabuyisa ukuthunjwa kwabo, dy itsho iNkosi.

<b>Jeremías 34</b><br><b>1</b> Ilizwi elafika kuJeremiya livela eNkosini, lapho uNebhukadinezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni, a lebutho lakhe lonke, lemibuso yonke yomhlaba yokubusa kwesandla sakhe, b lazo zonke izizwe, babesilwa bemelene leJerusalema bemelene layo yonke imizi yayo, lisithi: <b>2</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Hamba ukhulume kuZedekhiya inkosi yakoJuda c uthi kuye: Itsho njalo iNkosi: Khangela, nginikela lumuzi esandleni senkosi yeBhabhiloni, d ukuthi iwutshise ngomlilo. e 
<b>3</b> Lawe kawuyikuphunyuka esandleni sayo, f kodwa uzabanjwa isibili, unikelwe esandleni sayo; g lamehlo akho azabona amehlo enkosi yeBhabhiloni, h lomlomo wayo uzakhuluma lomlomo wakho; i njalo uzakuya eBhabhiloni. j <b>4</b> Kodwa zwana ilizwi leNkosi, Zedekhiya, nkosi yakoJuda: k Itsho njalo iNkosi ngawe: Kawuyikufa ngenkemba. <b>5</b> Uzakufa ngokuthula, l lanjengokubaselwa kwaboyihlo, m amakhosi endulo, ayephambi kwakho, ngokunjalo bazakubasela, bakulilele, n besithi: Maye inkosi! Ngoba mina ngilikhulumile lelolizwi, itsho iNkosi. 
<b>6</b> UJeremiya umprofethi wasewakhuluma wonke lamazwi kuZedekhiya inkosi yakoJuda eJerusalema, <b>7</b> lapho ibutho lenkosi yeBhabhiloni lisilwa limelene leJerusalema limelene layo yonke imizi yakoJuda eyayisele, limelene leLakishi o limelene leAzeka; p ngoba yiyo imizi ebiyelweyo eyayisisele phakathi kwemizi yakoJuda. q , r Mayelana lezigqili ezazingakhululwanga <b>8</b> Ilizwi elafika kuJeremiya livela eNkosini, emva kokuthi inkosi uZedekhiya esenzile isivumelwano labo bonke abantu ababeseJerusalema ukumemezela inkululeko kubo, s 
<b>9</b> ukuthi ngulowo lalowo ayekele isigqili sakhe t sihambe sikhululekile, u langulowo lalowo isigqilikazi sakhe, umHebheru kumbe umHebherukazi, ukuze kungabi khona umuntu owenza umJuda engumfowabo abe yisigqili. v <b>10</b> Kwasekulalela zonke iziphathamandla labo bonke abantu ababengene lesosivumelwano sokuthi ngulowo lalowo ayekele isigqili sakhe langulowo lalowo isigqilikazi sakhe sihambe sikhululekile, w bangaphindi bazenze zibe yizigqili; basebelalela, baziyekela zahamba. 
<b>11</b> Kodwa emva kwalokho baphenduka, x bazibuyisa izigqili lezigqilikazi ababeziyekele zihambe zikhululekile, y bazehlisela phansi zaba yizigqili lezigqilikazi. <b>12</b> Ngakho ilizwi leNkosi lafika kuJeremiya livela eNkosini, lisithi: z <b>13</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Mina ngenza isivumelwano laboyihlo mhla ngibakhupha elizweni leGibhithe, aa endlini yobugqili, ngisithi: 
<b>14</b> Ekupheleni ab kweminyaka eyisikhombisa lizayekela ahambe, ngulowo lalowo umfowabo umHebheru ac , ad owayethengiswe kuwe, ae esekusebenzele iminyaka eyisithupha, umyekele ahambe ekhululekile esuka kuwe. Kodwa oyihlo kabangilalelanga, af kababekanga indlebe yabo. ag <b>15</b> Lina seliphendukile lamuhla, lenza okulungileyo emehlweni ami, limemezela inkululeko, ngulowo lalowo kumakhelwane wakhe, ah lenza isivumelwano phambi kwami endlini ebizwa ngebizo lami. ai 
<b>16</b> Kodwa liphendukile, langcolisa ibizo lami, aj libuyisa, ngulowo lalowo isigqili sakhe langulowo lalowo isigqilikazi sakhe, ak ebeliziyekele zahamba zikhululekile njengokufisa kwazo, al lazehlisela phansi, zaba yizigqili lezigqilikazi zenu. 
<b>17</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi: Lina kalingilalelanga, ukumemezela inkululeko, am kube ngulowo lalowo kumfowabo langulowo lalowo kumakhelwane wakhe; khangelani, ngizamemezela kini, an itsho iNkosi, inkululeko ao enkembeni, ap kumatshayabhuqe wesifo, aq lendlaleni; ar ngilinikele libe yisesabiso as kuyo yonke imibuso yomhlaba. at <b>18</b> Nginikele amadoda aseqileyo isivumelwano sami, angaqinisanga amazwi esivumelwano asenza phambi kwami, au lapho adabula ithole kabili, av adlula phakathi kweziqa zalo; 
<b>19</b> iziphathamandla zakoJuda, leziphathamandla zeJerusalema, abathenwa, aw labapristi, labo bonke abantu belizwe, abadabula phakathi ax kweziqa zethole. <b>20</b> Yebo, ngizabanikela esandleni sezitha zabo lesandleni salabo abadinga impilo yabo; ay lezidumbu zabo zizakuba yikudla kwenyoni zamazulu lenyamazana zomhlaba. az <b>21</b> LoZedekhiya inkosi yakoJuda leziphathamandla zakhe ngizabanikela esandleni sezitha zabo, lesandleni salabo abadinga impilo yabo, ba lesandleni sebutho lenkosi yeBhabhiloni, abenyuke basuka kini. bb 
<b>22</b> Khangelani, ngizalaya, bc itsho iNkosi, ngibabuyise kulumuzi; bd njalo bazakulwa bemelene lawo, be bawuthumbe, bf , bg bawutshise ngomlilo; bh , bi lemizi yakoJuda ngizayenza ibe lunxiwa bj olungelamhlali. bk 
<b>Jeremías 35</b><br><b>1</b> Ilizwi elafika kuJeremiya livela eNkosini ensukwini zikaJehoyakhimi indodana kaJosiya, inkosi yakoJuda, a lisithi: <b>2</b> Yana endlini yamaRekabi, b ukhulume lawo, uwalethe endlini yeNkosi kwelinye lamakamelo, c uwanathise iwayini. <b>3</b> Ngasengithatha uJahazaniya, indodana kaJeremiya, indodana kaHabaziniya, labafowabo, lawo wonke amadodana akhe, lendlu yonke yamaRekabi. 
<b>4</b> Ngabaletha endlini yeNkosi, ekamelweni lamadodana kaHanani, d indodana kaIgidaliya, umuntu kaNkulunkulu, e elaliseduze lekamelo leziphathamandla, f elalingaphezu g kwekamelo likaMahaseya, h indodana kaShaluma, i umlindimbundu. j <b>5</b> Ngasengibeka phambi kwamadodana endlu yamaRekabi izimbiza ezigcwele iwayini, lezinkezo, ngathi kibo: Nathani iwayini. <b>6</b> Kodwa bathi: Kasilinathi iwayini, ngoba uJonadabi, indodana kaRekabi, k ubaba, wasilaya esithi: Linganathi iwayini, lina labantwana benu, kuze kube nininini. 
<b>7</b> Njalo lingakhi izindlu, lingahlanyeli inhlanyelo, lingahlanyeli izivini, lingabi lazo; kodwa lihlale emathenteni zonke insuku zenu, ukuze liphile insuku ezinengi ebusweni bomhlaba lapho elihlala khona njengabezizwe. l <b>8</b> Ngakho salilalela ilizwi likaJonadabi, indodana kaRekabi, ubaba, kukho konke asilaya ngakho, ukuthi singanathi iwayini zonke insuku zethu, thina, omkethu, amadodana ethu, lamadodakazi ethu; <b>9</b> lokuthi singakhi izindlu zokuhlala kwethu; njalo singabi lesivini kumbe insimu kumbe inhlanyelo; 
<b>10</b> kodwa sihlala emathenteni, silalele senze njengakho konke uJonadabi ubaba asilaya khona. <b>11</b> Kodwa kwathi lapho uNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni m esenyukela elizweni, sathi: Wozani siye eJerusalema ngenxa yebutho lamaKhaladiya n langenxa yebutho lamaSiriya. o Sasesihlala eJerusalema. p <b>12</b> Laselifika ilizwi leNkosi kuJeremiya lisithi: <b>13</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Hamba uthi ebantwini bakoJuda lakwabahlali beJerusalema: Kaliyikwemukela yini ukulaywa ukuthi lilalele amazwi ami? q itsho iNkosi. 
<b>14</b> Aqiniswa amazwi kaJonadabi, indodana kaRekabi, awalaya abantwana bakhe ukuthi banganathi iwayini; ngoba kabalinathanga kuze kube lamuhla, ngoba bawulalele umlayo kayise. Lami ngikhulumile kini ngivuke ngovivi ngikhuluma, r kodwa kalingilalelanga. 
<b>15</b> Futhi ngithume kini zonke inceku zami abaprofethi, s ngivuka ngovivi ngibathuma, t ngisithi: Ake liphenduke, ngulowo lalowo endleleni yakhe embi, u lenze izenzo zenu zibe zinhle, v lingalandeli abanye onkulunkulu ukuthi libakhonze, w ukuze lihlale elizweni engalinika lina laboyihlo. Kodwa kalibekanga indlebe yenu, x kalilalelanga kimi. <b>16</b> Ngoba amadodana kaJonadabi, indodana kaRekabi, awuqinisile umlayo kayise awalaya wona, kodwa lababantu kabangilaleli, 
<b>17</b> ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi, uNkulunkulu wamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Khangelani, ngizakwehlisela phezu kukaJuda laphezu kwabakhileyo bonke beJerusalema bonke ububi engibukhulume ngabo; ngoba ngikhulumile kibo, kodwa kabalalelanga, ngibabizile, kodwa kabaphendulanga. y <b>18</b> Lakuyo indlu yamaRekabi uJeremiya wathi: Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Ngenxa yokuthi lilalele umlayo kaJonadabi uyihlo, lagcina yonke imilayo yakhe, lenza njengakho konke alilaya khona, 
<b>19</b> ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Kakuyikuqunywa muntu kuJonadabi indodana kaRekabi oma phambi kwami insuku zonke. z 
<b>Jeremías 36</b><br><b>1</b> Kwasekusithi ngomnyaka wesine kaJehoyakhimi, indodana kaJosiya, inkosi yakoJuda, a lelilizwi lafika kuJeremiya livela eNkosini, lisithi: <b>2</b> Zithathele umqulu wogwalo, b ubhale kiwo wonke amazwi engiwakhulume kuwe ngoIsrayeli c langoJuda d langazo zonke izizwe, e kusukela ngosuku lokukhuluma kwami kuwe, kusukela ensukwini zikaJosiya, f kuze kube lamuhla. 
<b>3</b> Mhlawumbe abendlu kaJuda bazakuzwa bonke ububi engicabanga ukubenza kubo; g ukuze baphenduke, ngulowo lalowo endleleni yakhe embi, h ukuze ngithethelele ububi babo lesono sabo. i <b>4</b> UJeremiya wasebiza uBharukhi indodana kaNeriya; j uBharukhi wasebhala okuvela emlonyeni kaJeremiya, wonke amazwi eNkosi k eyayiwakhulume kuye, emqulwini wogwalo. l <b>5</b> UJeremiya waselaya uBharukhi esithi: Ngivinjelwe, m kangilakungena endlini yeNkosi. 
<b>6</b> Ngakho hamba wena, ufunde emqulwini owubhalileyo, kuvela emlonyeni wami, amazwi eNkosi endlebeni zabantu endlini yeNkosi ngosuku lokuzila ukudla; n uwafunde futhi endlebeni zakhe wonke uJuda abavela emizini yabo. <b>7</b> Mhlawumbe ukuncenga kwabo kuzakuwa phambi kweNkosi, o baphenduke, ngulowo lalowo endleleni yakhe embi; p ngoba lukhulu ulaka lentukuthelo iNkosi ekukhulume kulababantu. <b>8</b> UBharukhi indodana kaNeriya wasesenza njengakho konke uJeremiya umprofethi ayemlaye khona, efunda egwalweni amazwi eNkosi endlini yeNkosi. 
<b>9</b> Kwasekuthi emnyakeni wesihlanu kaJehoyakhimi, indodana kaJosiya, inkosi yakoJuda, ngenyanga yesificamunwemunye, bamemezela q ukuzila ukudla phambi kweNkosi, bonke abantu eJerusalema, r labantu bonke abavela emizini yakoJuda eJerusalema. <b>10</b> UBharukhi wasefunda egwalweni amazwi kaJeremiya endlini yeNkosi, ekamelweni likaGemariya, s indodana kaShafani, t umbhali, u egumeni elingaphezulu, v emnyango wesango elitsha lendlu yeNkosi, w endlebeni zabantu bonke. 
<b>11</b> UMikhaya, indodana kaGemariya, indodana kaShafani, x wasesizwa wonke amazwi eNkosi avela kulolugwalo, <b>12</b> wehlela endlini yenkosi, ekamelweni lombhali; y khangela-ke, zonke iziphathamandla zazihlezi lapho, uElishama z umbhali, aa loDelaya indodana kaShemaya, loElinathani indodana kaAkhibhori, ab loGemariya indodana kaShafani, ac loZedekhiya indodana kaHananiya, ad leziphathamandla zonke. <b>13</b> UMikhaya wasebatshela wonke amazwi ayewezwile, lapho uBharukhi efunda egwalweni endlebeni zabantu. 
<b>14</b> Zonke iziphathamandla zasezithuma uJehudi, indodana kaNethaniya, indodana kaShelemiya, ae indodana kaKushi, kuBharukhi, zisithi: Umqulu ofunde kuwo endlebeni zabantu, uthathe ngesandla sakho uze. Ngakho uBharukhi indodana kaNeriya wathatha umqulu ngesandla sakhe, waya kuzo. <b>15</b> Zasezisithi kuye: Ake uhlale phansi, uwufunde endlebeni zethu. UBharukhi wasefunda endlebeni zazo. 
<b>16</b> Kwasekusithi sezizwile wonke amazwi, zethukile, ngulowo lalowo ekhangele kumakhelwane wakhe, af zathi kuBharukhi: Sizayitshela isibili inkosi wonke amazwi la. <b>17</b> Zasezimbuza uBharukhi zisithi: Ake usitshele, wawabhala njani wonke lamazwi emlonyeni wakhe? <b>18</b> UBharukhi wasesithi kuzo: Wangifundela wonke lamazwi evela emlonyeni wakhe, ngawabhala egwalweni ngeyinki. ag <b>19</b> Iziphathamandla zasezisithi kuBharukhi: Hamba ucatshe, wena loJeremiya, kakungabi lamuntu owaziyo lapho elikhona. 
<b>20</b> Zasezingena enkosini egumeni, kodwa umqulu zawubeka ekamelweni likaElishama umbhali; ah zakhuluma wonke amazwi endlebeni zenkosi. <b>21</b> Inkosi yasithuma uJehudi ukuthi awulande umqulu; wasewuthatha ekamelweni likaElishama umbhali; ai uJehudi wawufunda endlebeni zenkosi lendlebeni zazo zonke iziphathamandla ezazimi enkosini. aj <b>22</b> Inkosi yayihlezi-ke endlini yobusika ak ngenyanga yesificamunwemunye; al umlilo wawuvutha embawuleni phambi kwayo. 
<b>23</b> Kwasekusithi lapho uJehudi esefundile amakhasi amathathu loba amane, yawasika ngengqamu yombhali, yawaphosela emlilweni osembawuleni, waze watsha waphela umqulu wonke emlilweni osembawuleni. <b>24</b> Kodwa kabesabanga, am kabadabulanga izembatho zabo, an , ao inkosi lenceku zayo zonke, abawezwayo wonke lamazwi. <b>25</b> Loba kunjalo labo oElinathani loDelaya loGemariya ap beyincenga inkosi ukuthi ingawutshisi umqulu, aq kodwa kayibalalelanga. 
<b>26</b> Kodwa inkosi yalaya uJerameli indodana yenkosi, loSeraya indodana kaAziriyeli, loShelemiya indodana kaAbideli, ar ukuze babambe uBharukhi umbhali loJeremiya umprofethi; as kodwa iNkosi yabafihla. <b>27</b> Ilizwi leNkosi laselifika kuJeremiya emva kokuthi inkosi isiwutshisile umqulu lamazwi uBharukhi ayewabhalile evela emlonyeni kaJeremiya, lisithi: <b>28</b> Zithathele futhi omunye umqulu, ubhale kuwo wonke amazwi okuqala ayesemqulwini wokuqala, uJehoyakhimi inkosi yakoJuda eyawatshisayo. 
<b>29</b> Njalo uzakuthi kuJehoyakhimi inkosi yakoJuda: Itsho njalo iNkosi: Wena utshisile lumqulu, usithi: Ubhaleleni kiwo usithi: Inkosi yeBhabhiloni izafika isibili, ilichithe lelilizwe, at yenze ukuthi kuphele kulo abantu lenyamazana? <b>30</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi mayelana loJehoyakhimi inkosi yakoJuda: Kayikuba lamuntu au ohlala esihlalweni sobukhosi sikaDavida; av lesidumbu sakhe sizaphoselwa ekutshiseni aw kwemini lekuqandeni kobusuku. 
<b>31</b> Ngizamjezisa-ke yena, lenzalo yakhe, lenceku zakhe, ububi babo; ngehlisele phezu kwabo laphezu kwabahlali beJerusalema laphezu kwabantu bakoJuda konke okubi engikukhulume kubo, kodwa kabalalelanga. <b>32</b> UJeremiya wasethatha omunye umqulu, wawunika uBharukhi, indodana kaNeriya, umbhali, owabhala kiwo okuvela emlonyeni kaJeremiya wonke amazwi ogwalo uJehoyakhimi inkosi yakoJuda eyalutshisayo ngomlilo; kwengezelelwa-ke kiwo amanye amazwi amanengi afanana lawo. 
<b>Jeremías 37</b><br><b>1</b> Inkosi uZedekhiya indodana kaJosiya a wabusa-ke esikhundleni sikaKoniya indodana kaJehoyakhimi, b uNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni ambeka abe yinkosi elizweni lakoJuda. c , d <b>2</b> Kodwa kawalalelanga, yena e lenceku zakhe f labantu belizwe, amazwi eNkosi eyayiwakhulume ngesandla sikaJeremiya umprofethi. <b>3</b> Inkosi uZedekhiya yasithuma uJehukali g indodana kaShelemiya h , i loZefaniya j indodana kaMahaseya umpristi kuJeremiya umprofethi, k isithi: Ake usikhulekele eNkosini uNkulunkulu wethu. 
<b>4</b> UJeremiya wangena-ke waphuma phakathi kwabantu, l ngoba babengakamfaki entolongweni. m <b>5</b> Ibutho likaFaro laliphumile-ke eGibhithe; n kwathi amaKhaladiya ayevimbezela iJerusalema esizwa amahungahunga ngabo, enyuka asuka eJerusalema. o <b>6</b> Laselifika ilizwi leNkosi kuJeremiya umprofethi lisithi: <b>7</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Lizakutsho njalo enkosini yakoJuda elithume kimi ukungibuza: p Khangela, ibutho likaFaro eliphumele ukulisiza q lizabuyela elizweni lalo, iGibhithe. r 
<b>8</b> AmaKhaladiya azabuya futhi, s alwe emelene lalumuzi, t awuthumbe, u awutshise ngomlilo. v , w <b>9</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Lingakhohlisi imiphefumulo yenu lisithi: AmaKhaladiya isibili azasuka kithi; ngoba kawayikusuka. <b>10</b> Ngoba loba lilitshaya ibutho lonke lamaKhaladiya alwa emelene lani, x kusale amadoda agwaziweyo phakathi kwabo, azasukuma, yileyo laleyo ethenteni layo, awutshise lumuzi ngomlilo. y <b>11</b> Kwasekusithi ibutho lamaKhaladiya selenyukile lisuka eJerusalema ngenxa yebutho likaFaro, z 
<b>12</b> uJeremiya waphuma eJerusalema ukuya elizweni lakoBhenjamini aa ukuthi amonyuke phakathi kwabantu. ab <b>13</b> Kwasekusithi esesesangweni lakoBhenjamini, ac kwakukhona lapho induna yabalindi obizo layo lalinguIrija, indodana kaShelemiya, ad indodana kaHananiya; ae yasimbamba uJeremiya umprofethi, isithi: Uhlubukela kumaKhaladiya. af <b>14</b> UJeremiya wasesithi: Ngamanga; kangihlubukeli kumaKhaladiya. Kodwa kamlalelanga; ngokunjalo uIriya wambamba uJeremiya, wamletha kuziphathamandla. ag 
<b>15</b> Iziphathamandla zasezithukuthela ngoJeremiya, zamtshaya, zamfaka entolongweni, ah endlini kaJonathani umbhali, ai ngoba zaziyenze yaba yintolongo. <b>16</b> UJeremiya esengene endlini yomgodi aj lezitokisini, uJeremiya esehlale khona insuku ezinengi, <b>17</b> inkosi uZedekhiya yasithuma, yamthatha; inkosi yasimbuza endlini yayo ensitha ak yathi: Likhona yini ilizwi elivela eNkosini? UJeremiya wasesithi: Likhona. Wathi: Uzanikelwa esandleni senkosi yeBhabhiloni. al 
<b>18</b> UJeremiya wathi futhi enkosini uZedekhiya: Ngoneni kuwe lezincekwini zakho lakulababantu ukuthi lingifake entolongweni? <b>19</b> Bangaphi khathesi abaprofethi benu abaprofetha kini am besithi: Inkosi yeBhabhiloni kayiyikufika imelane lani kumbe imelane lalelilizwe? <b>20</b> Khathesi-ke, ake uzwe, nkosi yami, nkosi, kakuthi ukuncenga kwami an kuwe phambi kwakho, ungangibuyiseli ao endlini kaJonathani umbhali, ap hlezi ngifele khona. 
<b>21</b> Ngakho inkosi uZedekhiya yalaya ukuthi bamfake uJeremiya egumeni lentolongo, aq lokuthi anikwe isinkwa sibe sinye ngosuku esivela esitaladini sabaphekizinkwa, ar size siphele sonke isinkwa emzini. as Ngokunjalo uJeremiya wahlala egumeni lentolongo. at 
<b>Jeremías 38</b><br><b>1</b> UShefathiya indodana kaMathani, loGedaliya indodana kaPashuri, loJukali indodana kaShelemiya, a loPashuri indodana kaMalikiya, b sebezwile amazwi uJeremiya ayewakhuluma kubo bonke abantu esithi: c <b>2</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Osala kulumuzi uzakufa ngenkemba, ngendlala, langomatshayabhuqe wesifo; d kodwa ophuma aye kumaKhaladiya uzaphila, e ngoba uzakuba lempilo yakhe ibe yimpango, aphile. <b>3</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Lumuzi uzanikelwa isibili esandleni sebutho lenkosi yeBhabhiloni, f elizawuthumba. g 
<b>4</b> Iziphathamandla zasezisithi enkosini: Ake abulawe lumuntu, h ngoba ngalindlela uyekethisa izandla zamadoda empi asele kulumuzi, i lezandla zabo bonke abantu, ngokukhuluma kibo amazwi anje; ngoba lumuntu kadingi ukuthula kwalababantu, kodwa okubi. <b>5</b> Inkosi uZedekhiya yasisithi: Khangela, usesandleni senu, ngoba inkosi kayilakho okuphambene lani ngalutho. j 
<b>6</b> Basebemthatha uJeremiya, bamphosela emgodini kaMalikiya indodana yenkosi, k owawusegumeni lentolongo, l bamehlisa uJeremiya ngamagoda; kwakungelamanzi emgodini, m kodwa udaka; uJeremiya wasetshona odakeni. n <b>7</b> Kwathi uEbedimeleki o umEthiyophiya p indoda umthenwa, q owayesendlini yenkosi, esizwa ukuthi uJeremiya bamfake emgodini (lapho inkosi yayihlezi esangweni lakoBhenjamini), r <b>8</b> uEbedimeleki s waphuma endlini yenkosi, wakhuluma lenkosi esithi: 
<b>9</b> Nkosi yami, nkosi, abantu laba benzile okubi ngakho konke abakwenze kuJeremiya umprofethi, abamphosele emgodini, ngoba uzakufa endaweni yakhe t ngenxa yendlala, ngoba kakusekho isinkwa emzini. u <b>10</b> Inkosi yasimlaya uEbedimeleki umEthiyophiya, v isithi: Thatha lapha amadoda angamatshumi amathathu ngaphansi kwesandla sakho, umenyule uJeremiya umprofethi emgodini engakafi. 
<b>11</b> Ngakho uEbedimeleki wathatha amadoda ngaphansi kwesandla sakhe, w wangena endlini yenkosi waze waba ngaphansi kwesiphala, wathatha khona izidwedwe ezidabukileyo lezidwedwe ezigugileyo, x wazehlisela kuJeremiya emgodini ngamagoda. <b>12</b> UEbedimeleki umEthiyophiya y wasesithi kuJeremiya: Ake ufake izidwedwe ezidabukileyo lezigugileyo ngaphansi kwamakhwapha akho ngaphansi kwamagoda. z UJeremiya wasesenza njalo. 
<b>13</b> Basebemdonsa uJeremiya ngamagoda, aa bamenyula emgodini. UJeremiya wasehlala egumeni lentolongo. UZedekhiya uyelulekwa nguJeremiya <b>14</b> Inkosi uZedekhiya yasithuma yathatha uJeremiya umprofethi yamusa kuye, ekungeneni kwesithathu okusendlini yeNkosi. Inkosi yasisithi kuJeremiya: Ngizakubuza udaba, ungangifihleli lutho. <b>15</b> UJeremiya wasesithi kuZedekhiya: Uba ngikutshela, kawuyikungibulala lokungibulala yini? Loba ngikweluleka, kawuyikungilalela. 
<b>16</b> Inkosi uZedekhiya yasifunga kuJeremiya ekusithekeni ab isithi: Kuphila kukaJehova ac owasenzela lumphefumulo: ad Isibili kangiyikukubulala, futhi isibili kangiyikukunikela esandleni salamadoda adinga umphefumulo wakho. ae <b>17</b> UJeremiya wasesithi kuZedekhiya: Itsho njalo iNkosi, uNkulunkulu wamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Uba uphuma isibili af uye kuziphathamandla zenkosi yeBhabhiloni, ag umphefumulo wakho uzaphila, ah lalumuzi kawuyikutshiswa ngomlilo; uzaphila wena lendlu yakho. 
<b>18</b> Kodwa uba ungaphumi ai uye kuziphathamandla zenkosi yeBhabhiloni, aj lumuzi uzanikelwa esandleni samaKhaladiya, ak awutshise ngomlilo; al lawe kawuyikuphunyuka esandleni sawo. am <b>19</b> Inkosi uZedekhiya yasisithi kuJeremiya: Ngiyawesaba amaJuda ahlubukele kumaKhaladiya, an hlezi anginikele esandleni sawo, angiklolodele. ao <b>20</b> UJeremiya wasesithi: Kawayikukunikela; ake ulalele ilizwi leNkosi njengalelo engilikhuluma kuwe, ap khona kukulungele, aq futhi umphefumulo wakho uzaphila. 
<b>21</b> Kodwa uba usala ukuphuma, ar yileli ilizwi iNkosi engibonise lona lokuthi: <b>22</b> Khangela-ke, bonke abesifazana abasele endlini yenkosi yakoJuda bazakhitshelwa kuziphathamandla zenkosi yeBhabhiloni, bathi: Abangane bakho bakukhuthazile, as bakwehlula; at inyawo zakho zitshone odakeni; au babuyele emuva. av <b>23</b> Njalo azakhuphela abafazi bonke bakho lamadodana akho kumaKhaladiya; aw lawe kawuyikuphunyuka esandleni sawo, ax kodwa uzabanjwa ngesandla senkosi yeBhabhiloni, lalumuzi uzawutshisa ngomlilo. ay 
<b>24</b> UZedekhiya wasesithi kuJeremiya: Kakungabi khona umuntu owaziyo lamazwi, khona ungayikufa. <b>25</b> Kodwa nxa iziphathamandla zisizwa ukuthi ngikhulume lawe, az zize kuwe zithi kuwe: Ake usitshele, ubusithini enkosini, ungakufihli kithi, khona kasiyikukubulala; njalo inkosi ikhulumeni kuwe; <b>26</b> uzakuthi-ke kuzo: Mina ngiwise ukuncenga kwami phambi kwenkosi ba ukuthi ingangibuyiseli endlini kaJonathani ukuze ngifele khona. bb 
<b>27</b> Zasezifika zonke iziphathamandla kuJeremiya, zambuza; yena waziphendula njengawo wonke lamazwi inkosi ewalayileyo. Zasezithula kuye, ngoba udaba lwalungezwakali. <b>28</b> UJeremiya wasehlala bc egumeni lentolongo kwaze kwaba lusuku iJerusalema eyathunjwa ngalo; bd wayekhona lapho iJerusalema ithunjwa. 
<b>Jeremías 39</b><br><b>1</b> Ngomnyaka wesificamunwemunye kaZedekhiya inkosi yakoJuda, a ngenyanga yetshumi, kwafika uNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni lebutho lakhe lonke, bamelana leJerusalema, bayivimbezela. <b>2</b> Ngomnyaka wetshumi lanye kaZedekhiya, ngenyanga yesine, ngolwesificamunwemunye lwenyanga, umuzi wafohlelwa. 
<b>3</b> Zonke iziphathamandla zenkosi yeBhabhiloni zasezingena, b zahlala esangweni eliphakathi, c oNerigali-Sharezi, uSamiganebo, uSarisekimi, uRabisarisi, d uNerigali-Sharezi, uRabimagi, e lazo zonke ezinye iziphathamandla zenkosi yeBhabhiloni. <b>4</b> Kwasekusithi lapho uZedekhiya inkosi yakoJuda f lawo wonke amadoda empi bezibona, babaleka, baphuma emzini ebusuku ngendlela yesivande senkosi, ngesango eliphakathi kwemiduli emibili; waphuma ngendlela yamagceke. 
<b>5</b> Kodwa ibutho lamaKhaladiya laxotshana labo, lamfica uZedekhiya emagcekeni eJeriko; selimbambile lamenyusela kuNebhukadinezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni eRibila, elizweni leHamathi, g owakhuluma izigwebo ezimelene laye. h <b>6</b> Inkosi yeBhabhiloni yasibulala amadodana kaZedekhiya eRibila emehlweni akhe; i inkosi yeBhabhiloni yabuya yabulala zonke izikhulu zakoJuda. j <b>7</b> Yasiphumputhekisa amehlo kaZedekhiya, yambopha ngamaketane ethusi amabili ukuze imuse eBhabhiloni. k 
<b>8</b> AmaKhaladiya asetshisa indlu yenkosi lezindlu zabantu ngomlilo, l adilizela phansi imiduli yeJerusalema. <b>9</b> Lensali yabantu eyayisele emzini, lalabo ababehlubukile, m ababehlubukile kuyo, lensali yabantu eyayisele, uNebuzaradani induna yabalindi yabathumba yabasa eBhabhiloni. <b>10</b> Kodwa uNebuzaradani induna yabalindi watshiya elizweni lakoJuda ebantwini ababengabayanga n ababengelalutho, wabanika izivini lamasimu ngalolosuku. 
<b>11</b> UNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni waselaya ngesandla sikaNebuzaradani induna yabalindi ngoJeremiya esithi: <b>12</b> Mthathe, umise amehlo akho kuye, ungamenzeli ulutho olubi, kodwa njengokukhuluma kwakhe kuwe wenze njalo ngaye. <b>13</b> Ngakho bathuma oNebuzaradani induna yabalindi, loNebushazibani, uRabisarisi, loNerigali-Sharezi, o uRabimagi, lazo zonke induna zenkosi yeBhabhiloni, p 
<b>14</b> bathuma, bamthatha uJeremiya egumeni lentolongo, q bamnikela r kuGedaliya, s indodana kaAhikhamu, indodana kaShafani, t ukuze amkhuphe amuse endlini yakhe. Wasehlala phakathi kwabantu. u UEbedimeleki uzakhululwa yiNkosi <b>15</b> Ilizwi leNkosi laselifika kuJeremiya esavalelwe egumeni lentolongo, v , w lisithi: 
<b>16</b> Hamba ukhulume kuEbedimeleki umEthiyophiya x uthi: Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Khangela, ngizakwehlisela amazwi ami kulumuzi ngokubi y kungeyisikho ngokuhle, z njalo azakuba phambi kwakho ngalolosuku. <b>17</b> Kodwa ngizakophula ngalolosuku, itsho iNkosi, njalo kawuyikunikelwa esandleni sabantu obesabayo. aa <b>18</b> Ngoba ngizakukhulula isibili, kawuyikuwa-ke ngenkemba, kodwa impilo yakho izakuba yimpango kuwe, ab ngoba uthembele kimi, ac itsho iNkosi. 
<b>Jeremías 40</b><br><b>1</b> Ilizwi elafika kuJeremiya livela eNkosini emva kokuthi uNebuzaradani induna yabalindi esemyekele wahamba a esuka eRama, b esemthethe ebotshiwe ngamaketane c phakathi kwabo bonke ababethunjwe eJerusalema lakoJuda, abasiwa ekuthunjweni eBhabhiloni. d <b>2</b> Induna yabalindi yasimthatha uJeremiya yathi kuye: e INkosi, uNkulunkulu wakho, yakhuluma lokhu okubi ngalindawo. f <b>3</b> INkosi isikulethile yakwenza njengokutsho kwayo, ngoba lonile eNkosini, g , h kalilalelanga ilizwi layo; ngakho loludaba lwenzakele kini. 
<b>4</b> Khathesi-ke, khangela, lamuhla ngiyakukhulula kulamaketane asezandleni zakho. i Uba kukuhle emehlweni akho ukuya lami eBhabhiloni, woza, ngimise ilihlo lami phezu kwakho; kodwa uba kukubi emehlweni akho ukuya lami eBhabhiloni, yekela. Bona, ilizwe lonke liphambi kwakho; j lapho kukuhle kuqondile emehlweni akho ukuhamba, hamba uye khona. k 
<b>5</b> Kodwa engakabuyeli, wathi: Buyela l kuGedaliya, indodana kaAhikhamu, indodana kaShafani, m emmisileyo inkosi yeBhabhiloni abe phezu kwemizi yakoJuda, n uhlale laye phakathi kwabantu; o kumbe loba kungaphi lapho kuqondile emehlweni akho ukuya khona, hamba. Ngakho induna yabalindi yasimnika umphako p lesipho, q yamyekela ukuthi ahambe. <b>6</b> Ngakho uJeremiya wafika kuGedaliya indodana kaAhikhamu eMizipa, r wahlala laye phakathi kwabantu ababesele elizweni. s 
<b>7</b> Kwathi zonke induna zamabutho ezazisegangeni, zona kanye lamadoda azo, t besizwa ukuthi inkosi yeBhabhiloni ibekile uGedaliya indodana kaAhikhamu abe phezu kwelizwe, u lokuthi inikele kuye amadodana labesifazana labantwana, lakwabangabayanga belizwe, v kulabo ababengathunjelwanga eBhabhiloni, <b>8</b> khona beza kuGedaliya eMizipa, w oIshmayeli, indodana kaNethaniya, x loJohanani, loJonathani, y amadodana kaKareya, z loSeraya, indodana kaTanihumethi, lamadodana kaEfayi, umNetofa, loJezaniya, indodana yomMahakathi, aa bona lamadoda abo. 
<b>9</b> UGedaliya, indodana kaAhikhamu, indodana kaShafani, wasefunga kibo lamadoda abo, esithi: Lingesabi ukusebenzela amaKhaladiya; hlalani elizweni, liyisebenzele inkosi yeBhabhiloni; khona kuzalilungela. ab <b>10</b> Khangelani-ke, mina ngihlala eMizipa ac ukuma phambi kobuso bamaKhaladiya azafika kithi; kodwa lina buthani iwayini lezithelo zehlobo lamafutha, likufake ezitsheni zenu, lihlale emizini yenu eliyithetheyo. 
<b>11</b> Futhi, lapho wonke amaJuda ad ayekoMowabi ae laphakathi kwabantwana bakoAmoni af leEdoma ag lawayekuwo wonke amazwe esezwile ukuthi inkosi yeBhabhiloni ah itshiyile insali yakoJuda, ai lokuthi yabeka phezu kwabo uGedaliya, indodana kaAhikhamu, indodana kaShafani; <b>12</b> wonke amaJuda asebuya esuka endaweni zonke ayexotshelwe khona, aj eza elizweni lakoJuda, kuGedaliya eMizipa; abutha iwayini lezithelo zehlobo, kwaba kunengi kakhulu. ak Ukubulawa kukaGedaliya 
<b>13</b> UJohanani indodana kaKareya al lazo zonke induna zamabutho ayesegangeni am basebesiza kuGedaliya eMizipa, <b>14</b> bathi kuye: Uyakwazi isibili yini ukuthi uBhahalisi an inkosi yabantwana bakoAmoni ao uthume uIshmayeli indodana kaNethaniya ap ukukutshaya empilweni? Kodwa uGedaliya indodana kaAhikhamu kabakholwanga. 
<b>15</b> UJohanani indodana kaKareya wasekhuluma loGedaliya ensitha eMizipa esithi: Ake ungiyekele ngihambe ngiyetshaya uIshmayeli indodana kaNethaniya, kungabi lamuntu okwaziyo. Uzakutshayelani empilweni, ukuze bahlakazeke bonke abakoJuda ababuthene kuwe, kubhubhe insali yakoJuda? <b>16</b> Kodwa uGedaliya indodana kaAhikhamu wathi kuJohanani indodana kaKareya: Ungayenzi le indaba, ngoba uqamba amanga ngoIshmayeli. 
<b>Jeremías 41</b><br><b>1</b> Kwasekusithi ngenyanga yesikhombisa a kwafika b uIshmayeli, indodana kaNethaniya, c indodana kaElishama, d owenzalo yesikhosini, lezinduna zenkosi, lamadoda alitshumi laye, kuGedaliya indodana kaAhikhamu eMizipa, e badla isinkwa f khona ndawonye eMizipa. <b>2</b> UIshmayeli indodana kaNethaniya wasesukuma, g lamadoda alitshumi ayelaye, bamtshaya uGedaliya indodana kaAhikhamu indodana kaShafani ngenkemba, bambulala yena inkosi yeBhabhiloni eyayimbeke phezu kwelizwe. h 
<b>3</b> UIshmayeli wabulala lawo wonke amaJuda ayelaye, i eloGedaliya, eMizipa, lamaKhaladiya atholakala khona, j amadoda empi. <b>4</b> Kwasekusithi ngosuku lwesibili esembulele uGedaliya, kungelamuntu okwaziyo, <b>5</b> kwafika amadoda evela eShekema, k eShilo, l leSamariya, m amadoda angamatshumi ayisificaminwembili, ephucile indevu, n lezigqoko zidatshuliwe, o ezisikile, p elomnikelo wokudla lempepha esandleni sawo, q ukukuletha endlini yeNkosi. r 
<b>6</b> UIshmayeli indodana kaNethaniya s wasephuma eMizipa t ukuwahlangabeza, ehamba, ehamba ekhala inyembezi. Kwasekuthi lapho ehlangana lawo, wathi kuwo: Wozani kuGedaliya indodana kaAhikhamu. <b>7</b> Kwasekusithi sebefikile phakathi komuzi, uIshmayeli indodana kaNethaniya wawabulala wawaphosela phakathi u komgodi, v yena lamadoda ayelaye. 
<b>8</b> Kodwa kwatholakala amadoda alitshumi phakathi kwawo, athi kuIshmayeli: Ungasibulali, ngoba sileziphala ensimini, w ingqoloyi lebhali* lamafutha loluju. Ngakho wawayekela, kaze awabulala phakathi kwabafowabo. <b>9</b> Njalo umgodi uIshmayeli ayephosele kuwo zonke izidumbu zabantu ayebatshayile x kanye loGedaliya, wawuyiwo inkosi uAsa eyawenzayo ngenxa kaBahasha inkosi yakoIsrayeli; y lowo uIshmayeli indodana kaNethaniya wawugcwalisa ngababuleweyo. 
<b>10</b> UIshmayeli wasethumba yonke insali yabantu ababeseMizipa, amadodakazi enkosi, z labo bonke abantu ababesele eMizipa, uNebuzaradani induna yabalindi eyayibamisele uGedaliya indodana kaAhikhamu. aa UIshmayeli indodana kaNethaniya wasebathumba, wahamba ukuthi achaphele kubantwana bakoAmoni. ab <b>11</b> Kwathi uJohanani indodana kaKareya ac lazo zonke induna zamabutho ezazilaye ad besizwa konke okubi uIshmayeli indodana kaNethaniya ayekwenzile, 
<b>12</b> bathatha wonke amadoda, bayakulwa loIshmayeli indodana kaNethaniya; bamfica emanzini amanengi ae aseGibeyoni. af <b>13</b> Kwasekusithi bonke abantu ababeloIshmayeli bebona uJohanani indodana kaKareya lazo zonke induna zamabutho ezazilaye, ag bathokoza. <b>14</b> Basebetshibilika abantu bonke uIshmayeli ayebathumbe besuka eMizipa, baphenduka baya kuJohanani indodana kaKareya. ah <b>15</b> Kodwa uIshmayeli indodana kaNethaniya waphunyuka wasuka phambi kukaJohanani, lamadoda ayisificaminwembili, waya ebantwaneni bakoAmoni. ai 
<b>16</b> UJohanani indodana kaKareya aj lazo zonke induna zamabutho ezazilaye ak basebethatha yonke insali yabantu al ayebabuyisile besuka kuIshmayeli indodana kaNethaniya am eMizipa, esetshaye uGedaliya indodana kaAhikhamu, amadoda, amadoda empi, an labafazi, ao labantwana, labathenwa, ayebabuyisile bevela eGibeyoni. ap <b>17</b> Basebesuka bayahlala eGeruthi-Kimihamu aq eseduze leBhethelehema, ar ukuyangena eGibhithe as

<b>Jeremías 42</b><br><b>1</b> Zasezisondela zonke induna zamabutho, a loJohanani indodana kaKareya, b loJezaniya c indodana kaHoshaya, d labo bonke abantu, kusukela komncinyane kuze kube komkhulu, e <b>2</b> bathi kuJeremiya umprofethi: Ake kuthi ukuncenga kwethu kuwe phambi kwakho, usikhulekele eNkosini uNkulunkulu wakho, f umele le insali yonke, g ngoba sisele sibalutshwana kwabanengi, h njengalokho amehlo akho esibona; <b>3</b> ukuze iNkosi, uNkulunkulu wakho, isazise indlela esizahamba ngayo, i lento esizayenza. 
<b>4</b> UJeremiya umprofethi wasesithi kibo: Sengizwile; khangelani, ngizakhuleka eNkosini uNkulunkulu wenu njengokwamazwi enu; kuzakuthi-ke, lonke ilizwi iNkosi ezaliphendula ngizalitshela lona, j kangiyikuligodlela lizwi. k <b>5</b> Bona basebesithi kuJeremiya: INkosi kayibe ngumfakazi oqinisileyo l lothembekileyo m phakathi kwethu, uba singenzi ngokunjalo n njengalo lonke ilizwi iNkosi uNkulunkulu wakho ekuthuma kithi ngalo. o

<b>Jeremías 43</b><br><b>1</b> Kwasekusithi lapho uJeremiya eseqedile ukukhuluma kubo bonke abantu wonke amazwi eNkosi uNkulunkulu wabo, iNkosi uNkulunkulu wabo eyayimthume ngawo kibo, ngitsho wonke lamazwi; <b>2</b> wasekhuluma uAzariya indodana kaHoshaya, a loJohanani indodana kaKareya, b labo bonke abantu abazikhukhumezayo, c besithi kuJeremiya: Uqamba amanga. <b>11</b> Yenyukela eGileyadi, uyethatha ag ibhalisamu* ah, ntombi emsulwa, ai ndodakazi yeGibhithe; imithi uyandisela ize, kakulakwelatshwa kuwe. aj <b>12</b> Izizwe zizwile ihlazo lakho, lomhlaba ugcwele ukukhala kwakho; ak ngoba akhubekile, iqhawe limelene leqhawe, al womabili awele phansi ndawonye. am <b>13</b> Ilizwi iNkosi eyalikhuluma kuJeremiya umprofethi ngokuza kukaNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni ukutshaya ilizwe leGibhithe: an

<b>17</b> Bamemeza khona, bathi: UFaro inkosi yeGibhithe ungumsindo; az udlulise isikhathi esimisiweyo. ba <b>18</b> Kuphila kwami, bb itsho iNkosi, bc obizo layo yiNkosi yamabandla: bd , be Isibili njengeThabhori phakathi kwezintaba bf lanjengeKharmeli ngaselwandle bg uzafika. <b>19</b> Zenzele izikhali zokuthunjwa, bh ndodakazi ehlala eGibhithe; bi ngoba iNofi bj izakuba yincithakalo, bk itshiswe, kungabi lomhlali. bl <b>20</b> IGibhithe ilithokazi bm elihle kakhulu; isibawu siyeza, sivela enyakatho. bn 
<b>21</b> Labaqhatshiweyo bayo phakathi kwayo banjengethole lesibaya; bo ngoba labo baphendukile, bp babaleka kanyekanye; kabemanga, ngoba usuku lokuhlupheka kwabo lubehlele, bq , br isikhathi bs sempindiselo yabo. bt <b>22</b> Ilizwi layo lizahamba bu njengenyoka; bv ngoba bazahamba lebutho, beze kuyo belamahloka, njengabagamuli bezihlahla. bw <b>23</b> Bagamule ihlathi layo, bx itsho iNkosi, lanxa lingephenywe, by ngoba banengi okwedlula isikhonyane, bz kabalakubalwa. 
<b>24</b> Indodakazi yamaGibhithe izayangeka, izanikelwa esandleni sabantu benyakatho. ca <b>25</b> INkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli, ithi: Khangela, ngizajezisa uAmoni cb weNo, cc loFaro, leGibhithe, labonkulunkulu bayo, cd lamakhosi ayo, yebo uFaro labathembela kuye. <b>26</b> Ngibanikele esandleni salabo abadinga impilo yabo, ce lesandleni sikaNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni, lesandleni senceku zakhe; lemva kwalokho ihlalwe cf njengensukwini zendulo, cg itsho iNkosi. 
<b>27</b> Kodwa wena, ungesabi, ch nceku yami Jakobe, ungatshaywa luvalo, Israyeli, ngoba khangela, ngizakusindisa usekhatshana, lenzalo yakho elizweni lokuthunjwa kwayo; uJakobe uzaphenduka-ke, athule, onwabe, njalo kakho ozamethusa. <b>28</b> Wena, ungesabi, nceku yami Jakobe, itsho iNkosi, ngoba ngilawe; ci ngoba ngizaqeda ngokupheleleyo zonke izizwe engikuxotshele kuzo, kodwa kangiyikukuqeda ngokupheleleyo, ngikukhuze ngokufaneleyo, kodwa kangiyikukuyekela lokukuyekela ungajeziswanga. 
<b>Jeremías 47</b><br><b>1</b> Ilizwi leNkosi elafika kuJeremiya umprofethi limelene lamaFilisti, a uFaro engakatshayi iGaza. b <b>2</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Khangela, amanzi azakhukhumala c evela enyakatho, d azakuba yisifula esikhukhulayo, akhukhule ilizwe lokugcwala kwalo, umuzi labahlala kiwo; labantu bazakhala, labo bonke abakhileyo belizwe bazaqhinqa isililo, <b>3</b> ngomsindo wokugidiza kwamasondo amabhiza akhe alamandla, e ngomdumo wenqola zakhe, f inhlokomo yamavili akhe; oyise kabanyemukuli kubantwana g ngenxa yokuyekethiswa kwezandla; 
<b>4</b> ngenxa yosuku oluzayo ukuchitha wonke amaFilisti, h ukuquma asuke eTire leSidoni i , j wonke umsizi oseleyo; ngoba iNkosi izawachitha amaFilisti, insali k yesihlenge seKafitori. l <b>5</b> Ubumpabanga sebufikile eGaza, m iAshikeloni n iqunyiwe, insali yesihotsha sabo; o uzazisika p kuze kube nini? <b>6</b> Hawu, nkemba yeNkosi; q koze kube nini ungathuli? <b>Jeremías 48</b><br><b>1</b> Imelene loMowabi, a itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Maye ngeNebo, b ngoba ichithiwe; iKiriyathayimi c iyangekile, ithunjiwe; indawo ephakemeyo iyangekile, idilikile. d <b>2</b> Kakusayikuba khona udumo lukaMowabi eHeshiboni e; baceba okubi bemelene layo, besithi: Wozani siyiqume ekubeni yisizwe; f lawe Madimeni, g uzathula, inkemba izakulandela. <b>3</b> Ilizwi lokukhala livela h eHoronayimi, i incithakalo lokwephuka okukhulu! <b>4</b> UMowabi wephukile; abancinyane bakhe benze ukukhala kuzwakale. 
<b>5</b> Ngoba ekwenyukeni kweLubithi ukukhala kuzakwenyuka lokukhala, j ngoba ekwehleni k kweHoronayimi l izitha zizwile ukukhala kokuchitheka. m <b>6</b> Balekani, n likhulule impilo yenu, libe njengesihlahla esiyize enkangala. <b>7</b> Ngoba ngenxa yokuthembela kwakho emisebenzini yakho lemagugwini akho o lawe uzathunjwa; loKemoshi p uzaphumela ekuthunjweni, abapristi bakhe lezikhulu zakhe kanyekanye. q <b>8</b> Lomchithi uzafikela wonke umuzi, r njalo kakulamuzi ozaphunyuka; isihotsha laso sizachitheka, legceke s liqothulwe, t njengokutsho kweNkosi. 
<b>9</b> Nikani uMowabi amaphiko, u ngoba uzaphapha aphume; lemizi yakhe izakuba lunxiwa, v engelamhlali kuyo. w <b>10</b> Uqalekisiwe lowo owenza x umsebenzi weNkosi y ngokunganakekeli; z uqalekisiwe lalowo ogodla inkemba yakhe egazini. <b>11</b> UMowabi wonwabile kusukela ebutsheni bakhe, aa uphumule enzeceni zakhe, ab kathululelwanga esuka embizeni esiya embizeni, kahambanga ekuthunjweni; ngakho-ke ukunambitheka kwakhe kumi kuye, lephunga lakhe kaliphenduki. 
<b>12</b> Ngakho, khangela, insuku ziyeza, itsho iNkosi, lapho ngizathuma kuye abathambeki ac abazamthambeka, ad bathulule imbiza zakhe, babulale ae izinqayi zabo. af <b>13</b> Khona uMowabi uzakuba lenhloni ngoKemoshi, njengalokho indlu kaIsrayeli yaba lenhloni ag ngeBhetheli, ithemba layo. ah <b>14</b> Lingatsho njani ukuthi: Singamaqhawe lamadoda empi alamandla? <b>15</b> UMowabi uchithiwe, ai wenyuka emizini yakhe, lekhethelo lamajaha akhe selehlele ekubulaweni, aj itsho iNkosi, obizo layo nguJehova wamabandla. ak 
<b>16</b> Inkathazo kaMowabi iseduze ukufika, lobubi bakhe buyaphangisa kakhulu. <b>17</b> Mlileleni, lina lonke elimzingelezeleyo, lani lonke elazi ibizo lakhe; lithi: Yephuke njani al induku elamandla, am udondolo oluhle! an <b>18</b> Yehla ebukhosini, uhlale ao ekomeni, ap wena ndodakazi ohlala aq eDiboni; ar ngoba umchithi kaMowabi usenyukele ukumelana lawe, as wachitha inqaba zakho. <b>19</b> Mana endleleni, ulinde, at mhlalikazi weAroweri, au ubuze obalekayo lowesifazana ophunyukileyo, uthi: Kwenzakaleni? 
<b>20</b> UMowabi uyangekile, ngoba udilikile; av qhinqani isililo likhale; aw libike eArinoni ax ukuthi uMowabi uchithiwe. ay <b>21</b> Lesahlulelo sehlele phezu kwelizwe elilamagceke, az phezu kweHoloni, laphezu kweJahaza, ba , bb laphezu kweMefahathi, bc <b>22</b> laphezu kweDiboni, bd laphezu kweNebo, be laphezu kweBeti-Dibilathayimi, bf <b>23</b> laphezu kweKiriyathayimi, bg laphezu kweBeti-Gamuli, laphezu kweBeti bh-Meyoni, bi 
<b>24</b> laphezu kweKeriyothi, bj laphezu kweBhozira, bk laphezu kwayo yonke imizi yelizwe lakoMowabi, ekhatshana leseduze. <b>25</b> Uphondo lukaMowabi luqunyiwe, bl lengalo yakhe yephukile, bm itsho iNkosi. <b>26</b> Mdakiseni, bn ngoba ezikhulisile emelene leNkosi; bo uMowabi uzazibhixa emahlanzweni akhe, bp abe yinhlekisa, bq ngitsho yena. <b>27</b> Ngoba uIsrayeli kabanga yinhlekisa yini kuwe? br Watholakala yini phakathi kwamasela? bs Ngoba kusukela ngesikhathi samazwi akho ngaye wanyikinya ikhanda. 
<b>28</b> Tshiyani imizi, bt lihlale bu edwaleni, bv bahlali beMowabi, libe njengejuba bw , bx elakha isidleke emaceleni omlomo womgodi. by , bz <b>29</b> Sizwile ngokuzigqaja kukaMowabi ca (uyazigqaja kakhulu), ukuziphakamisa kwakhe, lokuzigqaja kwakhe, lokuzikhukhumeza kwakhe, lokuphakama kwenhliziyo yakhe. <b>30</b> Mina ngiyayazi, itsho iNkosi, intukuthelo yakhe, cb kodwa kakuyikuba njalo; ukusoma kwakhe kakungenzi njalo. cc , cd 
<b>31</b> Ngalokho-ke ngizaqhinqa isililo ngoMowabi, ce yebo, ngikhale ngaye wonke uMowabi; cf bazabubula cg , ch ngabantu beKiri-Heresi. ci <b>32</b> Ngokukhala inyembezi kweJazeri cj ngizakukhalela inyembezi, wena vini ck leSibima. cl Ingatsha zakho zedlula ulwandle, cm zafinyelela elwandle lweJazeri; cn umchithi wehlele phezu kwezithelo zakho zehlobo lemavinini akho avuniweyo. 
<b>33</b> Lokuthaba lentokozo kususiwe esivandeni co , cp lelizweni lakoMowabi; ngiqedile lewayini ezikhamelweni; cq kabayikunyathela ngomsindo wentokozo; cr umsindo wentokozo kawuyikuba ngumsindo wentokozo. cs <b>34</b> Ngenxa yokukhala kweHeshiboni, kuze kube seEleyale, ct kuze kube seJahazi, cu bakhupha ilizwi labo, kusukela eZowari kuze kufike eJoronayimi, cv ithokazi leminyaka emithathu; cw ngoba lamanzi eNimirimi azakuba yincithakalo. cx 
<b>35</b> Njalo ngizaqeda koMowabi, itsho iNkosi, lowo onikela endaweni ephakemeyo, cy lotshisela onkulunkulu bakhe impepha. <b>36</b> Ngenxa yalokho inhliziyo yami cz izakhalela uMowabi njengemihubhe, da lenhliziyo yami izakhala njengemihubhe ngabantu beKiri-Heresi; db ngoba inotho ababeyizuzile ichithekile. dc <b>37</b> Ngoba lonke ikhanda dd liyimpabanga, de lazo zonke indevu zigeliwe; df izandla zonke zisikiwe, dg lokhalweni dh kulesaka. di 
<b>38</b> Phezu kwazo zonke impahla zezindlu zakoMowabi dj lezitaladeni zakhe dk yonke indawo kulokuqhinqa isililo, ngoba ngimphahlazile uMowabi njengembiza engathokozelwayo, dl itsho iNkosi. <b>39</b> Bazaqhinqa isililo besithi: Udilike njani!
