<b>45</b> Emthunzini ee weHeshiboni ef ababalekayo bayema bengelamandla, ngoba umlilo uphumile eHeshiboni, eg lelangabi livela phakathi eh kukaSihoni, ei njalo kudlile ingonsi yakoMowabi, ej lenkanda yabantwana besiphithiphithi. <b>46</b> Maye kuwe, Mowabi! ek Abantu bakaKemoshi baphelile, ngoba amadodana akho athethwe asiwa ekuthunjweni, lamadodakazi akho ekuthunjweni. <b>47</b> Kodwa ngizabuyisa ukuthunjwa kukaMowabi el , em , en ekucineni kwezinsuku, eo , ep itsho iNkosi. <b>Jeremías 49</b><br><b>1</b> Mayelana labantwana bakoAmoni a itsho njalo iNkosi: UIsrayeli kalamadodana yini? Kalayo indlalifa yini? Kungani uMilkomu esidla ilifa lakoGadi, b labantu bakhe behlala emizini yayo? <b>2</b> Ngakho khangela, insuku ziyeza, itsho iNkosi, lapho ngizakwenza ukuthi kuzwakale umkhosi wempi c eRaba yabantwana bakoAmoni; d njalo izakuba yinqumbi yencithakalo, e lamadodakazi ayo atshiswe ngomlilo; f loIsrayeli adle ilifa labadla ilifa lakhe, g itsho iNkosi. 
<b>3</b> Qhinqa isililo, Heshiboni, h ngoba iAyi ichithiwe; i khalani, madodakazi eRaba, j libhince amasaka, k lilile, liye le lale ezintangweni; l ngoba uMilkomu uzahamba ekuthunjweni, abapristi bakhe leziphathamandla zakhe ndawonye. m <b>4</b> Uzincomelani ngezihotsha? Isihotsha sakho siyageleza, ndodakazi ehlehlela nyovane, n ethemba amagugu ayo o, usithi: Ngubani ozakuza kimi? p , q 
<b>5</b> Khangela, ngizakwehlisela phezu kwakho uvalo, r itsho iNkosi, uJehova wamabandla, luvela kubo bonke abakuhanqileyo; njalo lizaxotshwa, ngulowo lalowo phambi kwakhe; njalo kakho ozabutha ozulayo. s <b>6</b> Kodwa emva kwalokho ngizabuyisa ukuthunjwa kwabantwana bakoAmoni, t itsho iNkosi. Ilizwi ngeEdoma <b>7</b> Mayelana leEdoma, u itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla: Kakuselanhlakanipho yini v eThemani? w Icebo liphelile yini kwabaqedisisayo? x Inhlakanipho yabo isinyamalele yini? 
<b>8</b> Balekani, y buyelani emuva, hlalani endaweni ejulileyo, z bahlali beDedani; aa ngoba ngizakwehlisela inhlupheko kaEsawu phezu kwakhe, isikhathi sokumhambela kwami. ab <b>9</b> Uba abavuni bamavini befika kuwe ac bebengayikutshiya yini umkhothozo? ad Uba amasela efika ebusuku, ae azaphanga aze anele? <b>10</b> Kodwa mina ngimhlubule uEsawu, af ngizembule indawo zakhe zensitha, ukuze angabi lakho ukucatsha; inzalo yakhe ichithiwe, labafowabo, labomakhelwane bakhe, yena kasekho. ag 
<b>11</b> Tshiya izintandane zakho, mina ngizazilondoloza ziphila, ah labafelokazi bakho kabathembele kimi. <b>12</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi: Khangela, labo okugwetshwa kwabo kwakungeyisikho ukunatha inkezo bazayinatha lokuyinatha, ai wena-ke nguwe yini ozayekelwa ungajeziswanga ngokupheleleyo? aj Kawuyikuyekelwa ungelacala, kodwa isibili uzanatha. <b>13</b> Ngoba ngifungile ngami uqobo, ak itsho iNkosi, ukuthi iBhozira al izakuba ngokwesabekayo, am ibe lihlazo, an ibe lunxiwa, ibe yisiqalekiso; ao lemizi yayo yonke izakuba ngamanxiwa aphakade. ap 
<b>14</b> Ngizwile umbiko ovela eNkosini, aq , ar lesithunywa sithunywe phakathi kwezizwe, sisithi: Buthanani, as lize limelane layo, lisukumele impi. <b>15</b> Ngoba khangela, ngizakwenza ube mncinyane phakathi kwezizwe, udeleleke phakathi kwabantu. <b>16</b> Ukwesabeka kwakho kukukhohlisile, ukuziqhenya kwenhliziyo yakho, wena ohlala ezingoxweni zamadwala, at obamba izingqonga zoqaqa. Loba usenza au isidleke sakho sibe phezulu njengokhozi, av ngizakwehlisa usuka khona, aw itsho iNkosi. 
<b>17</b> IEdoma layo izakuba yinto eyesabekayo; ax , ay wonke odlula kuyo uzamangala kakhulu, az ancife ngenxa yenhlupheko yonke yayo. ba , bb , bc <b>18</b> Njengokuchithwa kweSodoma leGomora, bd , be labomakhelwane bayo, bf , bg itsho iNkosi; kakulamuntu ozahlala khona, bh , bi kakulandodana yomuntu ezahlala khona njengowezizwe. bj 
<b>19</b> Khangela, njengesilwane bk esivela ekukhukhumaleni kweJordani bl uzakwenyuka bm emelene lendawo yokuhlala okungapheliyo, kodwa ngizahle ngimgijimise asuke kuyo; njalo ngubani okhethiweyo engingammisa emelene layo? Ngoba ngubani onjengami? bn Ngubani-ke ozangibizela ukulandisa? Njalo nguwuphi lowomelusi bo ongema phambi kwami? bp 
<b>20</b> Ngakho, zwanini icebo leNkosi elicebe imelene leEdoma, lemicabango yayo eyicabange emelene labahlali beThemani: bq Isibili abancinyane bomhlambi bazabadonsa; isibili izachitha indawo yabo yokuhlala phezu kwabo. <b>21</b> Ngomsindo wokuwa kwabo umhlaba uyazamazama; br ukukhala, umsindo wakho wezwakala eLwandle oluBomvu. <b>22</b> Khangela, uzakwenyuka bs andize ngesiqubu njengokhozi, bt elulele impiko zakhe phezu bu kweBhozira; bv lenhliziyo yamaqhawe eEdoma bw ngalolosuku izakuba njengenhliziyo yowesifazana ohelelwayo. bx Ilizwi ngeDamaseko 
<b>23</b> Mayelana leDamaseko. by , bz IHamathi ca leArpadi cb kuyangekile, ngoba bazwile umbiko omubi; bancibilika, kulokunqineka elwandle, kungeke kube lokuthula. cc <b>24</b> IDamaseko cd isibuthakathaka, ce itshibilike ukuze ibaleke, cf lokwethuka kuyibambile, ukucindezelwa losizi kuyibambile cg njengobelethayo. ch <b>25</b> Kawutshiywanga njani ci umuzi wodumo, cj umuzi wentokozo yami! ck <b>26</b> Ngakho amajaha awo azawela ezitaladeni zawo, cl lawo wonke amadoda empi cm azabhujiswa ngalolosuku, cn itsho iNkosi yamabandla. 
<b>27</b> Ngizaphemba-ke umlilo emdulini co weDamaseko, cp ozaqothula izigodlo cq zikaBenihadadi. cr Ilizwi ngeKedari leHazori <b>28</b> Mayelana leKedari cs njalo mayelana lemibuso yeHazori owayitshayayo uNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni: Itsho njalo iNkosi: Sukumani, ct lenyukele eKedari, cu lichithe amadodana empumalanga. cv <b>29</b> Bazathatha amathente abo lemihlambi yabo, cw , cx amakhetheni abo cy lempahla yabo yonke lamakamela abo cz bazazithwalela khona; bamemeze kibo bathi: Uvalo enhlangothini zonke. da 
<b>30</b> Balekani, lizulazule kakhulu, db lihlale ngokujula, dc lina bahlali beHazori, itsho iNkosi; ngoba uNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni waceba icebo emelene lani, wacabanga umcabango emelene lani. <b>31</b> Sukumani, lenyuke limelene lesizwe esonwabileyo, dd esihlala ekuvikelekeni, de itsho iNkosi, esingelamasango, esingelamigoqo, df esizihlalele sodwa. dg 
<b>32</b> Lamakamela abo azakuba yimpango, lemihlambi yezifuyo zabo izakuba yimpango; njalo ngizabachithachitha kuyo yonke imimoya, abaphoselwe engonsini, dh ngilethe inkathazo yabo evela inhlangothi zonke zaso, itsho iNkosi. <b>33</b> LeHazori izakuba yindawo yokuhlala yamakhanka, di unxiwa kuze kube nininini; dj kakuyikuhlala muntu khona, dk lendodana yomuntu kayiyikuhlala kuyo njengowezizwe. dl Ilizwi ngeElamu <b>34</b> Ilizwi leNkosi elafika kuJeremiya umprofethi limelene leElamu ekuqaleni kokubusa kukaZedekhiya, inkosi yakoJuda, dm lisithi: 
<b>35</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla: Khangela, ngizakwephula idandili leElamu, dn induna yamandla abo. <b>36</b> Ngilethele iElamu imimoya emine evela emikhawulweni emine yamazulu, ngibahlakazele kuyo yonke leyomimoya; do njalo kakuyikuba khona isizwe lapho abaxotshiweyo beElamu abangayikufika khona. dp <b>37</b> Ngoba ngizakwenza abeElamu besabe phambi kwezitha zabo dq laphambi kwalabo abadinga impilo yabo, ngibehlisele okubi, ukuvutha kolaka lwami, dr itsho iNkosi, ngithume inkemba emva kwabo, ds ngize ngibaqede. 
<b>38</b> Ngizamisa-ke isihlalo sami sobukhosi eElamu, dt ngichithe kuyo inkosi leziphathamandla, itsho iNkosi. <b>39</b> Kodwa kuzakuthi ekucineni kwezinsuku du , dv ngibuyise ukuthunjwa kukaElamu, dw itsho iNkosi. 
<b>Jeremías 50</b><br><b>1</b> Ilizwi iNkosi eyalikhuluma imelene leBhabhiloni, imelene lelizwe lamaKhaladiya, ngesandla sikaJeremiya umprofethi, a lisithi: <b>2</b> Memezani phakathi kwezizwe, lizwakalise; misani uphawu, b lizwakalise, lingakufihli, lithi: IBhabhiloni ithunjiwe, c uBheli uyangekile, d uMerodaki ubhidliziwe; e izithombe zayo ziyangisiwe, f imifanekiso yayo ibhidliziwe. 
<b>3</b> Ngoba isizwe senyuka simelene layo esivela enyakatho, g esizakwenza ilizwe layo libe lunxiwa, h ukuze kungabi lomhlali kulo; i kusukela emuntwini kusiya enyamazaneni kuyabaleka kuhambile. j <b>4</b> Ngalezonsuku langalesosikhathi, itsho iNkosi, abantwana bakoIsrayeli bazakuza, bona labantwana bakoJuda kanyekanye; k behamba bekhala inyembezi, l bazahamba, bedinga iNkosi uNkulunkulu wabo. m <b>5</b> Bazabuza indlela eZiyoni, n ubuso babo bukhangele ngakuyo; wozani, lizihlanganise leNkosi o ngesivumelwano esiphakade esingayikukhohlakala. p 
<b>6</b> Abantu bami q bebeyizimvu ezilahlekileyo; r abelusi babo babaduhisile, bebaphendulela s ezintabeni; t bahamba besuka entabeni besiya eqaqeni, u bakhohlwa indawo yabo yokulala. v <b>7</b> Bonke ababatholayo babadla, w lezitha zabo zathi: x Kasiyikuba lacala, y ngenxa yokuthi bonile eNkosini, ikhaya lokulunga, z ngitsho iNkosi, ithemba laboyise. aa <b>8</b> Balekani ab liphume phakathi kweBhabhiloni, ac liphume elizweni lamaKhaladiya, ad libe njengezimpongo phambi komhlambi. ae 
<b>9</b> Ngoba khangela, ngizavusa ngenze ukuthi kwenyukele eBhabhiloni af ixuku lezizwe ezinkulu ag zivela elizweni lenyakatho, ah , ai zizihlele zimelene layo; aj kusukela lapho zithunjwe; imitshoko yakhe ak njengeqhawe al elilamandla am kayiyikubuyela ize an. <b>10</b> Njalo iKhaladiya izakuba yimpango; bonke abayiphangayo bazasutha, itsho iNkosi. <b>11</b> Ngoba lalijabula, ao ngoba lathokoza, lina bachithi belifa lami, ap ngoba liqolotsha njengethokazi aq etshanini, ar likhonye njengamabhiza amaduna; as 
<b>12</b> unyoko ulenhloni kakhulu, owalizalayo uyangekile. Khangela, ingemuva yezizwe iyinkangala at eyomileyo au lelugwadule. av <b>13</b> Ngenxa yolaka lweNkosi kayiyikuhlalwa, aw kodwa izakuba yincithakalo yonke; ax wonke owedlula eBhabhiloni uzamangala kakhulu, ay ancife ngenxa yazo zonke inhlupheko zayo. az <b>14</b> Zihleleleni ukumelana leBhabhiloni ba inhlangothi zonke, bb lina lonke eligobisa idandili, bc litshoke kuyo, lingagodli mtshoko, ngoba yonile eNkosini. bd 
<b>15</b> Memezani limelene layo be inhlangothi zonke; bf isinike isandla sayo, bg izisekelo zayo ziwile, bh imiduli yayo idiliziwe; bi ngoba lokho kuyimpindiselo yeNkosi; bj phindiselani kuyo, lenze kuyo njengokwenza kwayo. bk <b>16</b> Qumani umhlanyeli asuke eBhabhiloni, laye ophatha isikela ngesikhathi sokuvuna; bl ngenxa yenkemba ecindezelayo bm bazaphenduka, ngulowo lalowo kwabakibo, bn babaleke, ngulowo lalowo elizweni lakibo. bo 
<b>17</b> UIsrayeli uyimvu ehlakazekileyo; bp izilwane zimxotshile; bq kuqala inkosi yeAsiriya br yamudla, bs emva kwalokho lo uNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni bt wephule amathambo akhe. bu <b>18</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Khangela, ngizayijezisa inkosi yeBhabhiloni lelizwe layo, bv njengalokho bw ngayijezisa inkosi yeAsiriya. bx 
<b>19</b> Njalo ngizabuyisela uIsrayeli endaweni yakhe yokuhlala, by njalo uzadlisa bz eKharmeli ca , cb leBashani, cc , cd lomphefumulo wakhe usuthe entabeni yakoEfrayimi ce leGileyadi. cf , cg <b>20</b> Ngalezonsuku langalesosikhathi, itsho iNkosi, kuzadingwa ububi koIsrayeli, ch , ci kodwa bungabi khona, cj lezono zikaJuda, kodwa zingatholwa; ngoba ngizabathethelela ck labo engibenza babe yinsali. cl 
<b>21</b> Umelene lelizwe leMerathayimi, cm yenyuka umelene layo, njalo umelene labahlali bePhekhodi; cn , co uchithe, utshabalalise emva kwabo, cp itsho iNkosi, wenze njengakho konke engikulaye khona. cq <b>22</b> Umsindo wempi uselizweni, cr lokuchitheka okukhulu. <b>23</b> Yeka ukuganyulwa lokwephuka kwesando somhlaba wonke! cs Yeka ukwenziwa kweBhabhiloni ct unxiwa phakathi kwezizwe! cu <b>24</b> Ngakuthiya ngomjibila, cv futhi lawe wathunjwa, Bhabhiloni, lawe wawungazi; cw watholwa, futhi wabanjwa, ngoba waphikisana leNkosi. cx 
<b>25</b> INkosi ivulile isiphala sayo, yakhupha izikhali zolaka lwayo, cy ngoba lokhu kungumsebenzi weNkosi uJehova wamabandla elizweni lamaKhaladiya. <b>26</b> Wozani limelane layo livela ephethelweni, livule izibuya zayo, liyibuthelele cz njengezinqumbi, da liyitshabalalise, db ingabi lansali. dc <b>27</b> Bulalani amajongosi ayo wonke, dd kawehlele ekuhlatshweni. de Maye kuwo! Ngoba usuku lwawo lufikile, df isikhathi sempindiselo yawo. dg 
<b>28</b> Ilizwi lababalekayo labaphunyukayo bevela elizweni leBhabhiloni, dh ukuze bamemeze eZiyoni di impindiselo yeNkosi uNkulunkulu wethu, dj , dk impindiselo dl yethempeli layo. dm <b>29</b> Bizani abatshoki bamelane leBhabhiloni, dn bonke abagobisa idandili, do limise inkamba limelane layo inhlangothi zonke; dp kayingabi lokuphunyuka; dq liyiphindisele njengomsebenzi wayo, dr lenze kuyo njengakho konke ekwenzileyo; ngoba yenze ngokuziqhenya imelene leNkosi, ds imelene loNgcwele kaIsrayeli. 
<b>30</b> Ngakho amajaha ayo azakuwa ezitaladeni zayo, dt lawo wonke amadoda ayo empi du azabhujiswa ngalolosuku, dv itsho iNkosi. <b>31</b> Khangela, ngimelana lawe, wena oziqhenyayo, itsho iNkosi uJehova wamabandla; ngoba usuku lwakho selufikile, dw isikhathi engizakuhambela ngaso. dx , dy <b>32</b> Khona oziqhenyayo ezakhubeka awe, njalo kungekho ozamvusa; njalo ngizaphemba umlilo emizini yakhe dz , ea ozakudla konke okumhanqileyo. eb 
<b>33</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla: Babecindezelwe abantwana bakoIsrayeli labantwana bakoJuda ndawonye; ec njalo bonke ababathumbileyo babagagadlele; bala ukubayekela bahambe. ed <b>34</b> Umhlengi wabo ee ulamandla, ef iNkosi yamabandla libizo layo; eg isibili izamela udaba lwabo, eh ukuze inike umhlaba ukuphumula, kodwa inyakazise abahlali beBhabhiloni. <b>35</b> Inkemba iphezu kwamaKhaladiya, itsho iNkosi, laphezu kwabahlali beBhabhiloni, laphezu kweziphathamandla zayo, ei , ej laphezu kwabahlakaniphileyo bayo. ek , el 
<b>36</b> Inkemba iphezu kwabaqambimanga em ukuze benze ngobuthutha; en inkemba iphezu kwamaqhawe ayo eo ukuze adilizwe. <b>37</b> Inkemba iphezu kwamabhiza ayo, ep laphezu kwenqola zayo, eq laphezu kwayo yonke ingxube yexuku ephakathi kwayo, er ukuze babe ngabesifazana; es inkemba iphezu kokuligugu kwayo, et ukuze kuphangwe. <b>38</b> Imbalela iphezu kwamanzi ayo, eu ukuze ome, ngoba iyilizwe lezithombe ezibaziweyo; ev bayahlanya ngenxa yezithombe ezesabekayo. ew 
<b>39</b> Ngakho izilo zenkangala ex lamakhanka kuzahlala khona, ey lamadodakazi ezintshe azahlala kuyo, ez kakusayikuhlala muntu kuyo kuze kube nininini, fa kakuyikwakhelwa kuyo esizukulwaneni kusiya esizukulwaneni. fb <b>40</b> Njengoba uNkulunkulu wagenqula iSodoma leGomora fc labomakhelwane bayo, fd itsho iNkosi, ngokunjalo kakuyikuhlala muntu khona, fe njalo kakulandodana yomuntu ezahlala njengowezizwe kuyo. <b>41</b> Khangela, abantu bazavela ff enyakatho, fg lesizwe esikhulu, fh lamakhosi amanengi azavuswa evela emikhawulweni yomhlaba. 
<b>42</b> Bazabamba idandili lomkhonto; balesihluku, kabayikuba lomusa; ilizwi labo lizahlokoma njengolwandle, bagade amabhiza, ngulowo lalowo ehleliwe njengomuntu wempi emelene lawe, ndodakazi yeBhabhiloni. <b>43</b> Inkosi yeBhabhiloni izwile umbiko wabo, lezandla zayo zaba buthakathaka; ucindezelo lwayibamba, fi umhelo njengowobelethayo. 
<b>44</b> Khangela, njengesilwane esivela ekukhukhumaleni kweJordani fj uzakwenyuka emelene lendawo yokuhlala okungapheliyo, kodwa ngizahle ngimgijimise asuke kuyo; njalo ngubani okhethiweyo engingammisa phezu kwayo? Ngoba ngubani onjengami? Ngubani-ke ozangibizela ukulandisa? Njalo nguwuphi lowomelusi ongema phambi kwami? fk <b>45</b> Ngakho, zwanini icebo leNKOSI fl elicebe imelene leBhabhiloni, lemicabango yayo fm eyicabange imelene lelizwe lamaKhaladiya: Isibili abancinyane bomhlambi bazabadonsa; isibili izachitha indawo yokuhlala phezu kwabo. 
<b>46</b> Ngomsindo wokuthunjwa kweBhabhiloni fn umhlaba uyazamazama, fo lokukhala kuzwakala phakathi kwezizwe. fp 
<b>Jeremías 51</b><br><b>1</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi: Khangela ngizavusela iBhabhiloni lalabo abahlala enhliziyweni yalabo abangivukelayo a umoya ochithayo. b <b>2</b> Ngithume eBhabhiloni abelayo, c abazakuyela, bathulule ilizwe layo, ngoba bazamelana layo bevela inhlangothi zonke d ngosuku lobubi. e <b>3</b> Kakuthi otshokayo agobise idandili lakhe f emelene loligobisayo, g njalo emelene loziphakamisayo h ebhatshini lakhe lensimbi; i njalo lingawayekeli amajaha ayo, lilitshabalalise ibutho layo lonke. j 
<b>4</b> Ngokunjalo ababuleweyo bazakuwa elizweni lamaKhaladiya, labagwazwe k ezitaladeni zayo. l <b>5</b> Ngoba uIsrayeli kayikutshiywa, m loJuda nguNkulunkulu wabo, yiNkosi yamabandla, lanxa ilizwe labo ligcwele icala koNgcwele kaIsrayeli. <b>6</b> Balekani liphume phakathi kweBhabhiloni, n , o likhulule ngulowo lalowo umphefumulo wakhe; p lingaqunywa esiphambekweni sayo; q ngoba lesi yisikhathi sempindiselo yeNkosi, r ophindisela impindiselo kuyo. s 
<b>7</b> IBhabhiloni yayiyinkezo yegolide esandleni seNkosi, t eyayidakisa umhlaba wonke; izizwe zanatha okwewayini layo; u ngenxa yalokho izizwe zahlanya. v <b>8</b> IBhabhiloni w yahle yawa, yephuka; x qhinqani isililo ngayo, y lithathele ubuhlungu bayo ibhalisamu, z mhlawumbe ingelatshwa. <b>9</b> Besingayelapha iBhabhiloni, kodwa kayelaphekanga; itshiyeni, aa asihambeni ngulowo lalowo aye elizweni lakhe; ab ngoba isigwebo sayo sifinyelele ac emazulwini, ad siphakanyisiwe kwaze kwafika esibhakabhakeni. ae 
<b>10</b> INkosi ikhuphe af ukulunga kwethu; ag wozani, silandise eZiyoni ah umsebenzi weNkosi uNkulunkulu wethu. <b>11</b> Lolani imitshoko, ai liqoqe izihlangu; aj iNkosi ivusile umoya wamakhosi ak amaMede; al ngoba icebo layo limelene leBhabhiloni am ukuyichitha; ngoba lokho kuyimpindiselo yeNkosi, an impindiselo yethempeli layo. ao <b>12</b> Phakamisani isiboniso phezu kwemiduli yeBhabhiloni, ap liqinise ukulinda, aq libeke abalindi, ar lilungise abacathami, ngoba iNkosi isikucebile, futhi izakwenza as lokho ekukhulume ngabahlali beBhabhiloni. 
<b>13</b> Wena ohlala emanzini amanengi, at owande ngamagugu, au ukuphela kwakho sekufikile, isilinganiso senzuzo yakho embi. av <b>14</b> INkosi yamabandla ifungile aw ngomphefumulo wayo yathi: Isibili ngizakugcwalisa ngabantu ax njengamacimbi, ay njalo bazahlabelela umkhosi ngawe. az <b>15</b> Wenza umhlaba ngamandla akhe, ba walimisa ilizwe ngenhlakanipho yakhe, langokuqedisisa kwakhe wendlala amazulu. 
<b>16</b> Lapho ekhupha ilizwi lakhe, kulokuhlokoma kwamanzi amanengi emazulwini, ukhuphula inkungu ivela emkhawulweni womhlaba, enze imibane kanye lezulu, akhuphe umoya eziphaleni zakhe. <b>17</b> Wonke umuntu uyisiphukuphuku kalalwazi; wonke umkhandi wegolide uyangiswa yisithombe esibaziweyo, ngoba isithombe sakhe esibunjwe ngokuncibilikisa siyinkohliso, njalo kakulamoya kuzo. <b>18</b> Ziyize, umsebenzi wezinkohliso; ngesikhathi sokuhanjelwa kwazo zizabhubha. 
<b>19</b> Isabelo sikaJakobe kasinjengazo; ngoba yena nguye umbumbi wakho konke, loIsrayeli uyintonga yelifa lakhe; bb iNkosi yamabandla libizo lakhe. <b>20</b> Wena uyinduku yami yempi, izikhali zempi; bc , bd ngoba ngawe ngizaphahlaza izizwe, langawe ngichithe imibuso, <b>21</b> langawe ngiphahlaze ibhiza lomgadi walo, langawe ngiphahlaze inqola lomkhweli wayo, <b>22</b> langawe ngiphahlaze indoda lowesifazana, langawe ngiphahlaze ixhegu lomutsha, be langawe ngiphahlaze ijaha lentombi, bf 
<b>23</b> langawe ngiphahlaze umelusi lomhlambi wakhe, langawe ngiphahlaze umlimi lesipani sakhe, langawe ngiphahlaze ababusi lezinduna. bg <b>24</b> Njalo ngizayiphindisela iBhabhiloni bh labo bonke abahlali beKhaladiya konke okubi kwabo abakwenzileyo eZiyoni bi emehlweni enu, itsho iNkosi. <b>25</b> Khangela, ngimelana lawe, wena ntaba echithayo, bj itsho iNkosi, echitha umhlaba wonke; ngizakwelulela isandla sami kuwe, ngikugiqe usuke emadwaleni, ngikwenze ube yintaba yokutshiswa. bk 
<b>26</b> Njalo kabayikuthatha kuwe ilitshe libe ngelengonsi, bl lelitshe libe ngelezisekelo; ngoba uzakuba ngamanxiwa aphakade, itsho iNkosi. <b>27</b> Phakamisani isiboniso elizweni, bm livuthele uphondo phakathi kwezizwe, bn lilungise bo izizwe ukumelana layo, bp liyibizele ndawonye imibuso bq yeArarathi, br iMini, bs leAshikenazi; bt libeke umlawuli webutho emelene layo, bu lenyuse amabhiza njengentethe ezimaxhakaxhaka. bv 
<b>28</b> Lungisani izizwe bw ukumelana layo, bx amakhosi amaMede, by induna zayo, bz lababusi bayo bonke, lelizwe lonke lombuso wayo. ca <b>29</b> Lelizwe lizazamazama libe lobuhlungu, cb ngoba ngalinye lamacebo eNkosi lima limelene leBhabhiloni, cc ukwenza ilizwe leBhabhiloni libe yincithakalo cd engelamhlali. ce <b>30</b> Amaqhawe eBhabhiloni ayekele ukulwa, ahlala ezinqabeni, amandla awo aphelile, sebengabesifazana; cf bathungele indawo zayo zokuhlala, cg imigoqo yayo ch , ci yephukile. cj 
<b>31</b> Isigijimi sizagijima ukuhlangana lesigijimi, ck lombiki ukuhlangana lombiki, ukutshela inkosi yeBhabhiloni ukuthi umuzi wayo uthunjiwe kusukela ekucineni, cl <b>32</b> lamazibuko cm abanjiwe, cn lamaxhaphozi atshiswe ngomlilo, co lamadoda empi ethukile. <b>33</b> Ngoba itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla, uNkulunkulu kaIsrayeli: Indodakazi yeBhabhiloni injengebala lokubhulela; cp kuyisikhathi sokuyinyathela; cq kuseyisikhatshana, lesikhathi sesivuno sizafika kuyo. cr 
<b>34</b> UNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni ungidlile, cs ungichobozile; wangibeka ngaba yimbiza engelalutho, wangiginya ct njengomgobho, cu wagcwalisa isisu sakhe ngezibondlo zami, wangixotsha. cv <b>35</b> Udlakela lwami lolwenyama yami kube phezu kweBhabhiloni, utsho umhlalikazi weZiyoni; legazi lami libe phezu kwabahlali beKhaladiya, itsho iJerusalema. <b>36</b> Ngakho itsho njalo iNkosi: Khangela, ngizalumela udaba lwakho, cw ngiphindisele impindiselo yakho; ngomise cx , cy ulwandle lwayo, cz ngenze umthombo wayo wome. da 
<b>37</b> LeBhabhiloni izakuba ngamadundulu, db indawo yokuhlala dc yemigobho, dd isesabiso, de lento yokuncifelwa, df kungelamhlali. dg <b>38</b> Bazabhonga kanyekanye njengamabhongo ezilwane, dh bahwabhe njengemidlwane yezilwane. di <b>39</b> Ekutshisekeni kwabo ngizabenzela amadili, dj ngibadakise dk , dl ukuze bathokoze, balale ubuthongo obulaphakade, dm , dn bangavuki, do itsho iNkosi. <b>40</b> Ngizabehlisa njengamawundlu ekuhlatshweni, njengezinqama kanye lezimpongo. dp 
<b>41</b> Ithunjwe njani iSheshaki; dq yebo, lubanjiwe dr udumo lomhlaba wonke! ds IBhabhiloni isibe njani yisesabiso phakathi kwezizwe! dt <b>42</b> Ulwandle lukhukhumele phezu kweBhabhiloni, du isibekelwe ngobunengi bamagagasi alo. <b>43</b> Imizi yayo isingamanxiwa, dv ilizwe elomileyo, dw lenkangala, dx ilizwe okungahlali muntu kulo, dy okungadluli kulo indodana yomuntu. <b>44</b> Njalo ngizajezisa uBheli eBhabhiloni, dz ngikhuphe emlonyeni wakhe lokho akuginyileyo; ea lezizwe kazisayikujulukela kuyo; eb yebo, umduli weBhabhiloni uwile. ec 
<b>45</b> Phumani phakathi kwayo, bantu bami, ed likhulule ngulowo lalowo umphefumulo wakhe ee ekuvutheni kolaka lweNkosi. ef <b>46</b> Njalo hlezi inhliziyo yenu ibe buthakathaka, eg lesabe ngombiko ozwakala elizweni; eh ngoba kuzafika umbiko ngomunye umnyaka, lemva kwalokho umbiko ngomnyaka olandelayo, lodlakela elizweni, lombusi avukele umbusi. ei <b>47</b> Ngakho khangela, insuku ziyeza lapho engizahambela ej izithombe ezibaziweyo zeBhabhiloni; ek , el lelizwe lonke layo lizayangeka, em lababuleweyo bayo bonke bawele phakathi kwayo. en 
<b>48</b> Kuzakuthi-ke amazulu lomhlaba lakho konke okukukho eo kuhlabelele ngeBhabhiloni, ngoba abachithi bazayifikela bevela enyakatho, ep itsho iNkosi. <b>49</b> Njengalokhu iBhabhiloni yenza bawe ababuleweyo bakoIsrayeli, eq ngokunjalo ababuleweyo bomhlaba wonke bazakuwa eBhabhiloni. er <b>50</b> Lina eliphunyukileyo enkembeni, es hambani, lingemi; khumbulani iNkosi lisekhatshana, et leJerusalema ivele enhliziyweni yenu. eu 
<b>51</b> Siyangekile ngoba sizwile ihlazo; ev inhloni zisibekele ubuso bethu, ew , ex ngoba abezizwe sebefikile ezindaweni ezingcwele zendlu yeNkosi. ey <b>52</b> Ngakho khangela, insuku ziyeza, ez itsho iNkosi, lapho engizahambela izithombe zayo ezibaziweyo; fa lakulo lonke ilizwe layo fb kuzabubula ogwaziweyo. fc <b>53</b> Loba iBhabhiloni ingaphakamela emazulwini, fd njalo loba ingaqinisa ukuphakama kwamandla ayo, fe kube kanti abachithi bazayifikela bevela kimi, itsho iNkosi. 
<b>54</b> Kulomsindo wokukhala ovela eBhabhiloni, ff lokufohlela okukhulu elizweni lamaKhaladiya. <b>55</b> Ngoba iNkosi ichithile iBhabhiloni, yabhubhisa kuyo ilizwi elikhulu. Lapho amagagasi abo ahlokoma njengamanzi amanengi, fg umsindo wawo uzakhupha ilizwi. <b>56</b> Ngoba umchithi wehlela phezu kwayo, phezu kweBhabhiloni, amaqhawe ayo azathunjwa, amadandili awo ephukile; ngoba iNkosi, uNkulunkulu wezimpindiselo, uzayivuza sibili. fh 
<b>57</b> Ngizadakisa-ke fi iziphathamandla zayo, fj lezihlakaniphi zayo, fk ababusi bayo, fl lenduna zayo, fm lamaqhawe ayo; njalo bazalala ubuthongo obulaphakade, fn bangavuki, fo itsho iNkosi, obizo layo nguJehova wamabandla. fp <b>58</b> Itsho njalo iNkosi yamabandla: Imiduli ebanzi yeBhabhiloni izadilizwa ngokupheleleyo, fq lamasango ayo aphakemeyo athungelwe ngomlilo, fr labantu batshikatshikele ize, fs lezizwe ft zitshikatshikele umlilo, fu zikhathale. fv , fw 
<b>59</b> Ilizwi uJeremiya umprofethi alilaya uSeraya indodana kaNeriya, fx indodana kaMahaseya, ekuhambeni kwakhe loZedekhiya inkosi yakoJuda ukuya eBhabhiloni emnyakeni wesine wokubusa kwakhe. fy Njalo uSeraya wayeyisiphathamandla fz sokuthula. ga <b>60</b> UJeremiya wasebhala egwalweni konke okubi okuzakwehlela iBhabhiloni, wonke lamazwi abhaliweyo emelene leBhabhiloni. gb <b>61</b> UJeremiya wasesithi kuSeraya: Lapho usufikile eBhabhiloni, ubone ufunde wonke lamazwi. 
<b>62</b> Ubususithi: Nkosi, wena ukhulumile ngalindawo ukuthi uzayiquma, ukuze kungabi khona umhlali kiyo, gc kusukela emuntwini kusiya enyamazaneni, gd kodwa ibe zincithakalo phakade. <b>63</b> Kuzakuthi-ke usuqedile ukulufunda lolugwalo, ubophele ilitshe kulo, ge uluphosele phakathi kweYufrathi, <b>64</b> ubususithi: Ngokunjalo iBhabhiloni izatshona, gf ingabe isavumbuluka, ngenxa yobubi engizayehlisela bona; njalo bazadinwa. gg Kuze kube lapha ngamazwi kaJeremiya. gh 
<b>Jeremías 52</b><br><b>1</b> UZedekhiya wayeleminyaka engamatshumi amabili lanye esiba yinkosi, a wabusa iminyaka elitshumi lanye eJerusalema; lebizo likanina lalinguHamuthali, indodakazi kaJeremiya weLibhina. <b>2</b> Wasesenza okubi emehlweni eNkosi, njengakho konke akwenzayo uJehoyakhimi. <b>3</b> Ngoba ngenxa yokuthukuthela kweNkosi kwenzeka eJerusalema loJuda yaze yabaphosela khatshana lobuso bayo; uZedekhiya wasevukela inkosi yeBhabhiloni. 
<b>4</b> Kwasekusithi ngomnyaka wesificamunwemunye wokubusa kwakhe, b ngenyanga yetshumi, ngolwetshumi lwenyanga, uNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni wafika, yena lebutho lakhe lonke, ukumelana leJerusalema; basebemisa inkamba bemelene layo, bayakhela izinqaba zokuvimbezela bemelene layo inhlangothi zonke. <b>5</b> Ngokunjalo umuzi wangena ekuvinjezelweni kwaze kwaba ngumnyaka wetshumi lanye wenkosi uZedekhiya. <b>6</b> Ngenyanga yesine, ngolwesificamunwemunye lwenyanga, lapho indlala yayiqinile phakathi komuzi, abantu belizwe kababanga lokudla. 
<b>7</b> Basebewufohlela umuzi; wonke amadoda empi asebaleka, c aphuma emzini ebusuku, ngendlela yesango, phakathi kwemiduli emibili, elisesivandeni senkosi (amaKhaladiya ayemelene lomuzi inhlangothi zonke), ahamba ngendlela yegceke. <b>8</b> Kodwa ibutho lamaKhaladiya laxotshana lenkosi, lafica uZedekhiya emagcekeni eJeriko; lebutho lakhe lonke lahlakazeka lisuka kuye. <b>9</b> Basebeyibamba inkosi, bayenyusela enkosini yeBhabhiloni eRibila elizweni leHamathi, eyakhuluma izigwebo kuyo. 
<b>10</b> Inkosi yeBhabhiloni yasibulala amadodana kaZedekhiya emehlweni akhe, futhi yazibulala lazo zonke iziphathamandla zakoJuda eRibila. d <b>11</b> Yasiphumputhekisa amehlo kaZedekhiya, yambopha ngamaketane ethusi amabili, ukuze inkosi yeBhabhiloni imuse eBhabhiloni, yamfaka entolongweni, kwaze kwaba lusuku lokufa kwakhe. e 
<b>12</b> Njalo ngenyanga yesihlanu, f ngolwetshumi lwenyanga g (lo umnyaka wawungumnyaka h wetshumi lesificamunwemunye wenkosi uNebhukadirezari inkosi yeBhabhiloni), i uNebuzaradani induna yabalindi, eyayimi phambi kwenkosi yeBhabhiloni, wangena eJerusalema. <b>13</b> Watshisa indlu kaJehova, lendlu yenkosi, lezindlu zonke zeJerusalema lezindlu zonke zabakhulu wazitshisa ngomlilo. <b>14</b> Lebutho lonke lamaKhaladiya, elalilenduna yabalindi, labhidliza yonke imiduli yeJerusalema inhlangothi zonke. 
<b>15</b> UNebuzaradani induna yabalindi wasethumba abathile kwabangabayanga babantu, j lensali yabantu eyayisele emzini, lalabo ababehlamukile k, ababehlamukele enkosini yeBhabhiloni, labaseleyo bexuku. <b>16</b> Kodwa uNebuzaradani induna yabalindi watshiya abanye kwabangabayanga belizwe ukuthi babe ngabaphathi bezivini labalimi. l <b>17</b> Lensika zethusi ezazisendlini yeNkosi, m lezisekelo, n lolwandle lwethusi olwalusendlini yeNkosi, o amaKhaladiya akuphahlaza, athwala lonke ithusi lakho alisa eBhabhiloni. p 
<b>18</b> Athatha izimbiza, q , r lamafotsholo, s lezindlawu, lemiganu yokufafaza, t lezinkezo, u lazo zonke izitsha zethusi abakhonza ngazo, <b>19</b> lemiganu, lezitsha zokuthwala umlilo, v lemiganu yokufafaza, w lezimbiza, x lezinti zezibane, y lezinkezo, z lemiganu yomnikelo wokuthululwa; aa okwakuligolide golide lokwakuyisiliva siliva induna yabalindi yakuthatha. 
<b>20</b> Insika zombili, ulwandle olulodwa, lenkabi zethusi ezazilitshumi lambili ezazingaphansi kwezisekelo ab inkosi uSolomoni eyayikwenzele indlu yeNkosi; ithusi lazo zonke lezizitsha lalingelakulinganiswa ngesisindo. <b>21</b> Mayelana lezinsika, ubude bensika eyodwa babuzingalo ezilitshumi lesificaminwembili, lentambo yezingalo ezilitshumi lambili yayigombolozela, ac lohlonzi lwayo lwaluyiminwe emine, yayilomwolo. ad 
<b>22</b> Lesiduku esiphezu kwayo sasilithusi, lobude besiduku esisodwa babuzingalo ezinhlanu, ae lembule, lamapomegranati* phezu kwesiduku inhlangothi zonke; af konke kwakulithusi. Lensika yesibili yayilokufanana lalezi, lamapomegranati. <b>23</b> Njalo kwakulamapomegranati angamatshumi ayisificamunwemunye lesithupha eceleni; ag wonke amapomegranati ayelikhulu phezu kwembule inhlangothi zonke. ah <b>24</b> Induna yabalindi yasithatha uSeraya umpristi oyinhloko, ai loZefaniya umpristi owesibili, labalindi bombundu labo bobathathu. aj 
<b>25</b> Yasithatha emzini umthenwa othile owayengumphathi phezu kwamadoda empi, lamadoda ayisikhombisa kulabo ababona ubuso benkosi, ak atholakala emzini, lomabhalane wenduna yebutho eyayibuthela abantu belizwe emabuthweni, lamadoda angamatshumi ayisithupha abantu belizwe, ayetholakala phakathi komuzi. <b>26</b> UNebuzaradani induna yabalindi wasebathatha, wabasa enkosini yeBhabhiloni eRibila. <b>27</b> Inkosi yeBhabhiloni yasibatshaya, yababulalela eRibila elizweni leHamathi. Ngokunjalo uJuda wathunjwa esuswa elizweni lakhe. 
<b>28</b> Laba ngabantu uNebhukadirezari abathumbayo: Ngomnyaka wesikhombisa, amaJuda azinkulungwane ezintathu lamatshumi amabili lantathu; al <b>29</b> ngomnyaka wetshumi lesificaminwembili kaNebhukadirezari, kwasuka eJerusalema imiphefumulo engamakhulu ayisificaminwembili lamatshumi amathathu lambili; am <b>30</b> ngomnyaka wamatshumi amabili lantathu kaNebhukadirezari, uNebuzaradani induna yabalindi wathumba amaJuda, imiphefumulo engamakhulu ayisikhombisa lamatshumi amane lanhlanu; an yonke imiphefumulo yayizinkulungwane ezine lamakhulu ayisithupha. 
<b>31</b> Kwasekusithi ngomnyaka wamatshumi amathathu lesikhombisa wokuthunjwa kukaJehoyakhini inkosi yakoJuda, ao ngenyanga yetshumi lambili ngolwamatshumi amabili lanhlanu lwenyanga, ap uEvili-Merodaki inkosi yeBhabhiloni, ngomnyaka wokuqala wokubusa kwakhe, waphakamisa ikhanda likaJehoyakhini inkosi yakoJuda, aq wamkhupha entolongweni, <b>32</b> wasekhuluma kuhle laye, wabeka isihlalo sakhe sobukhosi phezu kwesihlalo sobukhosi samakhosi ayelaye eBhabhiloni. ar 
<b>33</b> Wasentshintsha izigqoko zokubotshwa kwakhe; njalo wadla isinkwa phambi kwayo njalonjalo insuku zonke zempilo yakhe. <b>34</b> Mayelana lesabelo sakhe, as isabelo esimiyo wasinikwa yinkosi yeBhabhiloni, into yosuku ngosuku lwayo, kwaze kwaba lusuku lokufa kwakhe, zonke insuku zempilo yakhe.
